# CLAUDE.md

This file provides guidance to Claude Code (claude.ai/code) when working with code in this repository.

> Last refreshed: **2026-11-10** (Net Balance in the Genius Assistant confirmation on branch fix/ai-receipt-net-balance; Genius Assistant + highlight + Income & Expense alerts deployed 2026-11-10, master 7eaf4f7); earlier: **2026-11-06** (payroll SSN removed on branch feature/ios-appstore-readiness); (AI roadmap Phases 0–6 merged to master + deployed 2026-11-05 08:54 CT at the user's request, master 503cccb). Not a git repo — the "Current State" section below is the change record; update it when finishing a work session.

## Build & Run

```bash
# Build and package (skipping tests)
./mvnw clean package -DskipTests

# Run the application (or use restart-app.bat on Windows: kills :8080 then runs)
./mvnw spring-boot:run

# Run unit tests (*Test, Surefire)
./mvnw test

# Run a single test class
./mvnw test -Dtest=EncryptionUtilTest

# Unit + integration tests (*IT, Failsafe, needs Docker/Testcontainers)
./mvnw verify
```

Make targets (each shells to `scripts/*.sh`): `build`, `unit`, `integration`, `quality` (SpotBugs+PMD), `deps`, `security` (OWASP+Trivy+ZAP), `e2e` (Playwright in `e2e/`), `load` (k6 in `load/`), `db`, `multitenant`, `gate` (full CI promotion gate), `deploy`. See `TESTING.md`. Prereqs: JDK 25, Docker, Node 18+, k6.

App runs on port **8080**; `/` forwards to `/login.html`. `BASE_URL` for e2e/load defaults to `http://localhost:8080`.

## Stack

- Spring Boot **4.0.6**, Java **25**, Maven wrapper (`com.churchgeniuspro:ChurchGeniusPro:0.0.1-SNAPSHOT`)
- Starters: web, data-jpa, mail, actuator, security-crypto, session-jdbc. **No spring-boot-starter-security** — auth is custom servlet filters.
- PostgreSQL (runtime), H2 + Testcontainers (tests), Lombok
- Twilio (SMS), web-push + jose4j + BouncyCastle (VAPID push), Apache POI (.doc/.docx song uploads), PDFBox, Micrometer/Prometheus
- **No Plaid SDK** — Plaid API called via hand-rolled `plaid/service/PlaidClient`. **No Stripe SDK** — per-tenant `StripeSettings` entity only.
- OpenAI via REST (`openai.api.key`): gpt-4o vision OCR (checks/bank statements), whisper-1 + gpt-4o-mini voice commands.

## Architecture

Single Spring Boot app serving the REST API and a **plain hand-written HTML + vanilla JS** frontend (NOT React — a leftover `static/assets/index-*.js|css` bundle exists but is unused). No frontend build step; edit the HTML/JS in `src/main/resources/static/` directly (122 HTML pages; shared JS: `shell.js` nav shell, `ai-assistant.js`, per-page voice helpers). The ~25 `.html` files in the project **root are empty 3-byte stubs** (BOM only) — ignore them; real pages live in `static/`.

`package.json` at root is only for `generate_manual.js` (pdfkit user-manual generator), not a frontend build.

### Package layout (`com.churchgeniuspro`)

- `controller/` — 93 REST controllers (members/family/groups, income/expense/donation/pledge/funds, events + check-in + volunteers, attendance, kids ministry + pickup, reminders, certificates, songbook, worship planning, notifications/push/SMS/WhatsApp, AI search/voice, auth/session/signup, service-admin, Stripe settings, `GlobalExceptionHandler`)
- `service/` — 71 services (Email/Sms/WhatsApp/WebPush, reminder schedulers, AI/vision OCR, ETL import suite, TemporaryAccess, Backup, SchemaFix)
- `repository/` — 140 Spring Data JPA repositories (no DAO layer)
- **`hibernate/` — the JPA entity layer** (144 `@Entity`, e.g. `AppUser`, `Family`, `Income`, `ChurchEvent`, `AttendanceRecord`, `StripeSettings`). New entities go here.
- `model/` — 15 plain `*BO` POJOs (request/response business objects, NOT entities)
- `webfilter/` — 9 servlet filters (see Auth)
- `payroll/` — self-contained W-2 payroll module (~68 classes: controller/dto/service/engine/entity/repository/pdf); own `payroll/README.md`; gated by `RoleGuard.requirePayroll`
- `plaid/` — bank sync module (~34 classes: link, webhooks, sync, review queue, token cipher, audit, email-verification gate)
- `bankimport/` — manual CSV/bank-statement import (separate from Plaid)
- `util/` — `EncryptionUtil`, `PasswordUtil` (BCrypt), `RoleGuard`, `SessionUtil`, ETL helpers
- `config/` — `WebConfig`, `SessionConfig` (JDBC HTTP session), `DatabaseIndexInitializer`
- Root: `ChurchGeniusProApplication`, `DataSeeder`

### Authentication & filters

No Spring Security. `webfilter/FilterConfig` registers filters in order:
`MultipartErrorFilter`(-2) → `CspFilter`(-1) → `NoAutoLogoutFilter`(0, `/api/*`) → **`AuthFilter`(1, `/api/*`)** → `LoginFilter`(2, debug logging) → `TempAccessFilter`(3) → `PrivatePageFilter`(4, network-based page gating + bypass token) → `NtagAccessFilter`(5, NFC-tag page whitelist).

`AuthFilter` IS registered and does real session validation (401 without valid session; whitelists `/login`, `/api/session`, `/api/signup/**`, etc.; re-checks `app_user.enabled`/`delete_flag` per request). Sessions are server-side HTTP sessions persisted to Postgres (spring-session-jdbc, 30m timeout). Controllers additionally enforce roles via `util/RoleGuard`. Multi-tenant scoping via `clientId` (`app_client_id`).

### Database & config

- Hibernate `ddl-auto=update` — schema evolves automatically; `scripts/sql/migrate_production.sql` (idempotent) is the manual companion run against prod **before deploying** a new JAR for changes Hibernate won't apply.
- Profiles (selected by `SPRING_PROFILES_ACTIVE`; Azure sets `prod`, `restart-app.bat` passes `-Dspring-boot.run.profiles=local` — nothing is hard-coded in the base file):
  - `local` — `jdbc:postgresql://localhost:5432/churchgeniuspro`, user `postgres` (password committed)
  - `prod` — env-injected `DB_URL_PROD`/`DB_USER_PROD`/`DB_PASSWORD_PROD`, secure cookies
  - `trial` — local `churchgeniustrial` DB
- `scheduler.properties` — 9 cron jobs (birthday/anniversary/celebrant/holiday/weekly-meeting reminders)
- Env-injected keys: `OPENAI_API_KEY`, Twilio, `plaid.*` (defaults to sandbox), `PUSH_VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY`
- `src/test/resources/sql/seed_test_data.sql` — idempotent test data for one hardcoded tenant; `sample-imports/` — CSVs for the ETL import feature

## Tests

`src/test/java`: 10 classes — unit (`*Test`: encryption, role-based access, `@WebMvcTest`+H2 web tests, multi-tenant architecture test, `PlaidTokenCipherTest`) and integration (`*IT`: `ApplicationSmokeIT`, `HelpAuditLogRepositoryIT` — Testcontainers Postgres). E2E: Playwright in `e2e/` (app must be running). Load: k6 in `load/`. Coverage/quality/security via Maven profiles `coverage`, `quality`, `security`.

## Key Conventions

- Package root: `com.churchgeniuspro`; entities in `hibernate/`, repos in `repository/`, business objects in `model/`
- REST controllers use `@RestController`; page-serving redirects use `@Controller`
- Auth = servlet filters + `RoleGuard`, never Spring Security annotations
- Every tenant-scoped query must filter by `clientId` (checked by `MultiTenantReadinessTest`)
- Schema changes: rely on ddl-auto for dev, mirror in `scripts/sql/migrate_production.sql` for prod

## Feature docs (root)

`ATTENDANCE.md` (attendance module, Phase 1), `PAYROLL.md` (+ `payroll/README.md`), `PLAID_BANK_SYNC_DESIGN.md` (**header says "no implementation code yet" — stale; Plaid is fully implemented**), `TEMPORARY_ACCESS.md` (time-limited badge/code access via `TempAccessFilter`), `LOGIN_SECURITY.md` (failed-login protection: temporary blocks, never permanent lockout), `LOGGING.md` (daily security/error/application logs, Azure App Service durability, credential masking, 30-day retention), `TESTING.md` (test/CI gate framework). Plaid review-queue flow: staging txns → approve → `income`/`expense`.

**2026-11-10: Notify Email → Content "✕ Clear" did nothing (user report). Branch `fix/notify-email-clear` (from master 7eaf4f7), pushed as a BRANCH only — NOT merged, NOT deployed. Page-only change (notifyEmail.html).**
CAUSE — the button ran `execCommand('removeFormat')` (title "Clear formatting"): it only strips bold / italic / underline from SELECTED text, so on plain text (e.g. a Draft with AI result) nothing visible happened.
FIX — "✕ Clear" (`#contentClearBtn`, `clearContent()`) empties the content box via select-all + `delete` (so Ctrl+Z works) and shows "Content cleared. [Undo]" (`#contentCleared`, `undoClearContent()` restores the exact HTML; offer goes after 15 s, when new text is typed, or on form reset). NEW "T̶ₓ Formatting" (`#contentClearFmtBtn`, `clearContentFormatting()`, mousedown kept from stealing the selection): removes formatting from the selection, or from all the content when nothing in the box is selected (lists unwrapped to one line per item). Toolbar wraps on phones. Other editors' removeFormat buttons (serviceadminhome, tax-report) untouched.
TESTS — Playwright (static page, stubbed APIs, 1440 + 390): 24/24 (Malayalam draft cleared, Undo + Ctrl+Z restore incl. formatting, typing drops the offer, Formatting on all / on a selection only, Bold unchanged, no dialogs, no page errors, no horizontal scroll). That premise was wrong: `MembershipFormController.memberHomePage` admits staff unless their `member` key is false, and the portal APIs match a staff login to its own member record by e-mail (`MemberSessionResolver`). session.js `applyNavAccess` hides every link whose path is false, and Family / Contributions / Groups / Classes / Volunteer / Directory open `/memberHome?tab=…` — only Song Book (`/songbook`) survived.
FIX — `navAccess` `/memberHome` = `page(req, "member")` (the route's own rule). Unchanged: session.js `applyMemberNavSections` (staff see My Profile only when a saved member.* tab is ticked; each tab by its own key; a never-saved staff login still has no My Profile section), member / church / Kids Portal sessions (no map), memberHome's own staff filter, the server checks.

Title, rows (contributor / category, fund, payment method, transaction date, reference) unchanged. ai-assistant.js receipt rows read the server's labels; a subtraction row is red (`.aa-tot-sub`). **2026-11-09 (latest): Income & Expense alerts, Genius Assistant validation + saved confirmation, highlight only within the first 100 entries (user spec "Income & Expense Alerts, Validation, and AI Entry Confirmation"). Branch `feature/entry-alerts` (from `feature/ai-genius-assistant` + merge of `feature/highlight-new-entry`), pushed as a BRANCH only — NOT merged, NOT deployed. Additive schema: `entry_alert_setting` (Hibernate creates it; guarded block at the end of `migrate_production.sql`; NOT run against production). No change to existing records.**
ALERTS — NEW `entryalert/EntryAlertService` = the ONE rule (`evaluate(Settings, kind, date, amount, today)`, pure): FUTURE_DATE date > today + N days, OLD_DATE date < today − N days, AMOUNT_HIGH amount > X, AMOUNT_LOW amount < X (strictly; each with a sentence naming the value and the threshold). Settings = NEW `hibernate/EntryAlertSetting` (`entry_alert_setting`: app_client_id, username, role (session role; "Church" for church logins), kind INCOME|EXPENSE, app_user_id, future/past/max/min enabled + value, updated_at; unique `ux_entry_alert_login_role_kind`) — no row = no alerts; per login AND per role AND per kind. `save()` validation: switched-on alert needs a value; days whole 1–3650; amounts > 0, ≤ 2 decimals, ≤ AMOUNT_MAX, "$1,000" accepted; less-than < greater-than; a switched-off alert may be blank; nothing saved while anything is invalid. A settings lookup failure = no alerts (logged), never blocks an entry. NEW `entryalert/EntryAlertController` `GET|PUT /api/entry-alerts/{income|expense}` (own row only; gate = the page's write rule `AiFinanceTools.mayCreateIncome|Expense`; 401/403/404; 400 `INVALID_SETTINGS` + `problems{field: sentence}`). Removed with the login (`StaffAccountDeletionService` → `deleteForLogin`); church deletion purges it (tenant column discovered by `TenantPurgePlanner`).
WHERE ALERTS APPLY (same service everywhere) — `POST /api/income` and `POST /api/expense` (`alertsFirst`, after the existing validation, before the save; `@Autowired(required=false)` so hand-built controller tests are unchanged): alerted + no `alertsConfirmed:true` → 409 `{code:"ENTRY_ALERTS", alerts:[{code,message}], confirmField}` and NOTHING saved; the same body with `alertsConfirmed:true` saves. Edits (PUT) are not checked. Callers: income.html / expense.html (`CGP_entryAlerts.post`), home.html quick add (`quickAddPost`), bank-import.html (`doSave`, remembered per line via `spec.alertsConfirmed`), income-voice / expense-voice (they call the page's own save). Genius Assistant (`AiGuidedRecordService`): `entryAlerts.check(owner(req), …)` as soon as the date and amount are valid (after the conversation's own future-date question, before the optional fields) → `asking:"alerts"`, `alerts[]`, short `question`, choices "Yes, continue to review" / "Change the date" / "Change the amount" / "Cancel"; yes → on, "No" → cancelled, any correction resets `alertsConfirmed`. Plaid review approvals and ETL imports go through the services, not these endpoints — no alerts there.
PAGES — NEW `static/entry-alerts.js` (`CGP_entryAlerts`): `review(alerts, noun)` in-page alertdialog (focus on "Go back and review", Tab trapped, Escape = go back, "Save anyway"), `post(url, payload, {noun})`, `mountSettings({kind, anchor})` → "🔔 Income/Expense alert settings" button above the form (hidden when the page's edit permission is false) + panel (four rows in the spec's wording, value disabled while off, sensible default when switched on (30 / 365 days), the server's rules repeated client-side, server `problems` shown on the field, "Saved …" status). textContent only; no browser dialogs.
GENIUS ASSISTANT VALIDATION (spec §4) — invalid date: a slash / ISO date that is not a real MM/DD/YYYY date (`badDate`, public static) → "The date format is invalid. Please enter the date in MM/DD/YYYY format." + " Did you mean 11/30/2026?" when the day/month swap is a real date (choices [suggestion, Today]); never read as another date, never fixed silently. Invalid amount: "income of Anson", "amount is fifty", "$abc", negative ("-50", "$-50"), more than 2 decimals, or an amount answer with no number → "The amount is invalid. Please enter a valid numeric amount, such as $100." (`AMOUNT_INVALID`); "of Anson" is NOT taken as the contributor. Unknown fund named ("to the Mission Fund") → "I couldn't find a fund called “Mission Fund” in this church's funds." + the fund question with the church's funds. The field with the problem is asked first (`problemField`, response `invalid`); everything else in the request is kept. The model fallback may still read names (fund / category / method / contributor / ref / note) but NEVER the amount or the date any more (`llmFill`), so an unclear amount/date is always asked. Existing checks unchanged: member not found → guest question, several → choice, duplicates → "Save anyway?", one-time token, inFlight guard.
SAVED CONFIRMATION (spec §3) — `AiFinanceTools.CreateIncome|CreateExpense.execute` read the fund total before and after the save inside the same transaction (`AiFinanceLookup.incomeFundTotal` = active income rows of this church in that sub-fund, `expenseFundTotal` = active expense rows paid from that main fund — the same scope as the Accountant dashboard's `ledger_month_summary`; `flushPending()` writes the JPA row first because income/expense ids are SEQUENCE-generated) → result `receipt {kind, title "Income of $100.00 was added successfully.", rows Contributor|Expense Category, Fund, Payment Method, Transaction Date (MM/DD/YYYY), Reference Number, totals {label "Total income recorded to <fund> (all dates)" | "Total expenses paid from <fund> (all dates)", previous, addedLabel, added, next}}` + plain-text `answer`. Totals only when both were read and differ by EXACTLY the amount; otherwise `totalsNote` "could not be shown reliably" (no misleading figure). `AiActionService.confirm` passes a tool's `answer` / `receipt` through (only after the save). ai-assistant.js `cardBody`: `.aa-receipt` (green bordered card, ✓ Saved, rows, Previous total / Added … / New total) and `.aa-alerts` (amber list; the bullet lines are dropped from the answer text); works in the Genius panel (✓ Saved bubble) and the classic box.
HIGHLIGHT — `revealNewRow(id, dateStr)`: `NEW_ROW_LIMIT = 100` replaces the 20-page search; pages are fetched only until the first 100 entries are loaded; a row beyond them (or not found) is NOT highlighted or revealed — "It is an older transaction (dated MM/DD/YYYY), so it is further down the list, past the first 100 entries — use Load More to find it."
HELP — helpCenter Income/Expense card section `#hc-income-expense-alerts`, HC_KB `entry-alerts`, `HelpContentCatalog.income` v2.2.

Engine answers are moved WHOLE into the bubble (`.ga-extra`); question bubbles show the server's short `question`; choices as cards (≤3 short = pills, >8 = filter box); review card `.ga-review` "Draft · not saved" → Saving… → ✓ Saved / Cancelled / Failed; older cards' buttons disabled. Fallback: `ai.chat.ui=${AI_CHAT_UI:true}` (`/api/session` `aiChatUi`); false = the classic results box and the classic /ai-assistant bar.
SERVER — `AiDataSearchController.withShortQuestion` (`question` on every answer with `choices`); review questions get Cancel; card fields carry `ask` (meeting category; income/expense fund, category, method; settings main fund, tax-deductible, level); meeting confirm label "Create Meeting". Routing, permissions, plans, consent, tenant isolation, duplicate checks and save paths unchanged. Name "Genius Assistant" in menus (More → Genius Assistant), AiNavigationCatalog, DESTS, Help Center (`#hc-ai-assistant`, NEW `#hc-ai-assistant-panel`), `HelpContentCatalog.ai_assistant` v1.4.

**2026-11-09: Income / Expense — the record just added is highlighted in its normal date position (user request; Option 1 chosen over "temporarily on top"). Branch `feature/highlight-new-entry` (from master e3178ee), pushed as a BRANCH only — NOT merged, NOT deployed. Page-only change (income.html, expense.html); no API, schema, sorting or paging change.**
After a successful ADD (form Save, voice / Converse Save — they call the same `saveIncome` / `submitForm`), `revealNewRow(id)` (id from the POST response) looks for the row in the list the server returned (date DESC, unchanged); when it is backdated beyond the fetched rows it fetches further server pages with the page's own `fetchIncomePage` / `fetchExpensePage` (≤ `NEW_ROW_MAX_PAGES` = 20 × 50 rows), raises Load More's counter to the next multiple of 10 that shows it, renders, scrolls it to the middle of the screen (instant with reduced motion) and gives it `tr.row-new` (green tint #e8f5e9 + 3 px green left edge, "New" tag beside the row number — the success-message colours). After `NEW_ROW_MS` = 8 s the tint fades (0.8 s) and the tag is removed. Row beyond row 10 → message "saved — it is row N (dated …), highlighted below"; not found within 20 pages → "It is further down the list (by its date) — use Load More to see it." Edits are not highlighted; any fresh load (edit, delete, reload) clears the highlight (`clearNewRow`). TESTS — NEW `ui/NewEntryHighlightTest` (2). Full unit suite 3,223 / 0 failures / 3 skipped. Playwright (stubbed APIs, 120 rows, 1280 + 390, both pages): today-dated add at the top, backdated add at row 88 (server page 2), not found within the cap, edit — 200/200 (fake clock, reduced motion) + 160/160 (real time, smooth scroll): order and dates unchanged, one highlighted row, in view, still there at 7 s, gone after 8 s, Load More still adds 10 without re-highlighting, no horizontal scroll, no page errors.

**2026-11-08 (latest 3): AI Search never edits or deletes existing records; duplicate names never created (user request after the settings milestone). Same branch `feature/ai-guided-create`, NOT merged, NOT deployed. No schema change.**
REDIRECT — NEW `ai/guided/AiManageRedirectService`, run FIRST in `AiDataSearchController.data` (before every start rule): an edit / delete verb at the start (edit|change|update|modify|rename|correct|fix|amend|adjust|delete|remove|erase|drop|deactivate|disable|inactivate|archive|hide|merge|get rid of|take out; never create verbs, "mark/complete" (follow-up tool) or "cancel") about a fund / sub-fund / purpose / transaction type (settings words of `AiGuidedSettingService.KIND_WORDS`, or the text after the verb is EXACTLY one of the church's Account Settings names — only for Account Settings users), an income / expense entry (record words or giving / spending verbs, amounts; the church's Account Settings names are masked first and only the part before "to / into / as" is read, so "rename the fund Tithe to Tithes" is a fund; a settings word before the record word wins) or a meeting → intent `manage.redirect`, `readOnly:true`, `record` (the existing name when found), message "AI Search can add new … but it can't change or delete existing ones. To delete “X”, open the … page (Account Settings → …)" + `link` (/fund, /purpose, /transactiontype, /income, /expense, /meetings) ONLY when the login may open that page (settings = `mayManageSettings`; income / expense = requireAccountantOrAdmin + page key; meetings = staff role + `general.meetings`), otherwise "You don't have access to the … page". Nothing is written, no proposal / token. While a meeting / income-expense / settings conversation or an action card is open (`draftOpen`), a correction of its own field right after the verb ("change the fund to …", "rename it", "remove the note", "change the time to 8 pm") is left to it; any other edit / delete request is redirected, the draft kept, `pending:true` + "Your unsaved entry here is still open". No AI tool edits or deletes master data or financial records (ratchet in `AiGuidedSettingTest.noEditOrDeleteTools`). ai-assistant.js: `looksLikeManage` — edit / delete phrases go to the server first (before the direct-navigation shortcut; `dataFirst` includes it; pinned in `AiSearchBarVocabularyTest`); if unhandled, the old flow runs.
DUPLICATES — unchanged rule (pages' scope: per church, sub-fund per main fund; case-insensitive, spaces collapsed, final "." / matching quotes removed) refused before the card ("… already exists. What name should I use instead?", no card) and again at Save; NEW: when the page's own service refuses at Save (a name added meanwhile) `AiActionService.confirm` answers `action.refused` "An entry with that name already exists, so nothing was saved" (was the generic failure). NEW `ai/guided/AiGuidedSettingService` (session `aiSettingDraft`): start = "create|add|make|set up|new|register|define [a|an|the] [new] (main fund|sub-fund|sub-category|fund|transaction type|payment method|purpose|expense category) [called|named|:] <name> [under <main fund>] [not tax-deductible]". Gate first and on every message: not member/church/Kids/temp + `AiSettingsTools.mayManageSettings` (requireAccountantOrAdmin + page `accounting.settings` = the Fund / Purpose / Transaction Type pages' rule) + plan `aiActions` + `ai.actions.enabled`. Asks only what is missing: name; "fund" → main fund or sub-fund (choices; "income is recorded to sub-funds, expenses are paid from main funds"); sub-fund → which main fund (choices = this church's main funds; none → "make it a main fund instead?"). A name that already exists (pages' scope: per church; sub-fund per main fund) → said + "What name should I use instead?". Card: Name (required), Fund level / Main fund (required), Tax-deductible (optional, default Yes); Save via `AiActionService.confirm`; corrections "change the name to …", "rename it to …", "make it a main fund / a sub-fund under …", "change the main fund to …", "change tax-deductible to no"; cancel. Start words also cover income / giving / donation / offering fund|category (→ sub-fund), expense fund (→ main fund), transaction / payment / offering / donation / giving / income type (→ transaction type), expense type|category|purpose (→ purpose), verbs enter / record; "fund raising …" is not a fund. `AiGuidedRecordService.START` no longer takes "add an expense category / type / fund …" (lookahead), so the three start rules never claim the same phrase. Routing: a settings start request runs first (drops the meeting + income/expense drafts); income/expense and meeting starts drop the settings draft; pending answers meeting → income/expense → settings → action agent. ai-assistant.js `looksLikeAction` + create|new|define|register|set up (so "New payment method Zelle" reaches the server). **2026-11-08 (latest): AI Search guided conversations — meeting improvements, guided income / expense, conversation thread in the bar. Branch `feature/ai-guided-create` (from master 2bdf671; separate from `fix/myprofile-nav-linked-member`), committed locally, NOT merged, NOT deployed. No schema change (proposals reuse `ai_action_proposal`, source `GUIDED`). Plan doc "AI Search Conversations (Meeting, Income, Expense) and My Profile Navigation — Audit and Plan" (claude.ai, 2026-11-08), decisions 1A–5A.**
MEETING (M2, `AiMeetingCreateService`) — "7:30" / "at 7:30" without AM/PM is never 07:30: `time()` reads h:mm without meridiem as 24-hour only for 0, 13–23 or a leading zero; `AMBIGUOUS_CLOCK` + `AMBIGUOUS_HOUR` keep the minutes in `timeHint` → "Is that 7:30 AM or 7:30 PM?" with `choices`. Past date → asked once (`pastConfirmed`; choices keep / different date). Stage REVIEW: a draft completed over several messages asks "Are you missing anything, or are you ready to review the meeting details?" (choices Review the details / Add a note → "What should the note say?"); a one-message request (`oneShot`) goes straight to the card as before. `correct()` at every stage: "change|set|update … (category|date|time|location|note) to …", "actually 8 pm" — clears the field, refills it, withdraws the old token, back to the card once reviewed. Card `confirm.fields` [{key,label,value,required,edit:"Change … to "}] + `label`, response `pending:true`; category question `choices`; `meetingName()` — never "Prayer Meeting meeting". Save path / token / duplicate check unchanged.
INCOME / EXPENSE (M3) — NEW `ai/guided/AiGuidedRecordService` (session `aiGuidedDraft`, 15 min): start = `START` ("create|add|record|enter|log|post|make|new [a|an|new] [$N] income|expense|donation|offering|contribution|gift|tithe"); gate first (before any lookup) and on EVERY message: not member/church/Kids/temp-access + the page's write rule `AiFinanceTools.mayCreateIncome|Expense` (requireAccountantOrAdmin + page `accounting.income|expense` + `accounting.income|expense.edit`) + plan feature `aiActions` + `ai.actions.enabled`. Asks one required field at a time (income: contributor → amount → fund → date → method; expense: category → amount → fund → date → method) with `choices`; future date asked once; then the optional question (exact text "You haven't entered the optional Reference Number (useful for check payments) or Notes. Would you like to add either of these, or should I skip them and proceed to review?"; Add a reference number / Add notes / Skip and review — skip = straight to the card); then (multi-message drafts) "Are you missing anything, or are you ready to review the income|expense details?"; then duplicate check (`AiFinanceLookup.incomeDuplicates` same contributor+fund+date+amount / `expenseDuplicates` same category+fund+date+amount → "Save another one anyway?" Save anyway / Cancel); then the card (Required: Contributor|Expense category, Fund, Amount, Date, Method; Optional: Reference number, Notes; Edit words; label Save income|expense; endpoint `/api/ai/actions/confirm`). Contributor: member of THIS church (`AiActionLookup.members`; several → pick; none → "Should I record it from a guest named …?"), "guest X", Anonymous / Loose Offering (guest names). Rules parse $ / "N dollars" / "of N" amounts (≤2 decimals, >0, ≤ `IncomeController|ExpenseController.AMOUNT_MAX`, now public), bookkeeping dates (month/day without a year = this year unless >7 days ahead → last year; bare weekday = most recent), the church's fund / category / method names (whole words, longest wins), "check #1234" = ref + method Check, "note: …". Corrections ("change the amount to 99", "actually Check", "change notes to …") and "cancel" at every step. Model fallback (`ai.guided.llm-fallback=${AI_GUIDED_LLM_FALLBACK:true}`) only when the rules read nothing: gets the reply text, field names and fund / category / method NAMES (never member names); every value matched to the church's lists, a contributor only when the words are in the reply. Routing (`AiDataSearchController`): a guided start request runs first and drops `aiMeetingDraft`; a meeting create request drops the guided draft; then meeting → guided answers → action agent → data search.
SAVE — NEW `ai/action/AiFinanceTools` (`create_income` / `create_expense`, `guidedOnly()`): resolve re-checks every id inside the church (`AiFinanceLookup` by-id finders, member by id), execute = `IncomeService.createIncome` 11-arg / `ExpenseService.createExpense` 10-arg (the pages' own methods; quickAdd false; created_by = username). `AiActionService.proposeGuided` stores the PROPOSED row (source GUIDED); confirm = the existing `confirm` (same user + church, expiry, plan + `allowed` re-checked, re-resolve, one transaction) — button or "yes"/"save" in the conversation. `confirm` now holds an in-JVM `inFlight` token set (a second press while the first saves → expired; all action cards). Guided-only tools: skipped by `AiToolRegistry.describe()` (LLM pick), refused by `propose()` (any source incl. MCP), skipped by MCP `list_actions`; `AiTool.guidedOnly()` default false. aiActivity source label "Guided conversation". Ratchet `ApiPermissionCheckRatchetTest` + `ai/action/AiFinanceTools.java` 2.
UI (M4, `ai-assistant.js`) — conversation THREAD in `#aiResultsWrap`: while an answer is pending (`isPendingAnswer`: `pending:true` or meeting/action/guided .ask/.confirm) every question + answer is appended (user bubble + card); older turns' buttons disabled ("Replaced by a newer card."); kept in sessionStorage `aa.thread` {active, who = session user, items ≤20} across pages of the tab; restored only for the same login; cleared when a conversation answer is final (done / created / cancelled / refused / denied / expired), by the card's Cancel, or "New conversation" (rejects an open action token + posts "cancel"). `renderCard` split into `cardBody(a, old)` + `wireCard(wrap)`; suggested answers `.aa-choice` (send as typed), review rows `.aa-fields` (Required / Optional badges, `.aa-edit` puts "Change … to " in the bar and focuses it), `_aaServerPending` stays true while the thread is open; voice reads short choice lists. Chat page `ai-assistant.html`: copies `.aa-fields` / `.aa-choices`, delegates choice / edit, disables older buttons (also after reload). Help: AI Actions card section `#hc-ai-guided` (roles + Accountant), HC_KB `ai-guided`, `HelpContentCatalog.ai_actions` v1.1.
TESTS — `ai/AiMeetingCreateServiceTest` 32 (27 updated for the review question + 5 new), NEW `ai/AiGuidedRecordTest` (12), NEW `integration/AiGuidedRecordIT` (4, PostgreSQL 16 — local server via CGP_IT_JDBC_URL: income step by step = one row in A through IncomeService + second Save nothing + B untouched + GUIDED/EXECUTED proposal + duplicate question; B's fund / member invisible to A, guest kept; expense by spoken yes, Admin defaults / User refused; meeting 7:30 PM + review + created), `AiSearchBarVocabularyTest` chat copy list. Playwright (static pages, stubbed APIs) 1280 + 390: 88/88 (thread turns, chips, disabled old turns, review rows + hostile note as text, Edit prefill + focus, newest token only, done ends the thread, thread restored on /income after navigation, New conversation, card Cancel rejects, other login's thread dropped, meeting chips, chat page, no horizontal scroll, no page errors, no dialogs).

**2026-11-08 (later): Guess It bilingual games (e.g. English + Malayalam). Branch `fix/guessit-round-flow-bilingual`, merged to master and pushed 2026-11-08 at the user's request (= deployed). No schema change.**
CLUES — `guessIt.html` `giParseClues` (both Create paths via `giReadGameForm`): one clue per line, but a line in a DIFFERENT script right after a one-line clue is its translation and joins it (`"English\nMalayalam"` = one clue; `giScript` = latin vs other by letter count); if the box contains blank lines, blank lines separate clues instead (multi-line clues). Clue boxes render the line break (`white-space:pre-line` on `.clue-text`, `.gg-clue`, older-clue rows, admin `.clue-pill`, memberHome Guess It clues). OPTIONS — `giParseOptions` strips list bullets (`* - • 1. 2)`) and duplicates. ANSWER — typed answer matched to an option by any language part, separators `| / – —` or spaced hyphen, case-insensitive, NFC (`"Moses – മോശെ"`, `"Moses"`, `"മോശെ"` → option `"Moses | മോശെ"`); no option or two options → refused with a message. Same rule server-side: NEW `util/GuessItText.canonicalAnswer(options, answer)` used by `POST /api/guess-it/admin/games` (which previously did not check the answer against the options at all — now 400 when it names no option), `PUT /admin/games/{id}` and `POST /admin/groups/{id}/games`; the STORED answer is the option's exact text, so play stays an exact comparison. TESTS — NEW `guessit/GuessItBilingualTest` (4) + `GuessItRoundEndTest.bilingualRoundCreatedAndWon`; page parser checked with node on the user's Moses example (5 clues, 10 options, 3 answer spellings). On top of master 8c1d52b: `mvnw clean package` 3,186 tests / 0 failures / 3 skipped. Known flake seen once while developing: `BillingPhase6Test.signatures` (tolerance+1 s edge; passes on re-run).

**2026-11-08: Guess It round flow — a correct answer ends the round; Start publishes a group round; host view refreshes. Same branch/commit as the bilingual entry above (merged + deployed 2026-11-08). No schema change.**
WHY — the host started round 1 of group 5N65KX from Master Controls but players kept seeing "Waiting for the next game": players only see a group round that is PUBLISHED, round 1 had never been published, and the Publish buttons were unreadable (unstyled `.btn-admin` on the dark round rows). The host's participant list also never refreshed. User asked that a winner stop the clock and leave the room ready for the next round.
WHAT — `GuessItController`: `start`, `restart` and `next-game` set `published=true` on a group game; restart now completes only the active game in the SAME lane (was any active game of the church) and takes back the group point a previous winner of that round scored (`@Autowired(required=false) GuessItGroupParticipantRepository`); member `/member/action` correct answer → game `completed` + `completedAt` (was "stays active"); `GET /public` with no active game shows the most recently completed game for 2 h (big screen keeps "Game Over" + winner). `GuessItGroupController.groupAction`: correct answer → `finishRound` (status completed, completedAt); `maybeAutoCloseGroup` now closes only when EVERY game of the group is finished (`GuessItGameRepository.countUnfinishedInGroup`; the published-only count would end a group after its first round now that Start publishes); state without a live game carries `lastRound {gameId, title, winners, youWon, correctAnswer}` (folded into `v`). Repo + `findFirstByClientIdAndStatusAndDeleteFlagFalseOrderByCompletedAtDesc`. `guessIt.html`: waiting players see "🏁 Round over" with the winner / their own point / the answer; group round rows show "Visible to players" / "Hidden" tags and a styled Publish / Hide button (`.gg-pub-btn`); rounds + leaderboard re-render every 4 s while the host has Master Controls open (`ggAdminRenderLive`, `ggAdminLiveTick` — duration select not redrawn); the host's Games list reloads whenever the live state changes. Ratchet `TenantScopedLookupRatchetTest` GuessItController 11 → 12. TESTS — NEW `guessit/GuessItRoundEndTest` (5; removing the finishRound call fails 2). Full unit suite 3,115 run / 0 failures / 3 skipped (9 errors only because the build copy lacked `scripts/sql/migrate_production.sql`).

**2026-11-07: Engagement signals where attendance is not recorded (user: a monthly giver who volunteers showed Watch in a church that takes no attendance). Branch `feature/ios-appstore-readiness`, MERGED to master and DEPLOYED 2026-11-07 ~00:45 CT at the user's request ("Please build, commit, merge and deploy to prod"). No schema or data change; existing snapshot rows are not rewritten — the next nightly run (02:30 CT) writes the new day with the new rules.**
RULE — `EngagementSignalRules.evaluate(f, today, t, churchTracksAttendance)`: attendance rules apply only to people who have been checked in (`lastAttended != null`) in a church that takes attendance (`MemberIntelligenceService.TRACKS_ATTENDANCE_SQL`: any non-deleted attendance_record of the church in the 90 days up to the snapshot day, any status). Everyone else → `evaluateWithoutAttendance`: ENGAGED = gift in the last 90 days OR serving OR in a group; WATCH = gifts in the last 12 months, none in 90; AT_RISK = gifts in the previous 12 months, none in the last 12; QUIET = none of these; NEW never (needs a first attendance date). Last reason line = `CHURCH_NO_ATTENDANCE_NOTE` or `PERSON_NO_ATTENDANCE_NOTE` (shown in the Family badge tooltip). The 3-argument `evaluate` = church takes attendance. Text: Ministry Report definitions + tile tooltips, Help card `#hc-ministry-report` note, `HelpContentCatalog.ministry_report` v1.1.
TESTS — `EngagementSignalRulesTest.withoutAttendance` (+1). Full unit suite 3,176 / 0 failures / 3 skipped (JDK 25.0.5 in the cloud container). MemberIntelligenceIT not re-run (no Docker here); its seed (every expected member has a check-in except Dan, still QUIET) fits the new rules.

**2026-11-06 (latest 10): Side menu = what the server allows (Accountant links, main-section / sub-section rules, Member Portal My Profile + Favorites) + Automations layout. Branch `feature/ios-appstore-readiness`, MERGED to master (fast-forward, 546a8aa) and DEPLOYED 2026-11-06 ~22:40 CT at the user's request ("build, commit, merge and deploy the changes to prod"), together with the Add/Edit Family phone layout (members + roles as cards, Phone/Email full-width rows under the photo — 08efaed, 546a8aa) and the Automations layout (f7240d3). No schema or data change.**
ROOT CAUSES — (1) the staff menu used permission keys + a section-level role list only, while the page routes also apply role guards (e.g. /attendance, /reminders, /certificates, /publicScreens, /followups, /notifyEmail, /ministry = SuperAdmin/Admin/User; /admin/songbook-access, /workflows, /savedLists narrower), so an Accountant saw links that answer Access Denied; (2) viewUsers `syncPermCheckboxes` walked the full PERM_TREE while the checkboxes are rendered from `visiblePermTree()` (plan-hidden options removed) → whenever a plan hides a section (e.g. Activity Corner) every later checkbox showed the wrong state (members' My Profile boxes unticked though saved/defaulted on; staff boxes off by one); (3) `GET/POST /api/members/{id}/permissions` (and member edit / soft-delete / send-reset) used `findById` + `belongsToChurch` touching the lazy family with open-in-view off → LazyInitializationException → 500: Member Portal permissions could not be loaded or saved; (4) memberHome never gated the My Profile sidebar section/items by `member` / `member.*` (only in-page tabs, and only partly); (5) /workflows used the old `.app-shell` wrapper (no side menu).
FIXES — `AiAccessPolicy.navAccess(req, clientId)`: path → may this STAFF login open it, from `AiNavigationCatalog` rules (route role guard + alias-aware page key + main-menu rule + plan feature — the catalogue is cross-checked against the controllers by `AiNavigationCatalogRouteCrossCheckTest`); `/memberHome` always false for staff (no member record — the page signs a staff login out); empty for member, church, Kids Portal, temp-access, NTAG and token sessions. `/api/session` returns `navAccess`; session.js `window.CGP_NAV_ACCESS` + `applyNavAccess()` (inside `applySubscriptionNav`, before `hideEmptyNavSections`) hides only links the server listed as false; the 15 s permission poll also compares navAccess and re-runs the role filter. NEW catalogue entry "Song Book Access" `/admin/songbook-access` staff(ADMIN). viewUsers `syncPermCheckboxes` walks `visiblePermTree()`. AppUserController member lookups → `findByIdAndTenant` (fetch join, tenant-scoped, excludes deleted members). memberHome: Step 3b — `member === false` hides the whole My Profile section (incl. Song Book item), an unticked tab hides only that item; `isMemberTabAllowed` / `applyMemberPrivileges` honour `member === false`. `RoleGuard.requireMemberPermission`: `member` false → every `member.*` refused. workflows.html → standard `.app > aside.sidebar + .main-area > header.topbar + main.page-content`.
DEFAULTS — Member Portal: never-saved members already get Favorites + My Profile (viewUsers ROLE_DEFAULTS.MemberPortal, memberHome `_mbrDefaultPrivs`, `RoleGuard.isMemberDefaultKey`); unchanged. Kids Portal: unchanged (same member page; no separate defaults added — pending the user's decision). Accountant saved with the screen defaults has Admin unticked → no Families (resolves the earlier "Accountant + Family" question by the saved map; a never-saved Accountant still sees Families).
TESTS — NEW `permissions/MenuMatchesAccessTest` (5), `AdminPermissionNavTest` +1 (every shell.js page has the standard sidebar). Full unit suite 3,156 / 0 failures / 3 skipped. Playwright on the live local server: staff scenarios 292/292 at 1280 + 390 (Accountant never-saved / Admin unticked / Admin+Families / Meetings off / Payroll off, User General off + Help Center off, User never-saved, SuperAdmin, Admin; every visible link opens), member scenarios 6/6 + Kids unchanged, permission poll hides Families within ~30 s without reload, no page errors; Automations 14/14.

ADMIN DEFAULTS — `viewUsers.html ROLE_DEFAULTS.Admin` no longer contains `accounting.payroll` (no Accounting key at all). NEW `util/RolePermissionDefaults` mirrors ROLE_DEFAULTS (pinned by `permissions/AdminPermissionNavTest`); `effective(role, saved)` = saved map, else the role's defaults ONLY for roles in `APPLY_WHEN_NOTHING_SAVED` = {Admin} (other roles: nothing saved = everything allowed, as before). PARENT RULE — `RoleGuard.pagePermissionAllows`: a page under a main menu (`MENU_SECTIONS` admin/accounting/general/more) is denied when that menu key is explicitly false. viewUsers `deriveParentKeys(map)` (on save, after `syncLegacyPermissionKeys`): sub-section = any action not false, section = any leaf not false. session.js: a section shows only when its key is not false AND at least one child is permitted; NEW `hideEmptyNavSections()` after the plan-feature pass; the old "Admin sees only Payroll inside Accounting" rules removed from session.js `applyRoleNavFilter` and shell.js (shell.js keeps the Payroll-only rule for church logins, whose Accounting section is hidden anyway); Admin's role lists (session.js ALLOWED, shell.js ROLE_ALLOWED) no longer contain accounting — the section appears only through the permission map. NAV_PERM `/bankSync` → `accounting.bankimport`.
API ENFORCEMENT — NEW `util/PermissionApiCatalog` + `webfilter/PermissionApiFilter` (`PermissionApiFilterConfig`, `/api/*`, order 8; staff sessions only — church, Member Portal and session-less requests pass through) → 403 `{code:PERMISSION_DENIED, permission}`: GET `/api/income` (INC), `/api/expense` (EXP), `/api/reports` (REP); all methods `/api/income|expense/check-scan|check-parse`, `/api/pledges`, `/api/bank-import`, `/api/donations`, `/api/donation-settings`, `/api/payroll`, `/api/plaid/{review,items,link-token,exchange}` (BANK); shared drop-downs any-of (`/api/income/contributors` INC|REP|PLEDGE|BANK, `/api/income/sub-sources` +DON, `/api/expense/purposes|funds` EXP|REP|BANK|DON). Writes on income/expense keep the controllers' edit/delete key checks. This overrides the earlier "no permission checks on data APIs" rule for these accounting APIs only (user request). Bank Sync pages (`PlaidPageController` /bankSync, /bankSyncVerify) now check `accounting.bankimport`; `AiNavigationCatalog` Bank Sync entry carries the key (ai-assistant.js DESTS already did).
LAYOUT — `apiTokens.html` now uses the standard `.app > aside.sidebar#sidebar + .main-area > header.topbar + main.page-content` (was `.app-shell/.content`, no side menu). `accountPrivacy.html` (and `aiDataSharing.html`) lacked the standard base reset (`*{box-sizing}`, `button{font-family:inherit;border:none;background:none}`), so the side-menu buttons rendered in Arial — reset added. Certificate pages also lack the reset (not touched).
TESTS — NEW `permissions/AdminPermissionNavTest` (6) + `permissions/TestPerms.ADMIN_WITH_ACCOUNTING`; tests whose Admin needs finance now grant it explicitly. Full unit suite 3,168 / 0 failures / 3 skipped. Playwright on the live local server (PostgreSQL), 1280 + 390: 90/90 (never-saved Admin, parent off, parent on + no children, Income only, Expense only, Payroll, direct URLs, API 403/200, layout font/width vs Families, SuperAdmin/Accountant/User/Church/Member/Child regression).
OPEN — Accountant + Family: the Permissions screen leaves Admin→Family unticked for Accountant but the role filter shows Families (unchanged, needs a decision). Login page: `/api/ntag-login/session` 401/403 on the login page counts as an ENDPOINT_DENIED failure for the IP throttle (local testing hit the IP block; pre-existing, not changed).

**2026-11-06 (latest 5): App Store readiness D6 — AI data-sharing consent in the iOS / Android app (user-approved 2026-11-06 with decisions A–D + one-time voice tokens). Same branch `feature/ios-appstore-readiness`, committed + pushed as a BRANCH only — NOT merged, NOT deployed. Additive schema: `ai_consent` (Hibernate creates it; guarded block at the end of `migrate_production.sql`; NOT run against production).**
NOTICES — `ai/consent/AiConsentNotices`: VOICE / ASSISTANT / SCAN, version 1 each, wording exactly as approved (Voice notice includes the fund / expense category / payment method names sentence); buttons Cancel / Agree & Continue. RULE: changing text requires a version bump — `AiConsentD6Test.noticeTextPinned` pins version + SHA-256 per notice.
TABLE — `hibernate/AiConsent` (`ai_consent`: app_client_id, login_id = `signup.id` of the INDIVIDUAL login, login_kind STAFF|MEMBER|CHURCH (informational), notice_type, notice_version, agreed_at, withdrawn_at, source IOS|ANDROID, updated_at; unique index `ux_ai_consent_login_type (app_client_id, login_id, notice_type)`). No question/recording/document text. Valid = agreed, not withdrawn, version = current. Every agree/withdraw also writes an `ai_audit_log` row (channel CONSENT).
LOGIN — `LoginController.buildResponse` puts `loginId` (signup.id) on the session; older sessions resolve once by session clientId + username (exactly one live row, else refused "sign in again"). Temp-access, NTAG, API-token and Kids Portal sessions cannot consent (AI refused in the app: `AI_CONSENT_UNAVAILABLE`).
APP DETECTION — `ai/consent/AppShellDetector`: UA marker `ChurchGeniusProApp/` OR header `X-CGP-Client: app` (native-bridge.js adds it + `X-CGP-Platform` to same-origin fetches, app only) OR sticky session flag `cgpAppShell` (set at sign-in / device sign-in / switch account via `markSession`, and by any AI or consent call carrying a signal; never cleared). NOT cryptographic proof — stops accidental bypasses, not deliberate spoofing; App Attest / Play Integrity = possible future enhancement. Mobile repo `scripts/check-config.mjs` fails the build if `appendUserAgent` loses the marker (file written on the user's PC; user pushes).
ENFORCEMENT — `AiGuardFilter` (after temp-access, before the rate limiter), app requests only, FAILS CLOSED (no gate / error → 403 `AI_CONSENT_CHECK_FAILED`): `ai/consent/AiConsentPaths` VOICE `/api/voice/command`; ASSISTANT `/api/ai-search`, `/api/ai-search/data`, `/api/ai/write`, `/api/ai/forms/draft`, `/api/workflows/draft`; SCAN `/api/income/check-scan`, `/api/expense/check-scan`, `/api/bank-import/scan`, `/api/family/scan-extract`, `/api/kids-ministry/child-scan-extract`; DUAL (voice only with a valid token, else ASSISTANT) `/api/ai-assist` (ops home/nav/help), `/api/help/assist` (home/help), `/api/voice/extract` (meeting). No notice: voice status/features, confirm, filter, saved-lists, ai/actions, forms/validate, check-parse / scan-parse (on-device OCR text). Refusal 403 `AI_CONSENT_REQUIRED {notice, version}`. Website requests skip the check entirely (no lookup, no wrapping).
VOICE TOKENS — `ai/consent/AiVoiceTokens` (in-memory, single instance): issued by `/api/voice/command` (response `voiceToken`) ONLY when the filter passed the Voice consent (`ISSUE_ATTR`) and the context has a follow-up; bound to session id + church + login + operation + SHA-256 of the normalised transcript; 5-min TTL; consumed atomically (header `X-CGP-Voice-Token`); expired / reused / unknown / mismatched → 403 `AI_VOICE_TOKEN_INVALID {reason}`; a mismatched token is burnt. The body's `voice` flag never decides. `AiConsentGate` reads the JSON body through `CachedBodyRequest` (≤256 KB) only for a dual path with a token.
DECISIONS — C: `VoiceVocabularyService.promptFor(..., includePeople)`; in the app the hint has no contributor names (fund / category / method names only); website hint unchanged. D: `AiActionService.handle` checks the `aiActions` plan feature BEFORE `llmPick` (no question sent to OpenAI when the plan lacks it).
API — `controller/AiConsentController`: `GET /api/ai/consent` {app, available, notices[]}, `POST /api/ai/consent {type, version}` (current version only, else 409 `AI_CONSENT_OUTDATED`), `POST /api/ai/consent/withdraw {type|all}`; session's own login only. `MemberApiScopeFilter` MEMBER_AREAS + `/api/ai/consent`.
CLIENT — NEW `static/ai-consent.js` (loaded by shell.js and memberHome.html; inert in a browser): `ensure(type)` (in-page dialog, Escape = Cancel, text via textContent), fetch safety net (403 AI_CONSENT_REQUIRED → notice → repeat once; invalid token → repeat without token), `rememberVoice`/token attach to the follow-up with the same text, `[data-ai-consent="SCAN"]` asks before the picker (income/expense Scan Check, family Upload Scanned Form, kids Upload Completed Form; bank import asks after the file is chosen, before upload to OpenAI), `openSettings()` "AI data sharing" (Stop sharing / Stop all). `voice-openai.js`: Voice notice BEFORE getUserMedia in the app (Cancel = nothing recorded), passes `voiceToken` on. Entry points: AI Activity card `#aiShareCard`, memberHome `🔐 AI sharing`, Help Center card `#hc-ai-data-sharing` (button) — app only (`html.cgp-ai-consent-app`). Help: HC_KB `ai-data-sharing`, `HelpContentCatalog.ai_data_sharing` v1.0; aiActivity CHANNEL_LABEL CONSENT.
TESTS — NEW `ai/AiConsentD6Test` (15), `integration/AiConsentIT` (1, PostgreSQL 16); `AiActionAgentTest.planCheckBeforeFallback`. Playwright (stubbed APIs) 390 + 1280: 105/105 ×3; live local server (PostgreSQL, build-1/2 signals = UA marker only, no header/Capacitor) vs browser: 22/22. Full unit suite 3,159 / 0 failures / 3 skipped; ITs 23/23 (AiActionAgentIT, McpIT, WorkflowIT, KidsPortalAgeIT, AiAuditIT, AiConsentIT, MemberIntelligenceIT, PeopleFilterIT).

**2026-11-05: AI drafting fixes — Describe-your-event and Automations "Draft the rule" no longer refuse whole drafts over harmless model format slips. Deployed in commit 835a4b7. No schema change.**
WHY — found while recording the updated tour video on production: `/api/ai/forms/draft` answered 502 for every description, and `/api/workflows/draft` sometimes answered "priority must be LOW, MEDIUM or HIGH". Both validators are strict by design and threw on ONE bad value (blank "hostName", fee 0, "9 AM", "Medium (default)", "2 days"). WHAT — `AiFormDraftService.normalizeModelOutput(node)` runs on MODEL output only, before `validate()`: drops null/blank/empty values and keys the form does not have (logged at INFO by key name), coerces text/number/boolean/maxCapacity, dates (first 10 chars of yyyy-MM-dd…), times via `toHHMM` ("9", "9:30 pm", "noon" → HH:MM; non-times dropped, never guessed), eventType wording, fee 0 → "Free", foodOptions string → list; questions: blank label dropped, type synonyms mapped (dropdown→select, yes/no→yesno, tel→phone …), a choice question with <2 options becomes text, `required` "yes" → true; `unsupported` string → list. `validate()` unchanged and still strict for client JSON (`/api/ai/forms/validate`); a question type outside FIELD_TYPES (e.g. file) still refuses the draft. `WorkflowRuleDrafter.normalizeModelOutput` before `WorkflowSchema.validate`: action params only — blank values dropped, priority wording → LOW/MEDIUM/HIGH (`priority()`; dropped when it means none), unknown tone dropped (known tone lower-cased), dueInDays first 1–3 digit number ("2 days", 2.0 → "2"). Unknown triggers/fields/ops/actions/param NAMES still refused. TESTS — NEW `ai/AiDraftNormalizationTest` (3). Full unit suite 3,123 / 0 failures / 3 skipped (built from a scratch copy in /tmp on the device VM with JDK 25.0.5).

**2026-11-05 (latest): AI features default to Pro/Trial ONLY (user rule, 2026-11-05 10:15 CT): "enabled by default only for Pro and Trial accounts; all other account types unchecked by default; Service Admin can still enable them per plan; don't change existing plan restrictions". Branch `fix/ai-defaults-non-pro-plans` (from master 503cccb). No schema change.**
WHAT — `SubscriptionFeatureCatalog.AI_DEFAULT_ON_PLANS` = {PRO, TRIAL}; `AI_PRO_ONLY_FEATURES` = aiWrite, aiActions, mcp, workflows (the 2026-11 roadmap keys); `aiFeatureKeys()` = the whole AI group (also aiSearch, aiVoice, aiConverse, scanCheck). `SubscriptionPlanSeeder.denyAiFeaturesOutsideProAndTrial(repo)` replaces the FREE/STANDARD-only `denyFeatureOnce` calls: on EVERY start, every plan row whose code is not PRO/TRIAL (FREE, STANDARD and any plan a Service Admin created) gets each roadmap key added as `false` once when the key is missing (a missing key = enabled); a saved true/false is never changed; the older AI keys are not touched (STANDARD keeps aiSearch on, as before). `denyFeatureOnce` now returns whether it added the key. `SubscriptionPlanAdminController.create` → `aiOffByDefault(code, json)`: a NEW plan outside PRO/TRIAL gets every AI-group key `false` unless the request named it `true` (CREATE only; update stores exactly what was ticked). `serviceadminhome.html` plan editor: a new plan opens with the AI group unticked (note "off by default on every plan except Pro and Trial"); ticked AI boxes are saved as explicit `true` so the server default never hides them; existing plans show their saved state. HOW A SERVICE ADMIN ENABLES AI FOR ANOTHER ACCOUNT TYPE — serviceadminhome → Subscription Plans → edit the plan (or create one) → tick the AI features → Save; the change applies to every church on that plan (plan-level, like every other feature; there is no per-church AI override apart from the existing per-church Voice settings). VISIBLE ON NEXT PROD START: any custom plan rows gain the four keys as false (logged "now carries <key>=false").
TESTS — `subscription/AiPlanDefaultsTest` (3: seeder across FREE/STANDARD/PRO/trial/ENTERPRISE/BASIC-with-saved-true/non-JSON — 15 keys added, saved values and older AI keys untouched, idempotent; create defaults incl. explicit true kept, PRO/TRIAL untouched, null/non-JSON; editor + seeder + controller source pins). `McpPhase5Test`/`WorkflowPhase6Test` wiring assertions updated. Full unit suite: see below.

**2026-11-05 (latest): AI Phase 6 — Workflow automation: a trigger → condition → action engine with a template gallery, describe-to-build drafting and run history (roadmap "ChurchGeniusPro – AI Competitive Gap Analysis and Development Plan"; user "Approved for all"; local commits per phase approved, push/merge/deploy still need the user's go). Branch `feature/ai-phase6-workflows` (from `feature/ai-phase5-mcp` 4319ec7), committed locally, NOT merged, NOT deployed. Additive schema only (`workflow_definition`, `workflow_run`; Hibernate creates both; guarded block at the end of `migrate_production.sql`). The ONLY model call is rule drafting on the page; the engine itself is deterministic.**
SAFETY SHAPE — every rule starts DISABLED; whoever saves or enables it becomes its OWNER and every run uses that owner's permissions, re-read on each run; staff-facing actions only (a rule never messages members, visitors or donors — recipients are staff logins of the church); the same event never runs a rule twice; per-church daily cap (`workflow.runs-per-tenant-per-day=200`, CAPPED rows after); `workflow.enabled=${WORKFLOW_ENABLED:true}` = engine kill switch (off → `WorkflowEvents.publish` does nothing, listener inert, zero rows); plan feature `workflows` (`SubscriptionFeatureCatalog`, `/api/workflows`; seeder FREE + STANDARD `"workflows":false` + `denyFeatureOnce` → VISIBLE ON FIRST PROD START; PRO/TRIAL on).
VOCABULARY — NEW `workflow/WorkflowSchema` (closed; `validate(Map|JsonNode)` → `Rule` or IllegalArgumentException naming the first problem): TRIGGERS with declared facts: `VISITOR_RECORDED` (name, kind, peopleCount, interest, followUpRequired, newVisitor, visitCount, serviceName, date), `ATTENDANCE_SAVED` (name, memberId, serviceName, status, method, date), `FOLLOW_UP_CREATED` (title, status, priority, assignedTo, linkedType, linkedLabel, createdBy, hasDueDate), `PRAYER_REQUEST_CREATED` (title, status, requesterName, assigned), `GIFT_RECORDED` (amount, fund, guest, memberId, name, date, quickAdd), nightly `ENGAGEMENT_SIGNAL_CHANGED` (name, memberId, familyId, signal, previousSignal, date) and `FOLLOW_UP_OVERDUE` (title, assignedTo, daysOverdue, linkedType, linkedLabel, priority); OPS eq/neq/contains/gt/gte/lt/lte/in/is_true/is_false/exists (type-checked against the fact: gt on a string refused, is_true on a non-boolean refused, numbers must parse); ACTIONS `create_follow_up` (title*, description, assignTo, dueInDays 0–365, priority LOW/MEDIUM/HIGH; needs the owner's Follow-ups), `notify_user` (title*, message*, to), `email_staff` (subject*, body*, to), `add_family_note` (title*, body*; needs Families; only on triggers that carry memberId), `draft_email` (prompt*, tone; needs Follow-ups + plan feature aiWrite); ≤10 conditions, ≤5 actions; unknown trigger/field/op/action/param → refused. `fill()` replaces `{{fact}}` placeholders. `describe()` = the vocabulary for the page and the drafter prompt. Facts never carry contact details or notes.
EVENTS — NEW `workflow/WorkflowEvent` (trigger, clientId, eventKey = idempotency key, subject, facts, occurredAt) + `workflow/WorkflowEvents` (@Component; `publish` is null-safe, no-op when disabled or the trigger is unknown, never throws). FIVE publication points, each an `@Autowired(required=false) WorkflowEvents workflowEvents` field so hand-built tests keep their constructors: `VisitorCheckInService.register` (one event per registration, key `visitor:<primaryId>:<date>:<targetId>`, newVisitor = any person not matched), `AttendanceService.checkInMember` (only when the row is NEW — a re-save is not a check-in; key `attendance:<id>`), `FollowUpController.create` (`followup:<id>`), `PrayerRequestController` create (`prayer:<id>`), `IncomeService.createIncome` (`income:<id>`, after the save, before the pledge hook). Delivery: Spring events; the engine's `@TransactionalEventListener(phase = AFTER_COMMIT, fallbackExecution = true)` so a rolled-back row never fires a rule and non-transactional callers fire at once.
ENGINE — NEW `workflow/WorkflowEngine.handle(event)`: for every ENABLED rule of the church for the trigger — (1) `runs.existsByDefinitionIdAndEventKey` → nothing (idempotent; the unique index `idx_wf_run_def_key` also refuses a racing duplicate); (2) daily cap → CAPPED; (3) owner re-read by `AppUserRepository.findByIdAndClientId` (disabled / deleted / moved / non-staff → DENIED), permission map from `UserPermissions` else `app_user.privileges`, resolved to an `AiAccess` through `AiAccessPolicy.resolve` on an `OwnerRequest` (NEW `workflow/OwnerRequest`: a `TokenSession` with the sign-in attributes behind a `java.lang.reflect.Proxy` HttpServletRequest — no token markers, nothing persisted); (4) `WorkflowActions.check` for EVERY action before anything runs — one refusal → DENIED ("Not run — the rule owner no longer has the Follow-ups page", unknown staff recipient, aiWrite plan) and nothing executes; (5) conditions → SKIPPED with the failing one named; (6) actions in ONE `TransactionTemplate` (REQUIRES_NEW — AFTER_COMMIT listeners sit in a finished transaction) → DONE, or FAILED (class name only in the log, nothing kept). Each outcome = a `WorkflowRun` (status, subject, plain-text log lines, duration) saved in its own REQUIRES_NEW transaction; DONE bumps `run_count`/`last_run_at`. `dryRun(def, facts)` for the page: conditions trace + "Would …" lines, nothing written. NEW `workflow/WorkflowActions`: `create_follow_up` → `FollowUp` PENDING, assignee = owner's username or the staff login's name, `created_by = automation:<owner>`, due = church-today + N, linked MEMBER (facts.memberId) or VISITOR (facts.visitorId); `notify_user` → `WebPushService.logAndSendToUser(appUser.user_id, cid, "staff", …, "/followups", "cgp-workflow-<defId>")`; `email_staff` → `EmailService.sendOrgEmail(staff e-mail, …)` with a footer naming the rule (skips with a log line when the login has no e-mail / mail not configured); `add_family_note` → `Note` "Family note — <last name>: <title>" by "Automation (<owner>)" (skips when the member is not this church's); `draft_email` → `AiWritingService.write(draft, email, prompt, tone)` → saved as a follow-up "Draft: <subject>" for the owner (never sent). Recipients: `owner`, an `app_user` id, its `user_id` or its e-mail — enabled staff of the same church only. NEW `workflow/WorkflowNightlyService` (`workflow.nightly-cron=0 20 3 * * *` Chicago, after the 02:30 snapshot; only churches with an enabled rule for the trigger): `signalChanges` (latest snapshot day vs the previous snapshot day, per member, key `signal:<memberId>:<day>`), `overdue` (PENDING/MISSED follow-ups past due, key `overdue:<id>:<today>` → once per task per day). `AuditTableRetentionService` + `workflow_run` 180 d (`audit.retention.workflow-run-days`; constructor gained a 13th parameter; test updated).
TEMPLATES + DRAFTER — NEW `workflow/WorkflowTemplates` (ten, validated at class load: first_time_visitor, interested_visitor, third_visit, family_visit, at_risk_member, quiet_member_note, overdue_follow_up, high_priority_followup, new_prayer_request, large_gift). NEW `workflow/WorkflowRuleDrafter.draft(description ≤1,000)` → `OpenAiVoiceService.chatJson(systemPrompt, description, 0)` (system prompt = the vocabulary JSON + rules: recipients are "owner" unless a staff member is named, never a member/visitor, `{"refusal": …}` when it cannot be expressed, age-neutral) → `WorkflowSchema.validate` decides; result is a DRAFT for the form, never saved by the drafter.
API + PAGE — NEW `workflow/WorkflowController` (gate: staff session, not church/Member/Kids/temp/token, role SuperAdmin or Admin): page `GET /workflows` (+ page key `more.workflows`), `GET /api/workflows` → {engineEnabled, draftEnabled, rules[] (view incl. `broken` when the stored JSON no longer passes the schema), templates, vocabulary, staff[] (enabled staff id/name/role), maxRules 50}, `POST /api/workflows {rule | templateKey}` (created OFF, owner = creator, duplicate name gets " (2)", ≤50 rules, 400 with the schema's sentence), `PUT /{id}` (editor becomes owner; name clash 409), `POST /{id}/enable {enabled}` (enabler becomes owner; a broken rule cannot be enabled, 409), `DELETE /{id}` (soft + off), `GET /api/workflows/runs` and `/{id}/runs` (last 50, per church, with the log), `POST /{id}/test {facts?}` (dry run on `sampleFacts(trigger)` merged with supplied facts coerced to the declared types; unknown keys dropped), `POST /api/workflows/draft {description}` (AI Assistant permission; 503 when the model is off; 422 refusal; 200 validated rule + `draft:true`) — the one AI channel: `AiAuditService.CHANNELS` `/api/workflows/draft` → WORKFLOWS (rate limit + audit; `aiActivity.html` CHANNEL_LABEL WORKFLOWS "Automations"). Ratchets: `ApiPermissionCheckRatchetTest` BASELINE + `workflow/WorkflowController.java` 1; `MemberApiScopeTest` KNOWN_STAFF_ONLY + `/api/workflows`. NEW permission key `more.workflows` ("Automations", More) in `PermissionCatalog`, `viewUsers.html PERM_TREE` (standalone), session.js page map + SECTION_CHILDREN + feature map `/workflows` → `workflows`; nav More → Automations ⚙ (shell.js); `AiNavigationCatalog` `staff(ADMIN, "more.workflows")` + `ai-assistant.js DESTS`; Help: card `#hc-workflows` (members; SuperAdmin,Admin; perm more.workflows; feature workflows), HC_KB `workflows`, `HelpContentCatalog.workflows` v1.0; `AssistantKnowledgeTest` MENU_TO_ARTICLE + `/workflows`. NEW `static/workflows.html`: rule cards (name, trigger label, counts, owner, runs, last run, On/Off switch → `/enable`, Edit, Runs, two-click Delete), template gallery (+ Add → editor opens on the new rule), editor (describe → ✨ Draft fills the form; name/trigger/description; condition rows field/op/value from the trigger's facts; action blocks with per-action params — owner/staff selects for to/assignTo, priority, tone, due-in-days —; Save POST/PUT; ▶ Test run shows outcome, sample facts, condition trace and "Would …" lines), Runs card (church-wide or per rule, outcome pills Done/Skipped/Denied/Capped/Failed/Test + log lines), engine-off and plan-refusal notices, `[hidden]` rule out-ranking buttons.css, every error in-page, hostile text escaped.
NOT DONE (deliberate) — no OR / grouping between conditions (AND only); no scheduled "every Monday" trigger (events + the two nightly passes only); no member-facing sends (plan v1 rule); no tags / add-to-list actions (the app has no tags; saved lists are filters, not static lists); no AI classification step (drafting is the only model call); no undo of a run (each run is listed and the created follow-up / note is a normal record); the per-church cap and nightly pass run on a single instance (same ShedLock caveat as every scheduler); `ATTENDANCE_SAVED` fires for member check-ins only (visitors come through `VISITOR_RECORDED`); the Prayer trigger covers the staff page's create path, not the public Connect card (`PublicEngagementService`).
TESTS — `ai/WorkflowPhase6Test` (7: schema — valid rule, 17 refusals, placeholders, ten templates validate, describe(); operators — numbers vs strings, dates, in, exists, booleans, missing facts, trace lines; engine — DONE through the owner (follow-up fields, due date, VISITOR link, push to a named staff login, run_count), same event twice → one run, SKIPPED, DENIED ×3 (owner disabled, Follow-ups permission false → before any action, owner moved), CAPPED at the limit, FAILED stops the chain with no exception text, other church, disabled engine → no tx/no rows, publisher off never publishes, unknown trigger dropped; actions — e-mail to the owner's address with footer, family note, AI draft → follow-up, recipient validation (member address / unknown id refused; staff e-mail / id / user_id accepted), aiWrite plan gate, dry run writes nothing, unknown member → note skipped not failed; nightly — one event per changed signal with facts, no previous day → none, overdue with day count, future/undated ignored, engine off → nothing; API — 401/403 gates (Member, User role, church), page forward, list shape, template create (off, owned, "(2)"), 400 messages, User cannot create, PUT changes owner + 409 clash + 404, enable → owner + 409 broken + `broken` flag in the list, test run (DRY, sample facts, coercion, unknown keys dropped, no push), runs per church, soft delete, draft 503/403/422 ×2/200 not saved + system prompt contents; wiring — feature/seeder/properties/migration/channel/retention, the five publication points and five triggers, AFTER_COMMIT + REQUIRES_NEW, staff-only sends scan, nav/session/PERM_TREE/catalog/help/activity label/page). `integration/WorkflowIT` (4, PostgreSQL 16, two churches, real services: a real visitor registration runs A's enabled rule after commit (follow-up in A, assignee, VISITOR link), not B's, not the disabled one; the same event republished → one run; returning visitor → SKIPPED; owner's Follow-ups permission revoked via `user_permissions` → DENIED and nothing written, restored → DONE on a real `IncomeService.createIncome` (MEMBER link, e-mail attempt logged), small gift SKIPPED, B untouched; the ten templates dry-run and run on a real family visit + $1,000 gift as DONE/SKIPPED only (first_time DONE, interested DONE, third_visit SKIPPED, family DONE, large_gift DONE); history per church, retention purges only the aged run, migration block twice, unique index refuses a duplicate run). Playwright workflows.html + helpCenter (static pages, stubbed APIs) 1280 + 390: 72/72 ×3 (list with escaped names/trigger/owner/runs/on-state, toggle → enable POST + refresh, template add → POST key + editor, editor filled from a rule incl. selects, test run output, add condition + action with staff recipient → PUT shape, new rule: empty name refused, describe → draft fills the form, 422 shown, save → POST + off hint; runs church-wide and per rule with escaped subject and pills; two-click delete; engine-off warning; plan refusal; help card; no dialogs, no horizontal scroll). Full unit suite 3,117 / 0 failures / 3 skipped; WorkflowIT 4/4 on PostgreSQL 16.

**2026-11-05: AI Phase 5 — External MCP integration: personal access tokens + a stateless MCP server at `/mcp` for Claude Desktop / ChatGPT / any MCP client (roadmap "ChurchGeniusPro – AI Competitive Gap Analysis and Development Plan"; user "Approved for all"; local commits per phase approved, push/merge/deploy still need the user's go). Branch `feature/ai-phase5-mcp` (from `feature/ai-phase4-action-agent` 87dfd68), committed locally, NOT merged, NOT deployed. Additive schema only (`personal_access_token`, `mcp_tenant_setting`; Hibernate creates both; guarded block at the end of `migrate_production.sql`). No model call anywhere in this phase (the CLIENT's model reasons; the server only answers tool calls).**
THREE SWITCHES, ALL MUST BE ON — server `mcp.enabled=${MCP_ENABLED:true}` (off → every token 503, no new tokens); plan feature `mcp` (`SubscriptionFeatureCatalog`, gates `/api/tokens`; `SubscriptionPlanSeeder` FREE + STANDARD `"mcp":false` + `denyFeatureOnce(... "mcp")` → VISIBLE ON FIRST PROD START: the two plan rows gain the key once; PRO/TRIAL on); the church's own opt-in `mcp_tenant_setting.enabled` (NEW `hibernate/McpTenantSetting`, no row = OFF; only SuperAdmin/Admin flip it on the AI Connections page). All three are re-checked on EVERY request.
TOKENS — NEW `hibernate/PersonalAccessToken` (`personal_access_token`: app_client_id, app_user_id, username, name, token_prefix (first 12 chars for the list), token_hash (SHA-256 hex, unique index `idx_pat_hash`), scopes CSV, created_at, expires_at, last_used_at (written at most once a minute), revoked_at, revoked_by; index `idx_pat_client_user`) + `repository/PersonalAccessTokenRepository` (hash lookup, per-church / per-user lists, `findByIdAndAppClientId`, live count, `@Modifying revokeAll`). NEW `mcp/PersonalAccessTokenService`: secret `cgp_` + 32 random bytes base64url, shown ONCE, never stored; `create(cid, AppUser, username, name, scopes, expiryDays)` (name ≤80, expiry 1–365 d default 90, ≤10 live per person); SCOPES closed set `read` (implied) · `contact` (e-mail/phone/address unmasked) · `giving` (finance data at all) · `actions` (Phase 4 write tools, confirm-gated); `authenticate(raw)` → `Outcome(Principal | Refusal{status, code})`: INVALID_TOKEN 401, TOKEN_REVOKED 401, TOKEN_EXPIRED 401, USER_INACTIVE 401 (disabled / deleted / moved church — `AppUserRepository.findByIdAndClientId`), STAFF_ONLY 403 (role must be SuperAdmin/Admin/Accountant/User), PLAN 403, TENANT_OFF 403, MCP_DISABLED 503; the Principal carries the owner's saved permission map (`UserPermissionsRepository`, else `app_user.privileges`) exactly as sign-in loads it. `view()` never exposes the hash.
REQUEST PRINCIPAL — NEW `mcp/TokenSession`: an in-memory `HttpSession` for ONE request filled with the staff sign-in attributes `LoginController.buildResponse` sets (clientId, username, church=false, role, appClientId, appUserId, names, privileges, `privilegesCheckedAt`) plus markers `apiToken=true`, `apiTokenId`, `apiTokenScopes`; `TokenSession.wrap(req, session)` makes `getSession()` return it — so `RoleGuard`, `SessionUtil`, `AiAccessPolicy`, the action agent all work unchanged; nothing is persisted (spring-session never sees it, no cookie). `AiAccessPolicy.resolve`: a token session WITHOUT `giving` has `fin=false` and `pledges=false` whatever the owner's permissions (everything else = the owner's own access). NEW `webfilter/ApiTokenFilter` (+ `ApiTokenFilterConfig`, `/mcp` + `/mcp/*`, order 0; `FilterConfig` unchanged): `Authorization: Bearer` required (401 + `WWW-Authenticate: Bearer`; a cookie session is never accepted here), `authenticate` → 401/403/503 JSON `{error, code}`, per-token minute window (`mcp.rate.per-token-per-minute=60`, 0 = off) then the shared `AiRateLimiter` (per user/minute, per church/day) → 429 + Retry-After, refusals audited (channel MCP, RATE_LIMITED), any failure inside the filter fails CLOSED (401), then the chain runs with the wrapped request. `/mcp` is outside `/api/*`, so AuthFilter / MemberApiScopeFilter / CsrfOriginFilter / AccountStatusFilter / SubscriptionFeatureFilter / AiGuardFilter never see it — this filter is the whole gate (plan + rate limit + audit included); the `/*` filters (TempAccess, PrivatePage, NTAG, KidsPortalAge) pass a session-less request through.
SERVER — NEW `mcp/McpController` `POST /mcp` (JSON-RPC 2.0 over Streamable HTTP, STATELESS: no SSE stream, no `Mcp-Session-Id`; `GET`/`DELETE /mcp` → 405): `initialize` (protocolVersion negotiated from 2025-06-18 / 2025-03-26 / 2024-11-05 — `McpJsonRpc.PROTOCOL_VERSION` pins the default; capabilities tools only; `instructions` tell the client it is one church, the owner's permissions, age-neutral wording, contact withheld without the scope, and that writes are propose-then-confirm or that the token is read-only), `ping`, `tools/list` (name/title/description/inputSchema/annotations — `readOnlyHint` false for the write tools), `tools/call` → `{content:[{type:text, text:<pretty JSON>}], structuredContent, isError}`; notifications → 202 empty; `resources/*`, `prompts/*` → -32601; batch arrays, bad JSON, wrong jsonrpc/method/id → -32600/-32700; unknown tool OR a tool outside the token's scopes → -32602 "Unknown tool" (the list is the contract). Each call: `AiAccessPolicy.resolve/crossAccess` on the wrapped request → `McpToolAdapter.Ctx` → tool → `McpRedactor.redact` unless scope `contact` → ONE `ai_audit_log` row (channel MCP, intent = tool name, outcome OK / DENIED (isError) / ERROR; a tool exception is `isError` with a fixed sentence, never a 500). NEW `mcp/McpJsonRpc` (parse/result/error), `mcp/McpToolAdapter` (`name/title/description/inputSchema/requiredScope/writes/call`, `schema()` helper), `mcp/McpRedactor` (keys matching e-mail/phone/mobile/cell/address/street/zip/postal → `EmailMask` / `PhoneMask` / `[address withheld]`; every other string gets e-mail addresses and phone-shaped digit runs masked in place — `EmailMask.maskAll` + `PhoneMask.maskAll`; numbers, dates, names untouched).
TOOLS — NEW `mcp/McpToolCatalog` (closed list; every service `@Autowired(required=false)` so a missing one answers "not available"): READ `ask_church_data {question ≤500}` → `AiDataSearchService.answer(req, cid, q)` (the WHOLE assistant data route: structured answers, cross-reference, people filters, denials as `isError`), `search_people {predicates[1..12], limit≤500}` → `PeopleFilter.fromJson` (closed predicate set; unknown kind → "Invalid predicates") → `PeopleFilterService.run` (module gating = decision 1 of the cross-reference phase), `list_saved_lists` / `run_saved_list {id}` (SuperAdmin/Admin/Accountant + page key `more.savedlists`, tenant-scoped finders, updates last_run/last_count), `ministry_report {year?, month?}` → `MinistryReportService.monthly` with `Access(cross.attendance, a.incomeRecords, cross.groups, a.volunteers, a.followUps)` (default = last completed month; future month refused; no section → refused), `engagement_summary` → `MemberIntelligenceService.summary` (needs `cross.attendance`) + rule definitions. WRITE (scope `actions` AND plan feature `aiActions`): `list_actions` (the Phase 4 registry filtered by `allowed`), `propose_action {tool, params}` → `AiActionService.propose(..., source "MCP")` (ok intents action.ask / action.confirm; anything else `isError`), `confirm_action {token}` → `AiActionService.confirm` (ok = action.done; same user + church + expiry + permission + by-id re-resolution exactly as the assistant), `cancel_action {token}` → `reject`. No tool sends anything (test scans the mcp package for EmailService/SmsService/WebPush/WhatsApp).
PAGE + API — NEW `controller/ApiTokenController`: page `GET /apiTokens` (staff session, not Member/Kids/church/temp, not a token session, AI Assistant permission); `GET /api/tokens` → {serverEnabled, planAllows, tenantEnabled, admin, scopes, defaultExpiryDays, maxExpiryDays, endpoint (X-Forwarded-Proto/Host aware), mine[], all[] (admins)}; `POST /api/tokens {name, scopes[], expiresDays}` → the row view + `token` (once) + note; 503 server off, 403 PLAN / TENANT_OFF codes, 403 `actions` scope for non-admins ("a write token is the church's decision"), 400 validation, 409 at 10 live; `DELETE /api/tokens/{id}` (own; admins any of the church; 404 otherwise, idempotent); `POST /api/tokens/revoke-all` (admins); `PUT /api/tokens/settings {enabled}` (admins; on requires the plan). Ratchets: `ApiPermissionCheckRatchetTest` BASELINE + `controller/ApiTokenController.java` 1; `MemberApiScopeTest` KNOWN_STAFF_ONLY + `/api/tokens`. NEW `static/apiTokens.html` (`/apiTokens`, nav More → AI Connections 🔗 in shell.js; session.js `/apiTokens` → feature `mcp`, page key `more.aiassistant`, member OPT_IN_NAV): church switch (admins), + New token (name, expiry 30/90/180/365, scopes with the Actions row for admins only) → secret shown once with Copy, tokens table (name, prefix, owner (you), scope chips, created/expires/last used, status pill, Revoke — two clicks within 6 s, no dialog), Revoke all (two clicks), "Connect a tool" with the endpoint and ready snippets (Claude Desktop custom connector, `mcp-remote` config, curl); plan refusal (403 SUBSCRIPTION_FEATURE_DISABLED) → upgrade notice + create card hidden; `[hidden]` rule out-ranks buttons.css's `display:inline-flex !important` (`.btn-danger[hidden]`); an ok/error notice survives the list refresh, only stale warnings are cleared. `AiNavigationCatalog` + `ai-assistant.js DESTS` entry; Help: card `#hc-ai-connections` (members; SuperAdmin,Admin,Accountant,User; perm more.aiassistant; feature mcp), HC_KB `ai-connections`, `HelpContentCatalog.ai_connections` v1.0; `AssistantKnowledgeTest` MENU_TO_ARTICLE + `/apiTokens`. `aiActivity.html` already labels channel MCP "External (MCP)".
