NOT DONE (deliberate) — no OAuth 2.1 / dynamic client registration (plan: later increment; bearer PATs now); no SSE stream / server notifications / resources / prompts (stateless tools-only server); not yet tried from a real Claude Desktop / ChatGPT (needs a deployed HTTPS URL — acceptance item for the user after deploy; the protocol shape follows the 2025-06-18 spec and is unit-tested); the per-token rate window is in-memory (single instance, like `AiRateLimiter`); `last_used_at` is written at most once a minute; no per-tool scopes beyond read/contact/giving/actions; Service Admin has no cross-tenant token view (admins of the church see theirs).
TESTS — `ai/McpPhase5Test` (8: token lifecycle — hash at rest, prefix, scopes closed, limits, every authenticate refusal incl. disabled/deleted/moved/non-staff/plan/church/server switch, saved permission map, 10-live cap, revoke owner-only vs admin vs other church, revokeAll; filter — 401 without/with a bad token, cookie session refused, wrapped request carries the sign-in + scopes, per-token 429 + Retry-After + audit row, other token's own window, shared limiter 429, mid-day church switch 403, broken check fails closed; endpoint — initialize negotiation, notifications 202, ping, tools/list by scope (6 read / 10 with actions, annotations), scope-less action tool = unknown, method not found, batch/parse/invalid-request errors, no-token 401, GET 405, controller never trusts a cookie session; tools/call — ask delegates with the owner's session, redaction of keys and inline text, contact scope passes through, validation, denied answer = isError + DENIED audit, service failure = isError without the message, search_people predicates + cap 500 + invalid kind, ministry_report Access finance by giving scope + default month + bad months, engagement needs Attendance (Accountant refused), actions gate scope + plan, propose source MCP, confirm/cancel; AiAccessPolicy giving-scope narrowing; redactor; /api/tokens gates + flows; wiring/architecture), `integration/McpIT` (4, PostgreSQL 16, two churches through the REAL filter + controller on the full context: A's token lists A's adult only with masked e-mail (HasContact column), contact scope unmasks, B sees two, ask_church_data per church, saved list of B "not found" for A, run updates last_count; lifecycle — no token 401, bad 401, revoked 401 next request + secret absent from the table, church switch 403 for A only, B's admin cannot revoke A's; actions — read token = unknown tool, propose stores source MCP, B's actions token cannot confirm A's proposal (nothing written), confirm writes exactly one group_member row in A, second confirm no-op; audit rows per call with actor/role/intent/outcome + migration block twice). Playwright apiTokens.html + helpCenter (static pages, stubbed APIs) 1280 + 390: 70/70 ×3 (admin table with escaped hostile name / pills / owner / chips, switch, revoke-all, snippets, create validation + request shape + secret once + list by prefix, 409 in the panel, two-click revoke and revoke-all, switch off → PUT + disabled create, User view (own tokens, no switch, no actions scope, admin-off notice), plan refusal, help card; no dialogs, no horizontal scroll). Full unit suite 3,110 / 0 failures / 3 skipped; McpIT 4/4 on PostgreSQL 16.

**2026-11-05: AI Phase 4 — Action Agent: the assistant proposes a change, the user confirms, then it is written (roadmap "ChurchGeniusPro – AI Competitive Gap Analysis and Development Plan"; user "Approved for all"; local commits per phase approved, push/merge/deploy still need the user's go). Branch `feature/ai-phase4-action-agent` (from `feature/ai-phase3-writing-forms` 62eb3b4), committed locally, NOT merged, NOT deployed. Additive schema only (`ai_action_proposal`; Hibernate creates it; guarded block at the end of `migrate_production.sql`). RULE OF THE PHASE: NOTHING IS WRITTEN WITHOUT A FRESH CONFIRM — every tool execution runs only from `POST /api/ai/actions/confirm` with a one-time token, and the record is re-resolved by id and re-authorised at confirm time.**
SCHEMA — NEW `hibernate/AiActionProposal` (`ai_action_proposal`: app_client_id, username, tool, params_json (resolved ids + labels), summary, status PROPOSED|EXECUTED|REJECTED|EXPIRED|FAILED, token (UUID, one use), expires_at, created_at, decided_at, source TEXT|LLM|VOICE, result_json; indexes `idx_ai_action_client_status`, `idx_ai_action_token`) + `repository/AiActionProposalRepository` (`findByTokenAndAppClientIdAndStatus`, top-50 per church / per user, `@Modifying expireOverdue(now)` run on every list call). No question text is stored, only the tool, its resolved parameters and the summary sentence.
TOOLS — NEW `ai/action/AiTool` (name/label/description/parameters; `allowed(req, AiAccess, CrossAccess, cid)`; `resolve(raw, cid, username)` → `Resolution.ok(resolved) | ask(question, choices) | refuse(reason)`; `summary(resolved)`; `execute(resolved, cid, username)`), `ai/action/AiToolRegistry`, `ai/action/AiActionLookup` (NamedParameterJdbcTemplate, every query tenant-scoped: live members by name (first/last/nick/"first last", exact → contains) or id, families, groups (exact → contains → stem without group/team/ministry/class), membership test, member e-mail, volunteer roles, open follow-ups (words, optional assignee), meeting types) and `ai/action/AiTools` — SIX tools, each with the page's own permission rule: `add_follow_up` (a.followUps(); FollowUpRepository, PENDING, linked MEMBER when a person was named, assigned to the caller), `complete_follow_up` (a.followUps(); open task by words → COMPLETED), `add_family_note` (a.members() = Families page rule; NoteRepository, title "Family note — <family>"), `add_group_member` (crossAccess.groups() AND `admin.groups.edit`; `GroupMemberService.addMember` 6-arg with the member id; already a member → refused), `create_meeting` (a.meetingsEdit(); `MeetingService.save`; same type + same date/time → refused as a duplicate), `assign_volunteer` (a.volunteers() AND SuperAdmin/Admin; `VolunteerAssignment` pending for a date). Ambiguous name → ASK with up to 6 choices; nobody → refused ("I couldn't find …"); confirm-time re-resolution is BY ID (`memberById`, `groupById`, …) so a renamed or deleted record fails safe. A tool never sends mail/SMS/push (architecture scan in the test: no EmailService/SmsService/WebPush in `ai/action`).
SERVICE — NEW `ai/action/AiActionService` (`FEATURE="aiActions"`; session attrs `aiActionDraft` / `aiActionPendingToken`): `handle(req, cid, question)` runs from `AiDataSearchController` AFTER `AiMeetingCreateService` (so the conversational meeting flow is the untouched regression baseline) — order: yes/no to a pending token (→ `confirm` / `reject`), answer to an open clarifying question (`pick(answer, choices)` by number or name; another action phrase replaces the draft; anything else drops it and returns null so the question goes on to the other routes), deterministic parse (`parse(q, today)`: `P_GROUP` "add X to [the] Y", `P_FOLLOWUP_ADD` "add a follow-up for X: title due <date>", `P_REMIND` "remind me to …", `P_FOLLOWUP_DONE` "mark/complete/close … [as done]", `P_NOTE` "add a note to the X family: …", `P_ASSIGN` "assign X as Y on <date>"; how-to wording (`HOWTO`) never; "meeting" phrases are left to the meeting flow), then LLM fallback (`ai.actions.llm-fallback`): `openAi.chatJson(system, q, 0)` with `AiToolRegistry.describe()` → `{tool, params}` or `{tool:null}`; a tool name outside the registry or a tool the caller may not use → ignored. Feature off for the plan → `action.unavailable` (only when the phrase looked like an action). `propose(...)` → intents `action.denied` (permission), `action.refused`, `action.ask` (`choices`), `action.confirm` (`confirm {token, proposalId, label, endpoint:/api/ai/actions/confirm, summary rows incl. Expires}` + "Shall I go ahead?"); `confirm(req, cid, token)`: same church AND same user, PROPOSED, not expired, feature + `allowed` re-checked, re-resolved by ids, executed in a `TransactionTemplate` → EXECUTED (`action.done`, link to the page) or FAILED (`action.failed`, nothing partial); expired → `action.expired`; `reject` → REJECTED (`action.cancelled`). `date(text, today)`: today/tomorrow/weekday/next <weekday>/ISO/M/D[/Y]/"<Month> D". Kill switch `ai.actions.enabled=${AI_ACTIONS_ENABLED:true}` (off = no proposals, every confirm refused), `ai.actions.llm-fallback=${AI_ACTIONS_LLM_FALLBACK:true}`, `ai.actions.ttl-minutes=10`. `AiAccessPolicy.feature(cid, key)` made public.
API — NEW `controller/AiActionController`: `POST /api/ai/actions/confirm {token}`, `POST /api/ai/actions/reject {token}`, `GET /api/ai/actions` (admins: whole church `all:true`; Users: own; `enabled`), `GET /api/ai/actions/tools` (the caller's allowed tools); gates = `AiWritingController.gate` (401; 403 Member / Kids / temp-access / church login / AI Assistant permission off). Plan feature `aiActions` in `SubscriptionFeatureCatalog` (`/api/ai/actions`); `SubscriptionPlanSeeder` FREE + STANDARD `"aiActions":false` + `denyFeatureOnce(... "aiActions")` (VISIBLE ON FIRST PROD START: the two plan rows gain the key once; PRO/TRIAL on). `AiAuditService.CHANNELS` + `/api/ai/actions` → ACTIONS (rate limit, temp-access block, audit row per call). Ratchets: `ApiPermissionCheckRatchetTest` BASELINE + `ai/action/AiTools.java` 1 (the `admin.groups.edit` check); `MemberApiScopeTest` KNOWN_STAFF_ONLY + `/api/ai/actions`.
UI — `ai-assistant.js`: the confirm card is generic (`d.confirm.label` on the button, `d.confirm.endpoint` posted; `.aa-cancel` posts `/api/ai/actions/reject` for action cards, the meeting card keeps `/api/ai-search/confirm` / its own cancel), "Working…" while posting, `action.failed` shown in place, result links to the page; `_aaServerPending` includes `action.ask` / `action.confirm` so the next message (a name, "yes") goes to the server even though it is not a data question; `looksLikeAction(q)` routes action verbs to the data route before navigation. `ai-assistant.html` gets the same by delegation. `aiActivity.html`: "Proposed actions" card (`#actionsCard`, `data-feature="aiActions"`; when / by / action / summary / status pill / source "Typed | AI picked the tool | Voice"; "Whole church" vs "Your own proposals"); a 403 hides the card AND removes its `data-feature` (session.js re-applies `display` to every `[data-feature]` when the plan loads — found by Playwright); CHANNEL_LABEL + WRITE/FORMS/ACTIONS. Help: card `#hc-ai-actions` (members; SuperAdmin,Admin,User; perm more.aiassistant; feature aiActions), HC_KB `ai-actions`, `HelpContentCatalog.ai_actions` v1.0.
NOT DONE (deliberate) — no voice entry point (source VOICE reserved; the voice pages keep their own flows); no multi-step plans (one tool per proposal); no e-mail/SMS/push tools (plan rule: draft only, never send); no undo (the proposal row + the page's own history is the trail); the existing `AiMeetingCreateService` conversational flow is unchanged (both can create meetings; the deterministic `create_meeting` tool only runs via `propose`/LLM fallback, because `MEETING` phrases are left to the older flow); the in-memory draft/pending state lives on the HTTP session (single conversation per login).
TESTS — `ai/AiActionAgentTest` (7: parser golden phrases incl. "Team" kept in a group name, dates, pick; state machine yes/no/ask/switch/drop; confirm re-checks — other user, other church, expired, used token, feature off, permission lost, record gone → nothing written; clarifying questions; each tool's allow rule + summary + refusals; LLM fallback incl. unknown tool / disallowed tool / `{tool:null}`, kill switch, feature off → unavailable; endpoints 401/403/200 and the admin-vs-own list), `integration/AiActionAgentIT` (4, PostgreSQL 16, two churches: group add + duplicate refusal + church B cannot see A's John; tampered params with A's ids under church B → refused, nothing written; follow-up add/complete, family note, volunteer assignment, create_meeting via `propose` → `confirm` all land in the live tables with the right tenant; migration block twice). Playwright (static pages, stubbed APIs) 1280 + 390: 50/50 (Home: action phrase → data route → card with label/summary/escaped answer/draft note → Confirm posts the token to `/api/ai/actions/confirm` (never the meeting endpoint) → Done + page link; Cancel → reject; clarifying question has no button and the answer goes to the server; chat page by delegation; AI Activity table with escaped hostile summary, status labels, source, admin note, card hidden on 403; no dialogs, no horizontal scroll). Full unit suite 3,102 / 0 failures / 3 skipped; AiActionAgentIT 4/4 on PostgreSQL 16.

**2026-11-04: AI Phase 3 — AI writing in the e-mail / SMS composers + describe-to-build on the Event form (roadmap "ChurchGeniusPro – AI Competitive Gap Analysis and Development Plan"; user "Approved for all"; local commits per phase approved, push/merge/deploy still need the user's go). Branch `feature/ai-phase3-writing-forms` (from `feature/ai-phase2-member-intelligence` 3438e9f), committed locally, NOT merged, NOT deployed. NO schema change. First phase that CALLS THE MODEL (gpt-4o-mini via `OpenAiVoiceService.chatJson` → `LlmGateway`); every result is a DRAFT — nothing here sends.**
PLAN FEATURE — NEW key `aiWrite` in `util/SubscriptionFeatureCatalog` (`/api/ai/write`, `/api/ai/forms`). `config/SubscriptionPlanSeeder`: FREE + STANDARD seeds carry `"aiWrite":false`; NEW `denyFeatureOnce(repo, code, key)` adds `"aiWrite":false` to an EXISTING FREE/STANDARD plan row whose JSON lacks the key (missing = allowed, so old rows would have had it on) — an explicit true/false set by an admin is never changed; PRO/TRIAL untouched (on). VISIBLE ON FIRST PROD START: the FREE and STANDARD plan rows gain that key once (logged). `ai/audit/AiAuditService.CHANNELS` + `/api/ai/write` → WRITE, `/api/ai/forms` → FORMS (so AiGuardFilter rate-limits, blocks temp-access and audits both; intents `write.<action>.<channel>`, `forms.draft`, `forms.validate`). `OpenAiVoiceService.chatJson(system, user, temperature)` overload (the 2-arg one keeps temperature 0).
WRITING — NEW `ai/write/AiWritingService`: allow-lists ACTIONS draft|rewrite|shorten|expand|fix|translate, TONES warm|formal|casual|urgent|encouraging|celebratory, LENGTHS short|medium|long, CHANNELS email|sms, 16 LANGUAGES; caps prompt 2,000 / text 8,000 chars; draft needs a prompt, every other action needs text (the selection or the whole body). `systemPrompt`: writes DRAFTS; SMS = plain text ≤160 chars, no greeting/sign-off, NEVER STOP wording (SmsService adds it when sending); e-mail = plain paragraphs, no HTML/markdown, length by words; never invent facts (placeholders like [date]); NEVER mention age / birth year / years married (age-neutral rule — and no member data or DOB is ever in the prompt: the model sees only the request + selected text + optional church name); JSON reply `{body[, subject]}`. Temperature 0.6 (0.1 for fix). Output → `sanitize()`: `<script>/<style>` blocks with content, every tag (also entity-encoded), control chars removed; capped 6,000 (e-mail) / 320 (SMS); `note` when an SMS draft is over 160. NEW `controller/AiWritingController` `POST /api/ai/write` → `{action, channel, subject?, body, chars, note?, draft:true}`; gates (shared `gate()`): 401 no session; 403 Member / Kids / temp-access / church-owner / AI Assistant permission off; 400 validation; 503 no OpenAI key; 502 model failure. No `EmailService`/`SmsService`/WhatsApp anywhere in the Phase 3 classes or `ai-write.js` (pinned, comments excluded). Ratchet `ApiPermissionCheckRatchetTest` BASELINE + AiWritingController 1.
UI — NEW `static/ai-write.js` (`CGP_AI_WRITE.mount({id, channel, target, subject?, anchor?, channelOf?, onInsert?})`, `CGP_AI_WRITE.css()`): "✨ Draft with AI" button (`data-feature="aiWrite"` → session.js hides it on FREE/STANDARD) + inline panel (prompt, tone, length — fixed "One text (≤160)" for SMS —, language; Draft / Rewrite / Shorten / Expand (e-mail only) / Fix grammar / Translate; preview with the subject, chars and "draft — review before sending"; Insert = replaces the selection, else the whole body; Append; Copy; an empty subject is filled from the draft, a typed one kept). Works on a `<textarea>` or a contenteditable (`#contentEditor`: paragraphs via `toHtml`, text escaped; the last non-collapsed selection inside the editor is remembered on `selectionchange` because clicking the panel collapses it). Every error in the panel (no dialogs). Mounted: `notifyEmail.html` (toolbar `#aiWriteEmailAnchor`, `onInsert → onContentInput()`; page now loads `cgp-api.js` in `<head>`), `volunteers.html` (`volCmpEmailBody`+`volCmpSubject`, `volCmpSmsBody`), `kidsMinistry.html` (`kmCmpEmailBody`+`kmCmpSubject`, `kmCmpSmsBody`), `attendance.html` (`vnBody`+`vnSubject`; `channelOf` = sms when only the SMS box is ticked).
FORM BUILDER — NEW `ai/write/AiFormDraftService`: `draft(description, today)` (≤2,000 chars) → model JSON validated by `validate()` against a CLOSED schema: `event` = ONLY the Event form's own fields (eventName, eventType One Day|Multiple Days, eventDate, endDate, startTime/endTime HH:MM, registrationEndDate, fee, maxCapacity int, allowMaybeRsvp, showRegistrants, foodAvailable (+ foodLabel default "Dietary Preferences"), foodOptions[], accommodation*, host*, note) — unknown key / bad date / bad time / end before start → refused; `questions` = extra registration questions with `type` ∈ FIELD_TYPES text|textarea|email|phone|number|date|yesno|checkbox|select|radio|section (unknown type → refused), label, required, options (select/radio only, ≥2); `unsupported[]`. Invalid model output → null (502), never a throw. `questionsAsNote()`. NEW `controller/AiFormBuilderController`: `POST /api/ai/forms/draft {description}` (same gates) and `POST /api/ai/forms/validate` (schema check without a model call; unknown field type → 400 `valid:false`). `event.html`: "✨ Describe your event" (`#aiFormSection`, `data-feature="aiWrite"`) → preview of fields / questions / "Not available in this form" → "Fill the form" copies the values into the real fields (radio + `onEventTypeChange`, multi-day → `addDayRow` per day, food/accommodation toggles via their handlers) and puts note + "Registration questions: • …" into the Quill note (`setNoteHtml`) when the checkbox is ticked; the user still clicks Save (nothing is posted).
WHY THE FORM BUILDER LOOKS LIKE THIS — the plan assumed a form designer with custom fields; the app has none (events have fixed registration fields + food options; the membership form is fixed). So describe-to-build fills the Event form and carries the extra questions as note text. A real custom-field form module (storage, public renderer, registrant answers) is a separate decision — NOT built.
HELP — card `#hc-ai-writing` (communication; SuperAdmin,Admin,Accountant,User; perm more.aiassistant; feature aiWrite), HC_KB `ai-writing`, `HelpContentCatalog.ai_writing` v1.0. Properties: comment block only (same key/model as Converse; no new switch).
NOT DONE (deliberate) — no church-name / signature context in prompts (page does not know it; optional `churchName` field exists); no draft history; no WhatsApp composer (sending disabled in code anyway); no per-member personalisation (would need member data in prompts — against the no-PII rule); no streaming.
TESTS — `ai/AiWritingPhase3Test` (6: request allow-lists + caps; prompts carry the SMS 160 / no-STOP / age-neutral / no-invented-facts rules and only prompt+text reach the model (captured), temperatures, SMS over-160 note, model off → null; sanitiser incl. script bodies and entity-encoded tags; form schema valid draft + 12 refusals + invalid model output → null; endpoints 401/403×4/400/502/503/200 for write, forms draft + validate; architecture no-send scan, catalog + channel mapping, seeder `denyFeatureOnce` adds once / never overrides / empty JSON). Playwright (static pages, stubbed APIs + stubbed CDN) 1280 + 390: 82/82 (e-mail: button, empty prompt refused, request shape, escaped preview, insert as paragraphs with subject fill, rewrite on the selection only with subject kept, server error in panel, button hidden on a plan without aiWrite; SMS: one-text length, no Expand, insert, shorten on whole text, append; Event: describe → preview with escaped name / questions / unsupported → fill every field incl. food options + host + note with questions, nothing posted; no dialogs, no horizontal scroll). Full unit suite 3,095 / 0 failures / 3 skipped.

**2026-11-04 (latest): AI Phase 2 — member intelligence (engagement snapshots + signals), Family 360 badge, monthly Ministry Report (roadmap "ChurchGeniusPro – AI Competitive Gap Analysis and Development Plan"; user "Approved for all"; local commits per phase approved, push/merge/deploy still need the user's go). Branch `feature/ai-phase2-member-intelligence` (from `feature/ai-phase1-people-filters` 6983e71), committed locally, NOT merged, NOT deployed. Additive schema only (`member_engagement_snapshot`; Hibernate creates it; guarded block at the end of `migrate_production.sql`). No LLM call anywhere in this phase — every signal and every report sentence is a deterministic rule or template.**
SNAPSHOT — NEW `hibernate/MemberEngagementSnapshot` (`member_engagement_snapshot`: app_client_id, member_id, snapshot_date, attendance_30/90/365 (distinct Present/Late days), first_attended, last_attended, gifts_90/365/prior_365 (income ROW COUNTS — never amounts), last_gift, groups_count (confirmed: member link or an e-mail only they use), serving (non-declined assignment in the last 6 months or undated), signal, reasons_json, created_at; indexes `idx_mes_client_day_member`, `idx_mes_client_member_day`) + `repository/MemberEngagementSnapshotRepository`. NEW `ai/intel/MemberIntelligenceService`: `@Scheduled(ai.intel.snapshot-cron = 0 30 2 * * *, America/Chicago)` `nightly()` over `SubscriptionService.activeAccountClientIds()` (`ai.intel.enabled`); `snapshot(cid, day, force)` = ONE native query (`FACTS_SQL`, every table with its own tenant column: attendance_record.client_id, income.app_client_id, app_group.app_client_id (group_member rule of the cross-reference phase), volunteer_assignment.app_client_id, family.app_client_id) over the church's LIVE members (not deleted / inactive, family likewise) → rows; idempotent per church+day (-1 when the day exists; `force` replaces); failures per church are logged by class name only. Reads: `latestFor`, `summary(cid, onOrBefore)`, `family(cid, familyId, finance)` (members of the family incl. inactive ones, signal null without a snapshot; `finance=false` drops the giving facts AND filters out gift reasons — `isGivingReason`). Retention: `AuditTableRetentionService` + `member_engagement_snapshot` 730 d (`audit.retention.member-engagement-snapshot-days`; constructor gained a 12th parameter; test updated).
RULES — NEW `ai/intel/EngagementSignalRules` (pure: `evaluate(Facts, today, Thresholds)` → `Outcome(signal, reasons)`; thresholds `ai.intel.new-days`=90, `regular-min-days`=3, `at-risk-gap-days`=42, `watch-gap-days`=30), in order: NEW = first attendance < 90 days ago; QUIET = no attendance, gift, group or serving in 365 days (incl. people with no records); AT_RISK = ≥3 attendance days between 90 and 30 days ago, none in the last 30 and last attendance ≥ 42 days ago, OR gifts in the prior 12 months, none in the last 12 and no attendance in 90 days; WATCH = attendance in the last 90 but none in 30 and last ≥ 30 days ago, OR gifts in the year but none in 90 days with no attendance in 30, OR no attendance in 365 days while a group / serving / gift keeps them "active"; ENGAGED otherwise. Reasons are fixed sentences with counts and ISO dates only.
FILTERS — `PeopleFilter.Engagement(signal)` (module Attendance; labels Engaged / Watch / At risk / New people / Quiet; `fromJson` allow-list) → SQL `EXISTS` on the member's LATEST snapshot row of this church (people without a snapshot never match). Parser (`PeopleFilterParser`): signal words are matched and CUT before everything else ("at risk / drifting / disengaging / falling away", "new people / newcomers / new attenders / new faces / new members / first timers", "watch list / needs attention", "quiet / dormant / gone quiet / no activity", "engaged / most engaged / most active / core members") so "new attenders" is the NEW signal, not an attendance count; each is a strong (claiming) condition. Definition sentence added in `PeopleFilterService.definitions`.
FAMILY 360 — `GET /api/families/{id}/engagement` (FamilyController, `familyPageGuard` first → Users/Members without grant 403; church logins 403 by the guard): `allowed` = staff session with the Attendance page rule (`AiAccessPolicy.crossAccess().attendance()`), giving facts/reasons only with `incomeRecords()`; other church's family 404; `MemberIntelligenceService` is `@Autowired(required=false)` so hand-built controller tests keep their constructor. `family.html`: `loadEngagement(id)` after `populateFamily` → `.eng-badge eng-<signal>` beside each member in the members table and (primary member) in `#pFirstNameHint`; tooltip = signal + snapshot date + reasons; unknown signal values ignored; a failed call never blocks the page.
MINISTRY REPORT — NEW `ai/intel/MinistryReportService.monthly(cid, year, month, Access, today)`: per period (month, previous month, same month last year — `period()`), attendance (check-ins, distinct days, distinct members, distinct visitors, avg/day), visitors (first-time = `attendance_visitor.first_visit_date` in month; returning = checked in during the month with an earlier first visit), follow-ups (created / completed by `COALESCE(updated_at, created_at)` / open = PENDING|MISSED created ≤ month end), giving (finance only: total, gifts, distinct givers, top-8 funds — gross amounts), groups (live groups + distinct people by member id else e-mail, AS OF TODAY), volunteers (assignments dated in the month, distinct volunteers, declined); `engagement` = counts by signal on the latest snapshot ≤ month end + previous month's; `narrative` = template sentences with % comparisons (`compare`/`compareMoney`, none when the base month had nothing); `definitions`; `partial` when the month is not over. `Access(attendance, finance, groups, volunteers, followUps)` — each section only when allowed; every query tenant-scoped (`COUNT(*) FILTER` = PostgreSQL). NEW `controller/MinistryReportController`: page `GET /ministryReport` (staff session, not church, page key `more.ministryreport`), `GET /api/ministry-report?year&month` (default = last completed month; 401 / 403 member, kids, church, temp, permission off; 400 bad month / future month / year < 2000; 403 when no section is open; Access from `AiAccessPolicy`: attendance+groups = crossAccess, finance = incomeRecords, volunteers, followUps) and `GET /api/ministry-report/csv` (BOM, Section/Metric/month/previous/last-year columns, giving-by-fund rows, engagement rows, formula guard; finance rows only for finance viewers). NOT an AI channel (no audit row, no rate limit — nothing calls a model). NEW `static/ministryReport.html` (`/ministryReport`): month picker (25 months, default last completed), narrative, tiles with % vs previous / last year, giving table, engagement tiles with rule tooltips, "How this was worked out", ⬇ CSV, 🖨 Print (print CSS), CGP_api + in-page Retry. Nav More → Ministry Report (shell.js); session.js `/ministryReport` → `more.ministryreport` (+ SECTION_CHILDREN); `viewUsers.html PERM_TREE` + `more.ministryreport`; `PermissionCatalog` + `more.ministryreport`; `AiNavigationCatalog` `staff(STAFF, "more.ministryreport")` + `ai-assistant.js DESTS`; Help: card `#hc-ministry-report` (members; SuperAdmin,Admin,Accountant,User; perm more.ministryreport), HC_KB `ministry-report`, `HelpContentCatalog.ministry_report` v1.0; `AssistantKnowledgeTest` MENU_TO_ARTICLE + `/ministryReport`.
NOT DONE (deliberate) — no LLM narrative (template only; the plan's optional model summary can sit on top later); no per-member trend history page (snapshots are kept 24 months for it); the groups section is "as of today", not month-end (group_member has no join date); no e-mail/PDF delivery of the report; the snapshot runs on a single instance (no ShedLock — same limit as every scheduler); the rate limiter / audit do not cover the report (not an AI call).
TESTS — `ai/EngagementSignalRulesTest` (4: every signal from its facts + boundaries (day 89/90, gap 35/45), reasons = fixed vocabulary + numbers + dates, purity, thresholds; Engagement predicate parser words incl. canonical questions, JSON allow-list, SQL tenant columns + named signal parameter; FACTS_SQL tenancy / no amounts / no PII columns, Family 360 map without finance, report comparisons/definitions), `ai/MemberIntelligenceEndpointTest` (3: Family 360 gates — Admin with/without Income, Accountant allowed:false, attendance off, church/member/User 403, other church 404; report gates 401/403 ×5, default month, Access per role (Admin all, User no finance/groups, Accountant finance only), every section off → 403, bad input ×5; CSV BOM/columns/formula guard/finance rows), `integration/MemberIntelligenceIT` (4, PostgreSQL 16, two churches + B rows pointing at A people: 7 rows per church with the expected signal each, idempotent/force, facts vs hand SQL (duplicate day, deleted/ABSENT/other-church rows never count, deleted gift), no names/amounts in the table; Engagement predicate incl. gating by Attendance, Family 360 finance vs not + other church null, summary counts; report vs hand SQL (check-ins, visitors, follow-ups, giving total, groups, engagement), finance-less report has no $ or giving, other church separate, empty month; migration block twice + 730-day purge removes only aged rows). Playwright (static pages, stubbed APIs) 1280 + 390: 60/60 (report tiles/%/giving/hostile fund escaped/5 signal tiles with tooltips/month change/failure + Retry/CSV URL/User without finance; Family page badges incl. primary, hostile signal ignored, hostile name escaped, allowed:false → no badges; no dialogs, no horizontal scroll). Full unit suite 3,089 / 0 failures / 3 skipped; MemberIntelligenceIT 4/4 + PeopleFilterIT 4/4 on PostgreSQL 16.

**2026-11-04 (latest): AI Phase 1 — composable people filters + Saved Lists (roadmap "ChurchGeniusPro – AI Competitive Gap Analysis and Development Plan"; user "Approved for all"; local commits per phase approved, push/merge/deploy still need the user's go). Branch `feature/ai-phase1-people-filters` (from `feature/ai-phase0-foundation` f9ac8fb), committed locally, NOT merged, NOT deployed. Additive schema only (`ai_saved_people_list`; Hibernate creates it; guarded block at the end of `migrate_production.sql`). No LLM call anywhere in this phase.**
MODEL — NEW `ai/filter/PeopleFilter` (record of a closed set of sealed `Predicate`s, ANDed): `AgeBand(min,max)`, `BirthMonth`, `Role(Head|Spouse|Adult|Child)`, `Gender(Male|Female)`, `Status(active|inactive|deleted|any)`, `MemberType(Member|Guest|Visitor|NonMember)`, `HasContact(negated, email|phone)`, `Attended(negated, minDays, from, to, label)`, `Gave(negated, from, to, label, fund)`, `InGroup(negated, group)`, `Serving(negated, since)`, `InCampaign(negated, campaign)`, `RegisteredForEvent(negated, event)`. `toJson()` / `fromJson()` (allow-list: unknown kind, bad number/date/enum → IllegalArgumentException; ≤12). Each predicate names its module (Members, Attendance, Income, Groups, Volunteers, Pledges, Events) and a chip label.
PARSER — NEW `ai/filter/PeopleFilterParser` (deterministic; input = the AI Search canonical question; `Context` = the church's fund / group / campaign / event names + the ai.crossref thresholds): lifts age ranges ("between 18 and 30", "over 60", "older than 60"→61+, "under 18", "65+" via `preNorm` before `norm` drops the plus, adults 18+, teens 13–19, seniors 65+, young adults 18–30) and birth months first, then splits on but/and/who/that/which/while/comma; per clause NEGATION BY POSITION (a negating word negates only the conditions AFTER it: "regular attenders not in a group" = attendance positive, group negated; "gave nothing" negated); church names matched longest-first with both spellings of a possessive ("Men's Breakfast" ↔ "mens breakfast"), cut out of the clause so "Youth Group" is a group, not teenagers; funds are NOT cut because "tithe" is also the giving word (a fund named like the word used restricts to it). Periods: "since <month|year|easter|christmas>", "in the last N days/weeks/months", `AiDataSearchService.parsePeriod`, bare year; giving default this year; attendance default last 12 weeks (negated: last 4 weeks). CLAIM RULE: the filter route takes a people question only with ≥2 conditions, or 1 condition the older routes cannot express (age, gender, birth month, type, status, named group, campaign, event, contact) — single giving / attendance / volunteers / children questions keep their routes; how-to wording never. An event verb without a known event name → clarify.
SQL — NEW `ai/filter/PeopleFilterQueryBuilder`: one native statement, `family_member fm JOIN family f` (f.app_client_id = :cid), status rule (default active = not deleted/inactive, member or family), one `EXISTS`/`NOT EXISTS` (or column test) per predicate with its own tenant column (attendance_record.client_id, income.app_client_id (+ sub_source for a fund), app_group.app_client_id via group_member with the member-link-or-unique-email rule of the cross-reference queries, volunteer_assignment.app_client_id (not declined, dated ≥ since or undated), pledge_member/pledge_campaign.client_id, event_registration.client_id + church_event.app_client_id by unique e-mail), only named params (:p0…, :today, :limit), `COUNT(*) OVER()`, `LIMIT :limit`; age = `CASE WHEN` valid birth date `THEN EXTRACT(YEAR FROM age(:today, make_date(y, m, LEAST(d, 28))))` (null without a complete date → never matches). Columns: id, names, role, member_type, gender, age, email, phone, last_attended (when an attendance predicate), gave_total (when a giving predicate), total.
SERVICE — NEW `ai/filter/PeopleFilterService` (@PersistenceContext EntityManager; `setEntityManager` for tests): `route(original, canonical, AiAccess, CrossAccess, cid, today)` (null unless claimed; Member / Kids / church sessions never), `run(filter, …[, cap])`, `emails(filter, …, cap)`; PERMISSIONS = decision 1 of the cross-reference phase: every module of the filter must pass (`missing()`: Members = a.members() i.e. the /viewfamily rule, Attendance/Groups = crossAccess, Income = a.incomeRecords(), Volunteers, Pledges, Events) or the WHOLE question is refused (intent `filter.denied`, `missing` list); windows > `ai.search.max-range-days` → `narrow`; answers (`filter.people`) carry columns/rows (Name, Role, +Type/Age/Last attended/Gave/Email/Phone by predicate), total/shown/truncated, `memberIds`, sources, definitions ("combined with AND"…), `filter {predicates, labels}` for the chips, link /viewfamily. `AiDataSearchService`: `setPeopleFilter` (field-injected, null in old tests); the route runs AFTER `AiCrossReferenceService` (so the five existing questions answer first, unchanged) and BEFORE the volunteers / giving / structured intents. `AiCrossReferenceService.toDate` made public.
API — NEW `controller/AiPeopleFilterController` (all under `/api/ai-search` → plan feature `aiSearch`, AiGuardFilter, audit channel DATA): `POST /api/ai-search/filter {predicates}` (chip ✕ re-run; staff + AI Assistant permission), `GET|POST /api/ai-search/saved-lists`, `PUT|DELETE /…/{id}`, `POST /…/{id}/run` (updates last_run_at/last_count), `GET /…/{id}/emails` (distinct addresses, cap 2,000, `withoutEmail` count), `GET /…/{id}/export` (CSV, BOM, formula guard, cap 2,000) — saved-list calls need SuperAdmin/Admin/Accountant + page key `more.savedlists`; a list can only be SAVED when the session may run it; ≤200 lists per church, unique name (case-insensitive); the list stores the filter JSON, never the people. NEW `hibernate/AiSavedPeopleList` + `repository/AiSavedPeopleListRepository`. Ratchet `ApiPermissionCheckRatchetTest` ceiling +1 (the one `requireFeature` in `gate()`).
UI — `ai-assistant.js`: filter answers render chips (`.aa-chips`, ✕ only when >1 condition) + "Save as list" (`window.prompt` for the name → POST saved-lists; toast "Saved — find it under More → Saved Lists"), the filter JSON travels in a `<script type="application/json" class="aa-filter-json">`; `rerunFilterWithout`, `saveFilterAsList` exported on `window.CGP_ASSISTANT`; `looksLikeDataQuestion` / `looksSpecific` know the people words (women, men, adults, teens, seniors, guests, born, aged, over/under N, no email…). `ai-assistant.html` (chat page) copies `.aa-chips/.aa-filter-json` into the bubble and wires ✕ / Save by delegation. NEW `static/savedLists.html` (`/savedLists`, `AiActivityPageController.savedLists`: staff SuperAdmin/Admin/Accountant, not church, page key `more.savedlists`): cards (name, chips, last run / count / by), ▶ Run (result card with table, definitions), ⬇ Download CSV, ✉ Email this list (→ `sessionStorage cgpSavedListEmails` → `/notifyEmail` selects Group Email and adds the addresses as tags — `notifyEmail.html init()` consumes it once), ✏️ Rename (prompt), 🗑 Delete with in-page confirm (refresh from server after). Nav More → Saved Lists (shell.js; session.js `/savedLists` → `more.savedlists`, feature `aiSearch`); `viewUsers.html PERM_TREE` + `more.savedlists` (More); `PermissionCatalog` + `more.savedlists`; `AiNavigationCatalog` + `ai-assistant.js DESTS` entries; Help: AI Search card gets a "People filters" section, NEW card `#hc-saved-lists` (members, SuperAdmin,Admin,Accountant, perm more.savedlists, feature aiSearch), HC_KB `saved-lists`, `HelpContentCatalog.saved_lists` v1.0.
NOT DONE (deliberate) — no LLM parse fallback (`ai.filter.llm-fallback` from the plan; deterministic only); no OR between conditions; no "has open follow-up" predicate (member follow-ups store no member id — same limit as the cross-reference phase); Users (role) cannot use people filters or Saved Lists (the Families page rule); voice vocabulary hints for fund/group names not added.
TESTS — `ai/PeopleFilterParserTest` (6: 40+ golden phrases, negation per clause, names incl. possessives, claim rule, JSON round-trip + injection refusals), `ai/PeopleFilterSqlTest` (4: every table carries its tenant column, named params only, LIMIT, no question text in SQL; status/negation SQL; gating without DB; `AiDataSearchService` route order — cross-reference first, composed question → filter, single intents untouched), `ai/AiPeopleFilterControllerTest` (4: 401/403 gates incl. Member/temp/User/permission off, JSON validation, tenant-scoped CRUD/run/export with formula guard, save-only-what-you-may-run + duplicate + cap), `integration/PeopleFilterIT` (4, PostgreSQL 16, two churches + B rows pointing at A ids: every predicate vs hand SQL, group link/unique-email/shared-email rule, deleted gift/attendance, combinations, other church's own rows, answer shape, cap, gating/narrow). Playwright (static pages, stubbed APIs) 1280 + 390: 46/46 (Home chips → ✕ re-run → Save; Saved Lists run/refusal/rename/in-page delete/CSV disabled on refusal/e-mail hand-off into notifyEmail Group Email; hostile names escaped; no horizontal scroll). Full unit suite 3,082 / 0 failures / 3 skipped (after the AiSearchBarVocabularyTest selector ratchet was updated).

**2026-11-04 (latest): AI Phase 0 — foundation & security for the AI roadmap (report "ChurchGeniusPro – AI Competitive Gap Analysis and Development Plan", claude.ai doc, 2026-11-04; user: "Approved for all" phases 0–6, implemented one phase at a time). Branch `feature/ai-phase0-foundation` (from master 28cb654), NOT committed, NOT merged, NOT deployed. Additive schema only (`ai_audit_log`; Hibernate creates it; guarded block at the end of `migrate_production.sql`).**
GATEWAY — NEW `ai/llm/LlmGateway` (@Component, also usable as `new LlmGateway()`): the ONE door for OpenAI HTTP — one retry on 429/5xx/transport error (never on timeout; `Retry-After` honoured, ≤2 s), body capped at 2,000,000 chars, `usage`/`model` read from the body and published on the current web request (`LlmGateway.USAGE_ATTR`; several calls in one request are summed), process counters (`stats()`). `OpenAiVoiceService` (transcribe, command, extract, assist, chatJson) and `VisionCheckService` (image, text) call `gateway.send(req, purpose)` — their own `HttpClient`s are gone; `@Autowired(required=false) setGateway` keeps the no-arg constructors the tests use. Ratchet `AiPhase0FoundationTest.everyOpenAiCallGoesThroughTheGateway`.
AUDIT — NEW `hibernate/AiAuditLog` (`ai_audit_log`: client_id, actor, role, channel, path, intent, model, prompt/completion tokens, http_status, outcome OK|DENIED|RATE_LIMITED|ERROR, duration_ms, created_at; indexes `idx_ai_audit_client_time`, `idx_ai_audit_client_day_actor`) + `repository/AiAuditLogRepository` (keyset page, per-channel summary, Service-Admin per-tenant summary) + `ai/audit/AiAuditService` (`CHANNELS` path→channel map: /api/ai-search[/data|/confirm], /api/ai-assist, /api/voice, /api/help/assist, the six vision scan paths; `intent(request, key)` / `intentFrom(request, answerMap)` annotate the request; `record(...)` never throws). NO question text, answer or PII beyond the username is ever stored. Controllers annotate intent: `AiDataSearchController.audited()` (also sets `ai.denied` when the answer map has denied=true → outcome DENIED), `AiSearchController`, `VoiceCommandController.aiAssist` ("assist.<intent>"), `HelpController.assist` ("help.text|voice"). Retention: `AuditTableRetentionService` + `ai_audit_log` 90 d (`audit.retention.ai-audit-log-days`; constructor gained an 11th parameter).
GATE — NEW `webfilter/AiGuardFilter` (+ `AiGuardFilterConfig`, `/api/*`, order 8; FilterConfig constructor unchanged) on the CHANNELS paths only, after Auth(1)/MemberScope(2)/AccountStatus(6)/Subscription(7): (1) temporary-access session (`tempAccessId`) → 403 `AI_TEMP_ACCESS` (S1; `ai.gates.temp-access.enforce`, false = WARN only); (2) NEW `ai/AiRateLimiter` — per user per minute (`ai.rate.per-user-per-minute`=60) and per church per church-calendar day (`ai.rate.per-tenant-per-day`=5000), in memory (single instance), 0 disables a window, `ai.rate.enforce=false` = WARN only → 429 `AI_RATE_LIMITED` + `Retry-After` + scope (S4); (3) after the chain writes exactly ONE audit row (refusals included). Any failure inside the filter fails open and is logged. Properties with env overrides `AI_AUDIT_ENABLED`, `AI_RATE_*`, `AI_TEMP_ACCESS_ENFORCE` in application.properties.
POLICY — `AiAccessPolicy`: `isTemporaryAccess(session)`; `resolve` and `crossAccess` return none for it; `feature()` now FAILS CLOSED when clientId is null/blank (was true; `subscriptions == null` = unit tests still true). `util/SubscriptionFeatureCatalog`: `aiConverse` + `/api/help/assist`; `scanCheck` + `/api/income/check-scan`, `/api/bank-import/scan`, `/api/bank-import/scan-parse`, `/api/family/scan-extract`, `/api/kids-ministry/child-scan-extract` → VISIBLE CHANGE: FREE plans lose the Help assistant and every scan; STANDARD loses the Help assistant and income/bank/form scans (plan seeder unchanged: STANDARD has no scanCheck/aiConverse). `SubscriptionFeatureFilter`: a plan lookup error for a KNOWN tenant now fails CLOSED on `/api/*` (403 `SUBSCRIPTION_CHECK_FAILED` + ref, logged WARN) and still open for pages; errors before the tenant is known pass as before.
PERMISSION CATALOGUE — NEW `util/PermissionCatalog` (the 65 keys of `viewUsers.html PERM_TREE` with label + section; `keys()`, `isKnown()`); viewUsers is NOT rendered from it (page unchanged) — ratchet `AiPhase0FoundationTest.permissionCatalogInSync` fails when the two drift. `GET /api/permissions/catalog` (admin or church).
PAGE — NEW `static/aiActivity.html` (`/aiActivity`, `AiActivityPageController`, requireAdminOrChurch; nav Admin → AI Activity in shell.js, allowed for church logins in session.js `applyChurchNavFilter` (`isAiLog`)): summary tiles + per-feature table for 7/30/90 days + the caller's rate-limit usage; recent requests 10 at a time with keyset Load More (CGP_api, Retry on failure, hostile text escaped). NEW `controller/AiActivityController`: `GET /api/ai/activity?limit&beforeTs&beforeId`, `GET /api/ai/activity/summary?days`, `GET /api/serviceadmin/ai/activity?days` (all tenants + gateway stats; ServiceAdminAuthFilter), `GET /api/permissions/catalog`. Help: card `#hc-ai-activity` (roles SuperAdmin,Admin,Church), HC_KB `ai-activity`, `HelpContentCatalog.ai_activity` v1.0; `AiNavigationCatalog` + `ai-assistant.js DESTS` entry for /aiActivity (ratchets `AssistantKnowledgeTest` MENU_TO_ARTICLE, `ChurchHelpCenterNavTest` updated).
NOT DONE (deliberate) — viewUsers PERM_TREE still rendered from the page (catalogue is the mirror + ratchet, not the source); no NTAG-session exclusion (only temp-access); rate limiter is in-memory (resets on restart, single instance only). FIRST PRODUCTION WEEK: consider `AI_RATE_ENFORCE=false` to observe real traffic before limits bite.
TESTS — `ai/AiPhase0FoundationTest` (11: one row per entry point with intent + tokens, outcomes incl. audit failure never breaking the request, temp-access 403 + WARN mode, 61st call 429 + Retry-After + other user/next minute unaffected + tenant-day cap + WARN mode, policy hardening, catalog coverage of every AI path, plan filter fail-closed API / fail-open page / FREE refuses help assist, gateway retry/usage/cap, gateway ratchet, permission catalogue sync, intent helper), `integration/AiAuditIT` (1, PostgreSQL 16: migration block twice, rows via the real filter for two churches, page + keyset + summary tenant-scoped, role/church/no-session gates, Service Admin summary, 90-day purge removes only aged rows); `AuditTableRetentionServiceTest` + ai_audit_log. Playwright aiActivity.html (stubbed APIs) 1280 + 390: 28/28. Full unit suite 3,068 / 0 failures / 3 skipped.

**2026-11-04 (latest): Birthday / anniversary reminder EMAILS are age-neutral (user rule: no age, no ordinal like "18th Birthday", no years married — "Sam's Birthday", "John & Mary's Wedding Anniversary"). Scope narrowed by the user the same day: EMAILS ONLY — AI search answers and other searches keep showing the stored year. Branch `fix/age-neutral-celebrations` (from master c8b81f4), NOT committed, NOT deployed. No schema or data change.**
WHAT — `ReminderSchedulerService` only: `buildBirthdayCelebrantBody` (dropped "What a milestone — today you turn 18th"), `buildBirthdayAudienceBody` (dropped ", celebrating their 18th birthday"), `buildAnniversaryCelebrantBody` / `buildAnniversaryAudienceBody` (dropped "your 25th wedding anniversary" — now "wedding anniversary"; the year parameter is kept but ignored); `yearsMarried` and `ordinal` removed. Subjects, push, monthly summary, calendar titles were already age-neutral. UNCHANGED BY DECISION: AI search (`AiSearchService`, `AiDataSearchService`), home.html results, records, reports. Test `reminders/AgeNeutralCelebrationTest` (3: rendered emails with a birth/wedding year on file contain no ordinal/age/years/4-digit year; source has no ordinal/yearsMarried/turn wording).

**2026-11-04 (later): Kids Portal 13+ age gate. Branch `feature/kids-portal-age-gate` (from master f50309b), committed + merged to master 2026-11-04 at the user's request (= deployed, c8b81f4). No schema or data change. Plan doc "Kids Portal 13+ Age Gate — Audit and Plan" (claude.ai, 2026-11-04); user approved with D1 (block ANY member login of a person under 13 by the record, whatever the role; a blank date on an adult role is NOT blocked), D2 (refuse at sign-in, keep the login — works again on the 13th birthday — and revoke every stored remember-me/device sign-in of that login), D3 (Family page badge).**
RULE — NEW `util/KidsPortalEligibility`: date of birth = the church's family record only (month+day+year; partial, impossible (30 Feb), out-of-range or future = missing); age on the church date (`AppClock`); 29 Feb → 13 on 1 Mar in non-leap years. Child role (`FamilyRoles.canonical` = Child) → OK | MISSING_DOB | UNDER_13; other roles → UNDER_13 only when a valid date shows under 13. Messages never echo the date; code `KIDS_PORTAL_AGE`; login-page reasons `dob` / `age` / `age-member`.
SIGN-IN — `LoginController.ageRefusal(SignUp)` (MBR logins only) checked BEFORE a session is built in `/login` (403 + `recordDenied KIDS_PORTAL_AGE`, never a failure), `/api/auth/check-remember` and `/api/auth/device-login` (`status:none` + code/message), and switch-account to a linked member login (403, current session kept); switch-role is staff-only. Every refusal → `forgetStoredSignIns` (revokeCurrent + `revokeAllFor`). A lookup error does NOT block (same fail-open rule as the subscription check, pinned by `SubscriptionExpiryLoginTest.lookupFailureFailsOpen`) — safe because `buildResponse` then sets no memberId, so no Kids Portal session exists. OPEN SESSIONS — NEW `webfilter/KidsPortalAgeFilter` (+ `KidsPortalAgeFilterConfig`, `/*`, order 3; FilterConfig constructor unchanged): member sessions only (role Member + memberId), record re-read by `findByMemberRef(session clientId)` at most every 5 min (`kidsPortalAgeCheckedUntil` on the session), static files skipped; refused → session invalidated, `/api/*` → 401 `{code:KIDS_PORTAL_AGE, reason, message}`, pages → `/?reason=<reason>`; lookup error → allowed. memberHome `patchFetchForMember` and session.js 401 handlers read the code and send the reason; login.html shows fixed texts for the three reasons (never echoed from the URL) and the check-remember message; native-bridge `biometricLogin` passes the message through.
ACCOUNT CREATION — `MemberSignupController.ageBlocked` at lookup (before any code is generated or e-mailed), send-code and verify (verify also drops the code + handle) → 403 `status:age_blocked`; memberSignup.html already shows `data.message`. `MembershipFormController.linkSignup` refuses (403). Demo: `TestDataService` Kids Portal demo child (addPortalLogin) aged 13–17; `seedKidsMinistry` makes the first two children 13–14 (they hold the seeded child logins; the rest stay 6–13) and `seedChildPortalLogins` skips anyone the rule refuses.
CHURCH RECORD IS THE AUTHORITY — B1 `PATCH /api/member/update/{id}`: a changed birthday part → 403 `BIRTH_DATE_LOCKED` when the target is child-role or under 13 by the record, or the caller is a Kids Portal session (same values sent back pass; adults can still change their own). B2 `PUT /api/families/{id}` own-family path (not `isFamilyManager`/`memberHasFamilyGrant`): `keepRecordedRoleAndBirthDate` — existing members keep stored role + birth date, a NEW child-role member is saved undated; managers unchanged. memberHome family editor: DOB + role disabled for saved people (note "can only be changed by the church office"), a new Child's DOB cleared/disabled, primary's DOB + role locked. B3 (Membership Form → Admin approval) unchanged; B4 Connect card writes only new visitor records (checked).
D3 — `FamilyService.getById` member rows + `kidsPortalStatus` (MISSING_DOB | UNDER_13 only for a live member holding an active, not-deleted login; `LoginRepository` optional setter injection, constructor unchanged); family.html badge `.kp-badge` beside the name. Help: helpCenter Kids Portal card + FAQ + HC_KB, `HelpContentCatalog.kids_portal` v1.1.
TESTS — `kids/KidsPortalEligibilityTest` (6), `kids/KidsPortalSignInTest` (7), `kids/KidsPortalAccountCreationTest` (4), `webfilter/KidsPortalAgeFilterTest` (5), `controller/KidsPortalOwnFamilyEditTest` (2), `integration/KidsPortalAgeIT` (1, PostgreSQL 16, two provisioned churches: demo child 13+ signs in, cleared year → MISSING_DOB + remembered token revoked, 12 y → UNDER_13 + family row status, other church unaffected, 13th birthday today → allowed); `mobile/AppShellGateTest` child given a 15-year-old DOB. Playwright (static pages, stubbed APIs, SW blocked) 1280 + 390: 32/32 (three login reasons, unknown reason not echoed, remembered + password refusal messages, no horizontal scroll, member editor locks, primary lock, family badges incl. hostile status value). Full unit suite 3,054 / 0 failures / 3 skipped; VisitorCheckInIT 7/7, PledgeCampaignListIT 1/1.
EXISTING ACCOUNTS — read-only audit query in the plan doc (status MISSING_DOB / INVALID_DOB / FUTURE_DOB / UNDER_13 / OK_13_PLUS); NOT run in production. No backfill or deactivation: refused logins stay and reopen at 13.
LIMIT — a parent can still ADD a new adult-role person with a typed date of birth on the own-family path (only new child-role members are saved undated).

**2026-11-04: Attendance — visitor check-in (individual / family / group), services & events picker, attendance date, visitor edit/delete, five visitor reports, Follow-ups → Visitors tab. Branch `feature/attendance-visitors`, committed + merged to master 2026-11-04 at the user's request (= deployed). Additive schema only (Hibernate adds it on start; `migrate_production.sql` block not run by hand).**
USER DECISIONS (plan approved 2026-11-04) — D1 families/groups kept in the visitor list (`visitor_party`); D2 the Service/Event list = ONLY the church's own `attendance_service_type` rows (no Events-page events, no meetings), new ones added on the Attendance page with Attendance Edit only; D3 services unique ignoring case and spaces, events blocked on same name + same date, same name on another date → shown as a suggestion ("create new" needs `confirmNew`); D5 "Follow-up required" → ONE unassigned PENDING `follow_up` linked `VISITOR → primary visitor` per family/group, a repeat visit is appended to the open task; D6 interest / follow-up / note stored per visit on `attendance_record`, latest copied to the visitor; D8 past AND future dates allowed (10 years back / 2 ahead), any day other than today (church time) saved already checked out. D4/D7 dropped.
SCHEMA — `attendance_visitor` + first_name, last_name, gender(M/F), address1, address2, city, state, zip_code, party_id, relationship (PRIMARY/SPOUSE/ADULT/CHILD/OTHER), interest_level (INTERESTED/NOT_SURE/NOT_INTERESTED), follow_up_required, note, updated_at (legacy `name`/`address` kept in sync); `attendance_record` + service_type_id, party_id, interest_level, follow_up_required, visit_note, follow_up_id; `attendance_service_type.event_date`; NEW `visitor_party` (FAMILY|GROUP, group_name, primary_visitor_id). Indexes `idx_att_visitor_client_party`, `idx_att_record_client_visitor`, `idx_follow_up_client_linked`, `idx_visitor_party_client` (DatabaseIndexInitializer + guarded block at the end of migrate_production.sql, run twice in the IT).
SERVICE — NEW `service/VisitorCheckInService` (every lookup church-scoped): `targets(filter all|services|today|future|past, q)`, `createTarget`, `register(Registration)` — match = same e-mail AND first name, else same phone (last 10 digits) AND first name; a shared family e-mail never merges two people; one registration never maps two people to one visitor; no duplicate record per person + date + service (typed derived queries — no nullable parameters); `upsertFollowUp`; `listVisitors/getVisitor/updateVisitor` (refuses details that match another live visitor, 409) / `deleteVisitor` (soft; history + tasks kept; party primary moves on); reports `attendeeReport`, `visitorListReport`, `interestReport`, `followUpReport`, `partyReport` (range ≤ 5 years, optional service/event). `AttendanceService`: all "today" = `AppClock` (was server/UTC date), `checkInMember`/`familyCheckIn` overloads with `serviceTypeId` + `date` (old signatures kept), member dedupe via `findFirstBy…FamilyMemberId…` (the old `findDuplicate` with nullable params is no longer called); `recordMap` + visitorId/serviceTypeId/interestLevel/followUpRequired.
API (`AttendanceController`, same role gate; writes need `general.attendance.edit`, visitor delete `general.attendance.delete`) — `GET|POST /api/attendance/targets`, `POST /api/attendance/visitors/register`, `GET /api/attendance/visitors[?q]`, `GET|PUT|DELETE /api/attendance/visitors/{id}`, `GET /api/attendance/reports/v2/{attendees|visitors|interest|followups|parties}?from&to&targetId`; `/check-in` and `/family-check-in` accept `serviceTypeId` + `date` (bad date → 400 BAD_DATE, other church's id → 404). Errors `{status, code, error, message, …extra}`. `FollowUpController` `GET /api/followups/visitors[?status=OPEN|PENDING|MISSED|COMPLETED]` (admins all, Users only their own, + visitor contact details).
PAGES — `attendance.html`: searchable Service/Event picker (All / Services / Today's / Future / Past Events chips, + New service or event with duplicate/suggestion handling, fixed-height list, never steals focus), Attendance date (church today; note when not today; an event's date is used when picked), Visitor method = Individual / Family / Group form (first/last required, gender, relationship for families, phone, email, split address with "same address as the first person", interest, invited by, follow-up + note), result shows matched/new; NEW 🙋 Visitors tab (search, Edit modal, Delete with in-page confirmation); Reports + Attendee List / Visitor Report / Interest / Follow-up / Families & Groups (service/event filter, Print, CSV) replacing the old Visitors report tab (endpoint kept). Fixed: report tabs used to clear the check-in method tabs (handler now scoped to `#tab-checkin`). `followups.html`: 🙋 Visitors tab (open count, status filter, search, ✔ Done, ✏️ Assign opens the existing editor; `?tab=visitors`).
TESTS — `attendance/VisitorCheckInEndpointTest` (4), `integration/VisitorCheckInIT` (7, PostgreSQL 16, two provisioned churches: target rules + tenant isolation, individual matching/no-duplicate/past date closed/latest-visit rule, family with shared e-mail + one task appended on return + Visitors tab endpoint + edit/delete, member check-in with date via service and controller, five reports + church scoping, validation, migration block twice). Playwright (static pages, stubbed APIs) 1440/1280/820/390: 220/220, run 3× (picker filters/search/new/suggestions, dates, member + family payloads, visitors list/edit/delete, all reports, Follow-ups Visitors tab, no horizontal scroll, no dialogs, hostile name rendered as text). Full suite 3,030 / 0 failures / 3 skipped.
**2026-11-04: Pledges page 500 fixed — `/api/pledges/campaigns` failed for any church with a campaign that has NO end date. Branch `fix/pledge-campaigns-open-ended`, committed + merged to master 2026-11-04 at the user's request (= deployed). No schema or data change.**
ROOT CAUSE (reproduced on PostgreSQL 16) — `IncomeRepository.sumMemberSubSourceTotal` used `(:endDate IS NULL OR i.incomeDate <= :endDate)`; with endDate null PostgreSQL answers "could not determine data type of parameter $6", so `PledgeController.collectedForPledge` → `totalsForCampaign` → `listCampaigns` threw (500) for every campaign without an end date that has a member pledge on a fund (present since the M4 window change, 2026-10-08). The page loaded funds, members and campaigns in one `Promise.all`, so the failure also left the New Campaign "Subcategory (Fund)" drop-down empty. FIX — `sumMemberSubSourceTotal` is now a `default` method choosing `sumMemberSubSourceTotalBetween` (end date given) or `sumMemberSubSourceTotalFrom` (open-ended), same pattern as `aiCampaignMemberCollected`; results identical. `pledges.html` `loadAll` loads the three lists independently (each with its own error message) so one failure never empties the others. TESTS — NEW `integration/PledgeCampaignListIT` (PostgreSQL: provisioned demo church, list 200 before/after mixing end dates, every member pledge's sum equals plain SQL for bounded + open-ended); `pledge/PledgeCollectedBoundaryTest` unchanged and green. Playwright pledges 6/6 (funds populated with and without a campaigns failure). Full suite 3,026 / 0 failures / 3 skipped.

**2026-11-02 (night): View Family involvement — who volunteers, carries follow-ups, belongs to groups. Branch `feature/viewfamily-involvement` (from master 2393651), committed + merged to master 2026-11-02 at the user's request (= deployed; Hibernate adds the nullable column on start, migrate_production.sql not run by hand) (user approval of the plan, 2026-11-02, with: maybe = tentative never counted; four volunteer sources; today+ in Central time; open follow-ups ASSIGNED TO the member; member-id links for new rows, safe fallback for old rows).**
SCHEMA (additive, no backfill) — `follow_up.assigned_member_id` (nullable INTEGER) + indexes `idx_follow_up_client_member (client_id, assigned_member_id)` and `idx_group_member_client_member (app_client_id, family_member_id)` (column `group_member.family_member_id` already existed from ETL Phase 4). Hibernate adds the column; DatabaseIndexInitializer + `migrate_production.sql` (guarded DO block, last section) create the same; block run twice + on a blank DB.
NEW LINKS — follow-ups page "Assigned To" picker sends `assignedMemberId` (hidden `#fAssignedMemberId`; typing clears it; Add-New-Volunteer and Connect "add follow-up" pass the id); `FollowUpController.resolveAssignedMemberId`: kept only for a live member of this church (`FamilyMemberRepository.findByIdAndTenant`), field absent (old callers) → keep the stored id while the name is unchanged, clear it when the name changes; blank assignee → null; `toMap` + `assignedMemberId`. Groups "Add existing member" sends `familyMemberId`; `GroupMemberService.addMember(…, familyMemberId)` (6-arg; 5-arg kept) validates the same way; typed-in people stay unlinked; edits keep the link; rows expose `familyMemberId`. Demo: `TestDataService` demo group memberships and `TrialDemoDataSeeder` Connect follow-ups carry the member id (existing demo churches unchanged → fallback). Server-created Connect follow-ups have no assignee, so `PublicEngagementService` is unchanged.
SERVICE — NEW `service/FamilyInvolvementService` (read-only JDBC, every query church-scoped): Volunteers = Active `volunteer_profile` (+ roles) · `volunteer_assignment` pending/confirmed dated today+ (event END date or event_date; undated = upcoming like the Events page; event of the same church, not deleted) · `event_volunteer` LINKED members only, pending/confirmed, event last day today+ (+ roles) · `km_volunteer` not inactive, status blank/pending/confirmed/active (+ classroom, roles) · `prayer_volunteer` active; "maybe" = tentative (listed, never counted); declined/cancelled/inactive/deleted out. Follow-ups = PENDING/MISSED, not deleted, by `assigned_member_id`, else assignee name (first+last or display "First Last (Nick)") only when it belongs to exactly ONE live member of the church → otherwise "possible match" (never counted); a row carrying another member's id never falls back. Groups = live groups, by `family_member_id`, else email used by exactly ONE live member of the church; shared email / name only → possible match. Counts = distinct people; deleted members never included. Viewers: `AiAccessPolicy` rules per section (volunteers / kidsMinistry / prayer / followUps(+All: Users only `assigned_to = username`) / groups via crossAccess); Accountant, Member Portal, church logins → nothing (server does not send it).
API (FamilyController, `familyPageGuard` first) — `GET /api/families/involvement?familyIds=…` (≤50; 400 bad/too many) → `{allowed, families:{id:{volunteers?,followUps?,groups?}}}`; `GET /api/families/{id}/involvement` → `{allowed, members:[{memberId,name,volunteer,volunteers[],followUps[],possibleFollowUps[],groups[],possibleGroups[]}]}` (404 other church). `/api/families` unchanged.
PAGE `viewFamily.html` — card row `.fam-inv` (Volunteers n · Follow-ups n · Groups n; zero/unauthorised sections omitted; one request per list load via CGP_api, Load More asks only for new ids, cache kept across filter reloads; failure = no badges); Members popup: chips under each name (dimmed when 0, "Not volunteering"), ▸ toggle (aria-expanded) opens a detail row with Volunteer Yes/No + items (kind · role · date · status, "Tentative (Maybe)"), Follow-ups (open) and Groups lists, "Possible match" tags, "None"; details load beside the member list and never block it. `.fam-card > .fam-foot { margin-top:auto }`. NOTE (pre-existing, unchanged): the page navigates twice at start-up, so the list (and now the counts) load twice.
TESTS — `family/MemberLinkRecordingTest` (4), `family/FamilyInvolvementEndpointTest` (6), `integration/FamilyInvolvementIT` (6, PostgreSQL 16: two churches, 4-member family + same-name member in another family, every include/exclude rule, renamed member linked by id, shared family email, provisioned TRIAL church fully linked + same data with ids removed → fallback ≤ linked, migration block twice).
