**2026-11-02 (latest): Access Audit + Past events paged 10 at a time; Past tab compact; event retention (5-year archive / 10-year permanent delete) built with BOTH SWITCHES OFF. Merged to master + deployed 2026-11-02 (user: "Please merge and deploy"); retention switches still OFF in production. Additive schema only.**
ACCESS AUDIT — `GET /api/private-access/audit?limit=10[&beforeTs=<ISO local>&beforeId=]`: keyset newest first (`PrivateAccessLogRepository.findNewestPage` / `findPageBefore`, ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC), rows carry `id`, `hasMore`, `summary` on the first page only; bad beforeTs → 400. Index `idx_pal_client_time (client_id, created_at DESC, id DESC)` (DatabaseIndexInitializer + migrate_production.sql). Page: 10 rows, Load More (disabled + spinner while loading, "Couldn't load — Retry", hidden at the end — `.audit-more [hidden]{display:none!important}` because shared button CSS forces display), id de-dupe, Refresh restarts.
EVENTS TABS — decided on the SERVER with the church date (`AppClock.today()`, America/Chicago); the page used the browser's UTC date, so after ~6–7 PM Central today's events showed under Past. END date = last `church_event_day` for "Multiple Days", else `event_date` (`EventListService.END_DATE`); Upcoming = no date or END >= today; Past = END < today. NEW `service/EventListService`: `upcoming` (same row shape/order as before), `past(q, type, sort newest|oldest|name, cursor, limit≤50)` — keyset cursor `yyyy-MM-dd|id` for date sorts, offset `o<n>` for name; LIKE escaped with '!'; `stats` {total, upcoming, past, multi}. `ChurchEventService.rowsFor(ids, church)` (tenant-scoped `findByIdInAndAppClientIdAndDeleteFlagFalse`, keeps order). API: `GET /api/events?view=upcoming`, `GET /api/events/past`, `GET /api/events/stats`; plain `GET /api/events` unchanged for other pages. events.html: Upcoming & Today cards byte-identical (Playwright compares the grid HTML with the old page); Past = compact rows (40 px thumb, 30 px icon buttons — 34 px on phones — with title + aria-label: Check-In, Edit, Registrants, Volunteers, Invite, Register Page, Copy registration link, Delete; same permission checks as the cards), 10 per page + Load More ("Showing x of N"), search/type/sort reload from the server (300 ms debounce, stale replies dropped), delete refreshes from the server. Edited dates move tabs on the next load (always computed from the saved date).
DELETE TRACKING — `church_event.deleted_at`, `delete_reason` (USER | RETENTION | RESTORED; existing deleted rows NULL); `ChurchEventService.delete` writes USER.
RETENTION — NEW `service/EventRetentionService`, nightly 04:10 Chicago, does NOTHING unless `EVENTS_RETENTION_ENABLED=true` (archive) / `EVENTS_RETENTION_HARD_DELETE_ENABLED=true` (permanent delete); `EVENTS_RETENTION_ARCHIVE_YEARS`=5, `_DELETE_YEARS`=10, `_MAX_PER_RUN`=200, `_CRON`. Rules: by the event's LAST day, cutoff = today.minusYears(n) (calendar years; 29 Feb → 28 Feb), strictly before the cutoff; undated events never touched. Archive = one UPDATE of live, non-RESTORED rows → delete_flag + RETENTION (a person's delete keeps USER). Permanent delete = every event past 10 years whether archived or not, oldest first, one transaction per event, re-checked under FOR UPDATE, children first: volunteer_attendance, volunteer_assignment, event_volunteer_role, event_volunteer, event_registration_reminder_log, event_registration_reminder_contact, event_reminder, import_link (this church, entity event / event_registration only), event_registration, church_event_day, church_event_image, church_event; logs ids/church/counts only. Not touched: members, families, income, donations, attendance, audit tables. Service Admin: `GET /api/serviceadmin/events/retention` (dry run), `…/retention/csv` (formula-guarded cells), `POST /api/serviceadmin/events/{id}/restore` (RETENTION archives only → RESTORED, never re-archived; 10-year delete still applies); NO run-now endpoint; card "🗄 Event Retention" on serviceadminhome. TURN-ON ORDER (user decision needed each step): deploy → review dry run/CSV → full DB backup → archive switch → later, after review, hard-delete switch.
TESTS — `controller/EventRetentionUnitTest` (8), `integration/EventPagingRetentionIT` (10, PostgreSQL 16, four churches: tabs, multi-day end, paging all sorts without repeats, wildcard escaping, edit/delete mid-paging, tenant isolation, archive boundary/idempotence/user deletes/undated, leap day, restore, dry run, permanent delete removes exactly the listed rows and keeps a same-number person link + another church's link, second run deletes nothing; Access Audit keyset with equal timestamps + a row inserted mid-paging). Playwright (static pages, stubbed APIs) 1280 + 390 px: 77/77. Full suite 3,007 / 0 failures / 3 skipped. migrate_production.sql block run twice + on a blank DB. Fixed along the way: Past fetched page 1 twice on tab switch (a quick Load More was lost); Load More buttons stayed visible at the end (shared CSS).

**2026-11-02 (later): Income + Expense — guided Voice (🎤) and Converse (🗣). Committed + pushed to master 2026-11-02 at the user's request (= deployed). Front end only: no server, model, schema or cost change (whisper-1 + gpt-4o-mini kept, user decision).**
USER DECISIONS — auto-fill on a voice pick copies everything from the last gift EXCEPT Ref No; "Clear all" keeps the contributor, "Clear form"/"Start over" clear everything; open Fund/Method "if possible, else numbered list"; Income first, Expense later; keep the current OpenAI models.
WHAT — `voice-core.js` NEW `parseNavCommand(transcript, fieldWords, {options})`: deterministic, whole-phrase only → next / goto field / clearAll / clearForm / clearField / option number (only while a list is open); "Next Generation Fund", "Go to Missions", "Clear Creek Church" are NOT commands. `income-voice.js` guided controller (both modes): `guideUI(field)` highlights + focuses (desktop only — no focus on touch so the keyboard stays closed) + opens the Contributor drop-down, or for Fund/Method an attached numbered list `#voicePickList` (browsers open a native <select> only on a real click, so the list is the page's own); `needsAttention`/`nextAttentionIndex` (wraps round): empty → ask; `userSet` (given/picked/kept) → skip; `skipped` (said skip/next) → skip until Save Now; auto-filled Fund/Method/Note → skip; auto-filled AMOUNT → read back for confirmation; Converse still confirms a defaulted Date/Method. Converse now continues after a value even in field-focus mode ("Go to Fund" → value → next field). "No, 75" on a confirm step = change (keep word + new value). `clearContributorAndDependents` clears values still equal to the last-gift auto-fill and not given/kept by the user. Manual picks during a session count as the answer (wrapped `window.selectContributor`, trusted `change` on fFund/fMethod, list clicks). Results go through `handleResult`: in order (`voice-openai.js` opt-in `ordered:true` — other pages unchanged) and after the pending auto-fill (`waitAutoFill`, 3 s cap), so a late auto-fill never overwrites a spoken value. `income.html`: `selectContributor(id, name, skipAutoFill, opts)`; `autoFillFromLastIncome(memberId, opts)` returns what it filled, skips Ref No for `opts.voice`, ignores a reply when the contributor changed meanwhile; exposes `window.__incomeAutoFillPending` / `__incomeAutoFill`. Manual (non-voice) auto-fill still copies Ref No. Guide + Help modal text updated; `window.__incomeVoice.feed/guidedField/pickList` test hooks.
EXPENSE (same day, user: "do the same for expense") — `expense-voice.js` got the same controller with Expense category in the Contributor's place: "Clear all" keeps the expense category, "Clear expense" also clears the values copied from the last expense in that category and not given/kept by the user; `expense.html` `selectExpense(id, name, skipAutoFill, opts)` + `autoFillFromLastExpense(purposeId, opts)` (returns what it filled, no Ref No for `opts.voice`, ignores a reply when the category changed meanwhile; `window.__expenseAutoFillPending` / `__expenseAutoFill`); `window.__expenseVoice.feed/guidedField/pickList`.
TESTS — `voice/IncomeVoiceNavigationTest` (7, incl. Expense wiring). Playwright on the real pages (static files, stubbed APIs, fake silent mic, stubbed TTS), desktop 1280 + phone 390, both modes: Income 118/118 (guidance, auto-fill skip + amount read-back, no Ref No copy, next/go to/number picks, clear contributor/all/form, manual clicks, auto-fill race, no horizontal scroll), Expense 46/46. Full suite 2,999 run / 0 failures / 3 skipped (after a whitespace fix the Expense patch needed for ExpenseVoiceFieldDisciplineTest).

**2026-11-02: Song Book Access (/admin/songbook-access) — Members and Activity Log load 10 at a time with Load More; server-side paging. Committed + pushed to master 2026-11-02 (= deployed). No schema change, no new table, no index.**
WHY — `GET /api/songbook/admin/members` called `FamilyService.getAllMembers` (every member of the church with its family, all fields) plus `levelsForChurch` (every access grant) on each page load, and the page filtered/rendered the whole list in the browser; the Activity Log fetched 150 rows every time the tab was opened. API — `GET /api/songbook/admin/members?offset=0&limit=10&q=` (limit 1..100, default 10; q server-side, case-insensitive on "first last", nickname and email, LIKE wildcards escaped with '!'): body = JSON array (same row keys as before), headers `X-Has-More` and, on offset 0 only, `X-Total-Count` (count query skipped when the first page is not full). Query `FamilyMemberRepository.findSongBookAccessPage` / `countSongBookAccessMembers` (JPQL, same active rules as `findAllWithFamilyByAppUser`, tenant REQUIRED — no `:appClientId IS NULL` branch, no family fetch, ORDER BY last, first, id; `OffsetWindow` limit+1 → `PageSlice`); grants only for the page's ids (`SongBookAccessRepository.findByClientIdAndMemberIdIn`). Logic in `SongBookAccessService.memberPage/memberCount/likePattern` (constructor now also takes `FamilyMemberRepository`). `GET /api/songbook/admin/audit?limit=10[&beforeTs=<ISO instant>&beforeId=]` — KEYSET paging newest first (`SongAuditLogRepository.findNewestPage` / `findPageBefore`, ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC) so entries logged while the admin reads (e.g. an access change) never shift a page; rows now include `id`; `X-Has-More`; bad beforeTs → 400; default limit 100 kept for old callers (`SongBookService.auditPage`, `recentAudit` unchanged). Both endpoints: same admin check as before (`isAdminLike` or church login) + 401 when the session has no church (previously an empty id reached `getAllMembers`, whose query has an `IS NULL` all-tenants branch). `FamilyService` dependency removed from `SongBookAdminController`. PAGE `static/songBookAccess.html` (markup/styles unchanged apart from the Load More button, status line and a ≤640 px rule): first 10 members on load; Load More appends the next 10 in place (`insertAdjacentHTML`, scroll and tab kept), disabled with spinner + "Loading…" while in flight (repeat clicks ignored), "Couldn't load — Retry" on failure (rows kept; a failed first page/search retries from the start), hidden when no more; "Showing x of N members"; search debounced 300 ms → server, stale responses discarded (sequence number); an access change re-renders only that row and marks the activity log stale. Activity Log fetched only when the tab is first opened (or after an access change), 10 newest, Load More with the last row's (createdAt, id). NOT DONE (would need approval — schema): index on `song_audit_log (client_id, created_at DESC, id DESC)`. TESTS — `songbook/SongBookAccessPagingTest` (9, Mockito), `integration/SongBookAccessPagingIT` (4, PostgreSQL 16, two churches: 24 active members in 3 batches, no repeats, order, deleted/inactive/family-flag exclusion, search + wildcard escaping, tenant isolation, keyset with 6 equal timestamps and an entry inserted mid-paging). Playwright on the real page with stubbed APIs, 1280 + 390 px: 66/66.

**2026-10-31: AI Search Phase 1 — cross-referenced questions. Branch feature1, committed, NOT pushed, NOT deployed. No schema change, no new tables, no LLM calls.** NEW `service/AiCrossReferenceService` (deterministic, runs inside `AiDataSearchService.answer` right after `AiQueryLexicon.canonical`, before the single-module router) + `repository/AiCrossReferenceRepository` (native SQL, every query tenant-filtered on each joined table, LIMIT max-rows+1, COUNT(*) OVER()). Five questions: regular attenders not in a group; regular attenders not serving (no non-declined volunteer assignment in the last 6 months); lapsed attenders (regular mode, or a reference window such as Easter/Christmas/"since <month>"/"in the last N weeks"); givers who gave last year/quarter/month but not this one; Connect Card visitors with no follow-up or an open one (⚠ at ≥14 days). PERMISSIONS: `AiAccessPolicy.crossAccess` (attendance = Admin/User + general.attendance + plan feature attendance; groups = admins + admin.groups + feature groups; Members, Kids portal and church sessions get nothing) plus the existing AiAccess for income/volunteers/follow-ups; a combined question is refused WHOLE (intent `crossref.denied`, lists every missing module) — never partial. Follow-ups: non-admins see only tasks assigned to them. GROUP MATCH: confirmed = group_member.family_member_id, or exact case-insensitive email that belongs to exactly ONE live member of the church (shared family emails never confirm); same first+last name with no link is shown separately as "Possible match (unverified)" and never counts as membership. DEFINITIONS shown in every answer ("How this was worked out" + "Read from:" in ai-assistant.js/.html). Thresholds configurable: `ai.crossref.regular-min-days`=3, `regular-window-weeks`=12, `lapsed-weeks`=4, `followup-open-days`=14, `serving-window-months`=6 (commented in application.properties). DEVIATIONS from plan: no follow-up column on member questions (member follow-ups store only linkedLabel text, no member id); event registrations never used; attendance-entered visitors not in the visitor question (no follow-up link). TESTS `ai/AiCrossReferenceServiceTest` (20), `integration/AiCrossReferenceIT` (7, two churches + cross-tenant leak rows). Full suite 2,966: all pass except pre-existing `SubscriptionRequestFlowTest.customPriceMonthly`, which fails on the 31st of a month (`ChronoUnit.MONTHS.between(Oct 31, Nov 30)` = 0 → unit DAYS in `ServiceClientService` ~389) — not fixed, needs approval.

**2026-10-29 (later 4): Security audit — Member Portal logins vs staff pages/APIs. Dev/test only, NOT committed, NOT deployed. No schema change.** ROOT CAUSE: (a) a member whose permissions were NEVER saved had every key (memberPrivileges null = all allowed) while saved members get the Permissions screen defaults (member.* + favorites only); (b) `requireAdminOrUser`/`requireStaffOrMember`/`requireAuth` admit members by design (page routes), and many APIs have no permission check. FIXES: `RoleGuard.requirePermission`/`requireMemberPermission` treat a never-saved member as the saved defaults (`isMemberDefaultKey`: member, member.*, favorites) — staff keys off; staff with no saved map unchanged. NEW `webfilter/MemberApiScopeFilter` (/api/*, order 2, after AuthFilter): for role Member + memberId only — public paths (`AuthFilter.isPublicPath`), the portal's own areas (`MEMBER_AREAS`/`MEMBER_EXACT`/member-respond pattern) and staff areas of keys the church EXPLICITLY granted (`GRANT_AREAS`, derived from each staff page's API calls; `RoleGuard.memberHasExplicitGrant`) pass; else 403 `MEMBER_SCOPE`. RULE: a new Member Portal API call must be inside MEMBER_AREAS (ratchet `MemberApiScopeTest.everyApiCallInTheMemberPagesIsAllowed` scans the member pages). Guess It host controls (`requireAdmin` in GuessItController/GuessItGroupController: create/run/delete games, correct answers) refuse Member Portal and church sessions; guessIt.html hides the host panel for members. Member Directory + `/api/member/send-email` honour `member.directory` (directoryDeny; ratchet ceiling MembershipFormController 5→6). `/publicPrayerAdmin`, `/meetingReminders` → `requireStaffAdminOrUser`; `/notes` refuses members. memberHome defines the missing `window._mbrDefaultPrivs` (saved defaults) so a never-saved member's menu hides staff sections. VISIBLE: never-saved members now see what saved members see (no staff sections, no Help Center / Guess It tab unless ticked). Tests `webfilter/MemberApiScopeTest` (13). Full suite 2,945 / 0 failures. Worship: any member with the Worship page could already read schedules; writes need general.worshipplanning (explicit grant).

**2026-10-29 (later 3): Security fix — Member Portal logins could read other families / the Kids Ministry children list. Dev/test only, NOT committed, NOT deployed. No schema change.** KIDS: new `RoleGuard.requireStaffAdminOrUser` (SuperAdmin/Admin/User; never a Member or Kids-portal session; temp/NTAG sessions unaffected) replaces `requireAdminOrUser` on all 17 checks in `KidsMinistryController` (`requireAdminOrUser` itself unchanged — it admits members ON PURPOSE for page routes; never use it alone on a data API). FAMILIES (`FamilyController`): before, `GET /api/families` and `GET /api/members` had NO check, every change endpoint checked only a permission key (a member's map lacks staff keys → allowed) and `familyPageGuard` let any member with never-saved permissions through. Now: family managers = SuperAdmin/Admin/Accountant staff + Member Portal logins with an EXPLICIT Family grant (`memberHasFamilyGrant`: `admin.family` or `admin.family.*` true; never-saved = no grant) keep the permission-key checks (`changeGuard`); everyone else may only read/update their OWN family and remove a person from it (`isOwnFamily` via MemberSessionResolver — members and staff logins linked to a member; honours `member.family:false`); create/delete/inactive/restore/bulk are managers only; the family list is refused to members without the grant; `/api/members` returns names only (id, names, nickname, displayName, familyId/Name, role; no contact data, no deleted/inactive) to them. `/viewfamily`, `/family`, `/familySearchReport` member branch = explicit grant. VISIBLE CHANGES: staff User logins can no longer edit other families via the API (the page never allowed it); members whose permissions were never saved lose the staff Families pages. Test `security/MemberDataAccessTest` (16; 10 fail on the old code). Full suite 2,933 / 0 failures.

**2026-10-29: Error-handling Mediums M1–M14 (M13 = decision only). Committed + pushed to master 2026-10-29 (= deployed). No schema change. Report: `Claude outputs/ERROR_HANDLING_FIXES_MEDIUMS_REPORT.md`.**
BACKEND RULES — (1) a "notified/sent" flag or count reflects only a channel that reported success: volunteer assignment email via `sendOrgEmailOrThrow`, row flagged only when email or push worked, `failed[] {name, reason}`; SMS batches (Kids Ministry, event volunteers) go through `util/SmsOutcomes.Batch` (`sent` = accepted, `failed[] {to, reason, code}`, 502 when none went out for a provider reason; recipient faults 21211/21408/21610/21614/30003–30006/no consent are not 502); event-volunteer SMS with Twilio not configured → 409. (2) Side effects that can fail run in their own transaction: `PledgeController.applyIncomeToPledge`/`adjustPledgeByDelta` are REQUIRES_NEW and `IncomeService` WARNs with the income id; `PlaidReviewService.bulk` commits each row via the self proxy (`bulkOne`, REQUIRES_NEW) and reports `skipped[] {id, reason}` (authored text only). (3) Mail after commit: `util/AfterCommit.run(what, work)` (Sunday School `submitExam`); `adminSendResult`/`teacherSendResult` are no longer transactional. (4) Gates: an unreadable member-privilege map refuses (403); `PrivatePageFilter` fails CLOSED for gated `/api/` paths on an evaluation error (403 `NETWORK_CHECK_FAILED` + ref), pages stay fail-open; people/portal-limit checks and the subscription/account-status/demo-agreement filters stay fail-open by design but WARN with tenant/ref. (5) NTAG wrong PIN / code → 401 (LoginThrottleFilter counts it). (6) Check-scan vision quota charged only when the model returned fields; otherwise `visionError`. (7) SMS opt-in: invalid number → 400; any other failed confirmation → `smsSent:false` + `SmsOptInController.NO_TEXT_MSG` (same text as the already-confirmed branch — no subscriber oracle); `smsOptIn.html` shows "Sign-up recorded". (8) M13 DECISION (user, 2026-10-28): `PublicFormGuard` reCAPTCHA stays fail-open on Google outage / 5xx / timeout — deliberate.
PLAID (M8, reduced scope by user decision: no update-mode link token / Reconnect) — `PlaidSyncService.syncWithResult` returns `SyncResult(outcome OK|RETRY_LATER|LOGIN_REQUIRED|ERROR, staged, errorCode, message)`; `sync()`/`syncByItemId()` keep their int signatures. `TRANSACTIONS_SYNC_MUTATION_DURING_PAGINATION` restarts pagination once from the saved cursor; `RATE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED` gets one bounded 2 s pause + retry; still failing, plus `INSTITUTION_*`, `INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR`, `PLANNED_MAINTENANCE`, Plaid 5xx and timeouts = RETRY_LATER (status/cursor untouched, `error_code` recorded, audit `SYNC_DEFERRED`). `describe(code)` = human sentence, mirrored by plaidReview.html `CODE_LABEL`. `exchangePublicToken` never throws after the item is saved (`connected:true`, `firstSync ok|failed`, `errorCode`, `message`) — so nobody links the same bank twice. `POST /api/plaid/items/{id}/sync` → 200 OK / 502 transient (`retryable:true`) / 409 connection fault.
FRONT END RULE — pages talk to `/api` through `static/cgp-api.js` (first tag in `<head>` of migrated pages; session.js injects it elsewhere): `CGP_api` rejects non-2xx/network/unreadable bodies with `{status, message, ref, code, body}` (message = server `error || message`, ` (ref x)` appended on 5xx; JSON parsed only when the server says JSON; goes through `window.fetch` so the H2 401/403 interceptors still apply); `CGP_apiJson`, `CGP_errorMessage(e, fallback)`, `CGP_showError` (page toast or built-in, never a dialog), `CGP_sendOutcome` formats `{sent, failed[]}`. Deletes and status changes apply on the page only after the server agrees (refresh from server, no optimistic splice). Migrated: volunteers, kidsMinistry, sundaySchool, followups, pledges, attendance, temporaryAccess, home, meeting, viewFamily, family, event, event-volunteers, income, expense, memberHome, serviceadminhome. memberHome `showToastMH` is now actually visible (page had no `#toast`). `sw.js` `cgp-v52`: cache-first only for images/fonts; JSON (e.g. `/public/churchregistration/*`) never cached. Ratchets `errors/PageFetchRatchetTest` (migrated pages 0 unchecked parses; memberHome 2 = Guess It, reads non-2xx game state by design; other pages ≤57, was 179; sw.js) and `errors/CgpApiHelperTest`. `ConnectAdminRetiredTest` pins the literal `fetch('/api/connect-admin')`, so that one call keeps an explicit status check.
TESTS — `errors/MediumBackendTest` (14), `plaid/service/PlaidBulkPerRowTest` (4), `PlaidSyncOutcomeTest` (8), `PlaidExchangeReportingTest` (8), `errors/PageFetchRatchetTest` (7), `errors/CgpApiHelperTest` (5); `security/PublicSendLimitsTest` stubs the outcome variant. Full suite 2,903 / 0 failures / 3 skipped. Playwright 49/49 (stubbed APIs on the real pages).
NOT DONE — memberWorship `generateAutoAssignments` (~2015) swallows a failed DELETE then saves new rows (possible duplicates): inside the Automatic Assignment region pinned by SHA-256 in `ManualAssignmentPageTest`, left for explicit approval. 57 unchecked parses on pages outside the audit list (capped by the ratchet). Error-JSON unification (L4), `RoleGuard` denial codes (L3), other Lows.

**2026-10-28 (later 3): Error-handling audit (read-only) + the six Highs fixed. Committed + pushed to master 2026-10-28 (= deployed). No schema change. Audit: `Claude outputs/ERROR_HANDLING_AUDIT_2026-10-28.md`; changes: `Claude outputs/ERROR_HANDLING_FIXES_HIGHS_REPORT.md`.**
RULES NOW ENFORCED — (1) a 500 body never carries `getMessage()`: use `util/ErrorResponses.internal(log, "what", ex)` (logs under an 8-hex ref, returns `{error, ref}`) or `ErrorResponses.message(…)` for map bodies — pinned by `architecture/NoExceptionTextIn500Test`. (2) `GlobalExceptionHandler` maps `IllegalArgumentException` → 400 (404 when the message says "not found"/"does not exist"; message passed only when `safeMessage` finds it authored), `NoSuchElementException`/`EntityNotFoundException` → 404, `DataIntegrityViolationException` → 409 + ref, malformed JSON / type mismatch / missing param / NumberFormat / DateTimeParse → 400; `IllegalStateException` deliberately stays 500. The advice is package-scoped, so a request with no matching handler (405) gets Spring's own empty body. 413 text quotes `spring.servlet.multipart.max-file-size`. (3) A mail the user is waiting for (OTP, reset link, verification code) is sent with `EmailService.sendAccountEmailOrThrow`; on failure remove the stored code and answer 502 — never "sent". `sendAccountEmail`/`sendGenericEmail`/`sendOrgEmail` swallow failures by design (side-effect mail only). (4) `ChurchStripeGateway` error maps carry `type`, `code`, `status`, `transient`; controllers answer 502 for `isTransient` (page retries), 400 otherwise; `card_error` shows Stripe's sentence, everything else `SETUP_MSG`; `testConnection(secretKey)` + owner-only `POST /api/stripe-settings/test` + "Test Connection" on stripeIntegration.html. Church Stripe and Plaid clients have 10 s connect / 30 s read timeouts. (5) `WebPushService` reads the `HttpResponse` from `web-push` `send()` — 404/410 deactivate the row. (6) `session.js` `logout(reason)` → `/?reason=expired|idle&next=<path>`; `login.html` shows the reason and returns a staff sign-in to the remembered same-origin path (`sessionStorage cgpNextAfterLogin`); `memberHome.html` has its own `patchFetchForMember` (401 → login with reason; 403 codes SUBSCRIPTION_EXPIRED/DEMO_ACCESS_ENDED/TRIAL_STATUS explained in place) — the old "401 → pending approval" branch is gone. NTAG OTP is account mail and a send failure throws `NtagLoginException` without advancing to AWAIT_OTP.
TESTS — new `errors/ClientErrorMappingTest` (10), `errors/IntegrationFailureTest` (7), `architecture/NoExceptionTextIn500Test` (1); `BankSyncTrialGateTest`, `PublicSendLimitsTest`, `LowSweepTest` verify the throwing variant; `PublicSurfaceFollowupTest.tokensAreBoundToTheProcessKey` tamper made deterministic (was a no-op ~1 in 4,096 runs). Playwright 9/9 on memberHome/home/login expiry flows. Full suite: see the report.
NOT DONE (audit §4 steps 5–9): shared `CGP_api` fetch helper + ~290 unchecked fetches / ~50 optimistic mutations; `TRIAL_STATUS`/`DEMO_AGREEMENT_REQUIRED` in the staff `session.js` switch; volunteer "notified" flag; income↔pledge hook rollback; fail-open privilege/limit/PrivatePage gates; Plaid bulk per-row tx, transient codes, update-mode link token; vision quota on failure; NTAG wrong-PIN 401 for the throttle; SW cache-first scope; reCAPTCHA fail-open policy; error-JSON unification (`{status,message}` vs `{error,code}` vs `{error,ref}`); limiter not released when a mail fails.

**2026-10-28 (later 2): Data-integrity / duplicate-record audit (read-only) + phase 1 fixes (H1–H5, M15). Committed + pushed to master 2026-10-28 (= deployed). Flyway V23 (additive, guarded). Audit: `Claude outputs/DATA_INTEGRITY_AUDIT_2026-10-28.md`; changes: `Claude outputs/DATA_INTEGRITY_FIXES_PHASE1_REPORT.md`.**
AUDIT — 5 High / 15 Medium / 12 Low. Protected already: donation posting, member giving, platform billing, Plaid staging/approve, bank/ETL imports, `reminder_sent_log` claim-before-send jobs. Gaps: no unique key on `family`, `event_registration`(email), `km_checkin`, `attendance_record`, `event_volunteer`, `group_member`, `km_child`, `volunteer_profile`, `payroll_run`, `pledge_member`; only 7/127 controllers transactional; **no scheduler lock (ShedLock) — do not scale the Web App to 2 instances before adding one**.
FIXED (phase 1) — H1 `KmPickupAlertService` never `save()`s the listed check-in (the detached merge nulled `pickup_token` → every pickup link in an alert was dead); it claims via `KmCheckinRepository.claimOverdueAlert` (conditional UPDATE of `last_alert_sent_at`, BEFORE sending) and mints the token with `setPickupTokenIfAbsent`. H2 `MemberAccountDeletionService` suffixes `signup.client_id` (`MBR…#deleted-<id>`); `LoginRepository.findByClientId` is explicit JPQL excluding deleted rows; V23 partial unique index `ux_signup_live_client_id` (skipped with a NOTICE when live duplicates exist — check the first prod start log). H3 `donation.html` retries `/save` (0/1.5/4/8 s, 5xx + network), remembers the PaymentIntent in sessionStorage until recorded, records `?payment_intent=…&redirect_status=` returns on load, shows "Payment received" + `pi_` reference instead of "Thank you" when recording fails; `DonationController.saveDonation` catches the `uq_donation_intent` race ("already recorded"), falls back to Stripe `billing_details` for the donor when the form is gone, returns `amount`. AUDIT (H1–H3, M1–M7, L1–L9) — top: H1 staff/Service-Admin sessions never expired by default (`NoAutoLogoutFilter` → -1 when the `_noAutoLogout` key was absent/unparsable; Service Admin stores no privileges) + 30-day `JSESSIONID`; H2 `X-Forwarded-For` taken left-most in `ClientIpResolver`, `NetworkMatcher` (Private Page Access gate — `X-Forwarded-For: 127.0.0.1` bypassed it; no `:port` stripping so Azure's `ip:port` never matched a CIDR) and `PublicFormGuard` (always trusted, keyed per ephemeral port on Azure) — STILL ON HOLD, needs approval + a look at the raw Azure header; H3 `PAYROLL_SSN_ENC_KEY` unset in prod (derived constant key, WARN only) — rollout still unapproved. Deferred items unchanged: AES/ECB→GCM, CSP nonces/`unsafe-eval`, plaintext-password fallback, Key Vault, `ddl-auto=validate`, HSTS includeSubDomains (`www` NXDOMAIN), the 22 raw-`getMessage()` 500 bodies (M3).
STEP 2 IMPLEMENTED (user approved 2026-10-28; default = auto-logout, opt-in to stay) — `NoAutoLogoutFilter`: staff default is now 30-min idle unless privileges JSON has `_noAutoLogout:true` (missing/empty/unparsable/absent key → 1800); `ServiceAdmin` role → fixed `SERVICE_ADMIN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` = 3600, no opt-out; same default flipped in `session.js` (idle timer runs unless the key is explicitly true) and `viewUsers.html` (checkbox unchecked unless saved true; saves false when unticked). VISIBLE CHANGE: staff whose permissions never saved the key now time out after 30 min idle until an admin ticks "Don't log out automatically" in /viewusers. `KidsMinistryController.generateSecurityCode` → static `SecureRandom`. `GlobalExceptionHandler` → `@RestControllerAdvice(basePackages="com.churchgeniuspro")` (actuator errors keep their own status/body). `CspFilter` + `base-uri 'self'; form-action 'self'`. `CsrfOriginFilter`: `Origin: null` is refused outright (no Referer fallback); absent Origin still falls back to Referer, neither header = non-browser client (webhooks) — extracted `refuse()`. `application.properties`: stale commented Titan SMTP username/password lines deleted; `mail.smtp.connectiontimeout=5000` / `timeout=10000` / `writetimeout=10000`; `management.health.mail.enabled=false`. `application-trial.properties` removed from the git index (`git rm --cached`; file stays on disk, already .gitignored and jar-excluded).

**2026-10-28: Logging / sensitive-data audit + fixes (items 1–7 + ratchet). Committed + pushed to master 2026-10-28 (= deployed). No schema or data change; log output only. Audit: `Claude outputs/LOGGING_SENSITIVE_DATA_AUDIT.md`; what changed: `Claude outputs/LOGGING_FIXES_IMPLEMENTATION_REPORT.md`.**
RULES NOW ENFORCED — a log statement never receives a raw address, phone number, provider response body or mail/SMS throwable: wrap with `util/EmailMask.mask` (+ new `maskAll`), new `util/PhoneMask.mask` (`***-***-0100`), new `util/LogSafe.exception(t)` (class + masked, capped message) / `LogSafe.text(s)`. Pinned by new `architecture/SensitiveLogArgumentsTest` (scans every `log.*(…)` in src/main/java; string literals ignored; raise with a comment only). `logging/SensitiveDataMasker` (every appender incl. console/stack traces) now also redacts PostgreSQL `Failing row contains (…)` / `Key (…)=(…)` values, URL params `t/k/code/otp/cid`, Plaid `link-`/`public-` tokens and E.164 numbers. `GlobalExceptionHandler` logs the URI only (`?…` marks a query string). `CheckExtractor`/`VisionCheckService` never log OCR text or detected check fields (presence/lengths at DEBUG). `EmailService` logs no subjects (length only). `LoginProtectionService.mask()` (now public) really masks the typed username on AUTH_FAILURE/DENIED/BLOCKED/RESET audit lines and the Login/ServiceAdmin failure lines (`jo***@exa***.com` / `ab***`); SUCCESS lines and `login_attempt_log` rows unchanged (full username); the duplicate `[LOGIN] …` line in LoginController removed. `PlaidClient.post` `onStatus` → exception message `Plaid /path HTTP n error_type= error_code= request_id=` (body kept on the exception for PlaidSyncService). `@ToString(exclude=…)` on SignUp/ServiceAdmin/StripeSettings/WhatsAppSettings/PasswordResetToken/ServiceClient/AppUser/NtagCredential/TemporaryAccess/SignupBO/PlaidTransactionStaging/PlaidWebhookEvent/PayrollEmployee. `WebPushService.generateVapidKeys()` returns the pair instead of logging it. Base `logging.level.com.churchgeniuspro=INFO` (DEBUG only in application-local.properties). Member-contribution line logs donation/intent ids, not member+amount; Guess It join codes, AI question text and raw request bodies no longer logged; WhatsApp/Twilio and OpenAI response bodies go through LogSafe.

Full suite (cloud, JDK 25) 2,830 run / 3 skipped / only the 2 CRLF-artifact `AiSearchUiPolishTest` failures of the untouched LF file.

**2026-10-26 (later): AI Search status lists + Switch Account / session-invalidation fix. Dev/test only, NOT deployed, NOT committed. No schema change.**
AI SEARCH — "show/list/find deleted members", "deleted families", "inactive families", "inactive members", "list all unalert" (= members with the Family form's per-member Disable Alerts ticked; families have no alert flag, and viewFamily's "Unalert" toggle only aliases Show Inactive). `AiStructuredAnswerService.peopleByStatus` runs first in `route` (how-to wording → null; ambiguous → clarify), handlers `deletedMembers/inactiveMembers/unalertMembers/deletedFamilies/inactiveFamilies`. Status filter in SQL: 10 new native, tenant-scoped, LIMIT-bounded `FamilyMemberRepository.ai*` queries (names/role/status only; member counts as deleted/inactive when the member OR its family is). Access = `AiAccess.members()` (the /viewfamily rule: SuperAdmin/Admin/Accountant + admin.family, via AiAccessPolicy) AND not Member portal AND not church login; plan gate unchanged (`aiSearch` feature on /api/ai-search*). `ai-assistant.js`: data gate knows families/households/unalert; status words count as "specific" so the data search runs before navigation. Tests `ai/AiPeopleByStatusTest` (7); SQL run on PostgreSQL 16 with two tenants (correct rows, no cross-tenant).
SWITCH ACCOUNT — ROOT CAUSE (reproduced on the real app + PostgreSQL with the user's account shapes): Spring Session JDBC's default delete is `DELETE … WHERE SESSION_ID = ? AND MAX_INACTIVE_INTERVAL >= 0`, and `NoAutoLogoutFilter` sets -1 on every /api request for "Don't log out automatically" sessions (staff default). So `session.invalidate()` deleted nothing: `switch-account` (and `/login`, check-remember, device-login, switch-role) reloaded the OLD session and wrote the new account on top — a Member session kept the SuperAdmin's appUserId/privileges/appClientId, linked-accounts then marked SuperAdmin `isCurrent:true`, and memberHome's switcher refused to switch to it. Same no-op made Logout leave the session valid for its old cookie, and a sign-in over an existing session inherit the previous person's staff attributes. FIX: `SessionConfig` customizer `setDeleteSessionQuery("DELETE FROM %TABLE_NAME% WHERE SESSION_ID = ?")` (only explicit invalidation and expired-on-read use it; expired sessions always have interval >= 0). LoginController/AuthFilter NOT changed (an earlier speculative patch was reverted). Test `security/AccountSwitchTest` (5, real SessionRepositoryFilter + JdbcIndexedSessionRepository on H2: default query reproduces the leak, fixed query → new clean session, Logout deletes, normal sessions unchanged) + `config/SessionConfigAccess` bridge. Verified end to end on the packaged JAR. Full suite 2,795 / 0 failures / 3 skipped.
PRODUCTION FOLLOW-UP — sessions "logged out" before the fix stayed valid by old cookie and never expire (interval -1). 2026-10-26 22:26 the user ran in production (pgAdmin, committed): `DELETE FROM spring_session WHERE max_inactive_interval < 0` (535 rows counted before). The fix was NOT yet deployed then, so repeat the same delete right after deploying, then verify `SELECT count(*) FROM spring_session WHERE max_inactive_interval < 0` only covers live sign-ins.
SEPARATE LATENT ISSUE (not fixed) — `loginRepository.findByClientId(MBR…)` (AuthFilter, linked-accounts, switch-account) throws when a member has an old deleted signup row with the same client id (account deleted then re-registered).
LIMIT — a Member-portal login with admin.family explicitly granted can open /viewfamily, but AI status lists stay staff-only (decision 2026-10-21: member AI data only via member.directory).

WHY — with Show Deleted on, members of a DELETED FAMILY (family.delete_flag, member row never flagged) looked like active members: the panel only checked the member's own flag. Now `expenseCategoryIn` matches the church's own purpose ('P') or main-source category ('M') names (whole words, after `AiQueryLexicon.canonical` on both sides, longest wins, purpose before category of the same name; question words like "expenses" are never a filter) and `expensesFor` answers for that one only ("Spent on Charity for this year …: $500.00 across 1 entry", or "No expenses for X were recorded …" — never the overall total under that name). **2026-10-19 (latest, later): SMS compliance for Twilio toll-free verification — consent check, church name + STOP, branded HELP reply — ALL BEHIND OFF-BY-DEFAULT SWITCHES. Safe to deploy: with the defaults SMS behaves exactly as before. No schema change.**
SWITCHES (application.properties, all default false; set to true in Azure App Service settings only when production moves to the verified toll-free number +1 844-524-5476): `SMS_REQUIRE_OPT_IN` → `sms.require-opt-in` (SmsService consent check); `SMS_ADD_SENDER_IDENTITY` → `sms.add-sender-identity` (SmsService.withIdentityAndOptOut); `SMS_HELP_REPLY` → `sms.help-reply` (SmsOptInController HELP/INFO TwiML). User decision 2026-10-19: keep the church's existing Twilio account, credentials, Azure SMS settings and webhook unchanged until the new account is approved. Tests for the defaults: SmsConsentAndIdentityTest.defaultsChangeNothing / switchesIndependent, PublicSurfaceHardeningTest.helpIsUnchangedByDefault / helpGetsTheBrandedReplyWhenSwitchedOn. PC working tree built and fully tested 2026-10-19: 2,560 / 0 failures / 0 errors / 3 skipped. (Text below describes behaviour with the switches ON.)
WHY — Production SMS moves to the new Twilio toll-free number +1 844-524-5476 (account churchgeniuspro@gmail.com, upgraded 2026-10-19; +1 331-322-2229 released). Toll-free verification needs: texts only to consenting numbers, sender identity and opt-out in every message, HELP handling, and SMS wording in the website privacy policy / terms (website changes in ChurchGeniusPro_Web: /privacy §5 "Text Messages (SMS)", /terms §6 "SMS Messaging Program", new /sms page with a faithful non-functional copy of smsOptIn.html = the proof-of-consent URL).
WHAT — `SmsService.doSend` (the only call into Twilio): for tenant sends, `SmsOptInRepository.isOptedIn(e164, clientId)` must be true (signed up + replied YES) or the send fails with `NOT_OPTED_IN_MSG`; the opt-in confirmation request (`sendConfirmationRequest`) is exempt; switch `sms.require-opt-in` (default true). `withIdentityAndOptOut` prefixes "<name>: " (email display name, else registered church name; "ChurchGeniusPro" for tenant-less Service Admin sends) unless the body already starts with it, and appends "Reply STOP to opt out." unless the body mentions STOP — reminders / RSVP / opt-in texts are already compliant and unchanged; staff free-text (volunteers, kids ministry, attendance), pickup alerts and member-to-member texts now get both. Consequence: staff, volunteers and pickup-alert recipients must also sign up on the church's SMS page and reply YES before they receive texts; `sms.event-registration.require-opt-in=false` no longer bypasses consent (the central check applies). `SmsOptInController /webhook/sms`: HELP / INFO → TwiML "<Church>: Texts from <Church> via ChurchGeniusPro. Help: support@churchgeniuspro.com or churchgeniuspro.com/sms. Msg frequency varies. Msg & data rates may apply. Reply STOP to opt out." (Twilio's default opt-out handling answers HELP itself and does not forward it; this reply is used when Advanced Opt-Out forwards HELP.)
TESTS — `messaging/SmsConsentAndIdentityTest` (7, Twilio SDK mocked statically: refused without opt-in and Twilio never called, E.164 lookup, name + STOP added, compliant body unchanged, display name preferred, confirmation exempt, switch off, platform sends) and `controller/SmsHelpReplyTest` (2). Full suite 2,556 / 0 failures / 3 skipped (2026-10-19). Website `npm run build` OK (45 prerendered pages incl. /sms), pages rendered at 1280 and 390 px with no errors or horizontal scroll.
TODO (user) — deploy the website and the app; set prod `TWILIO_ACCOUNT_SID` / `TWILIO_AUTH_TOKEN` / `TWILIO_PHONE_NUMBER=+18445245476` for this account; set the number's "A message comes in" webhook to https://churchgeniuspro.net/webhook/sms (POST); then in the open toll-free verification form enter proof-of-consent URL https://www.churchgeniuspro.com/sms, tick Twilio's Terms of Service and submit.

**2026-10-18: Online donations — donation reference number, fee-expense identification, "Online Donations & Contributions" on the Transactions report, Quick Period date fixes. Dev/test only, NOT deployed, NOT committed. No Flyway migration, no schema change, no change to existing Income/Expense rows.**
WHY — Review of PRABHAKAR BANDARAM's $50.00 donation (#42, 2026-10-12): Income #1223 was created by the former automatic hook (`created_by = online-giving`, before the V14 review workflow went live 2026-10-14); that hook never wrote a fee Expense, and Donation Review deliberately writes nothing for a donation whose PaymentIntent is already in Income ("posted historical", approval decision 7). So the $1.75 fee Expense is MISSING (not hidden); prod query showed it is the only such donation. Fix for #42 = the user enters the $1.75 Expense by hand (their decision); no code backfill. Separately, posted fee Expenses were hard to find: default note had no donor name and the Transactions report never displayed expense notes.
WHAT — Donation number: `Donation.numberOf(id)` / `donationNumber()` / `idFromNumber()` → `DON-000042`, derived from the sequence id (unique, permanent, exists for every old and new donation; deliberately no stored column, so no migration). `DonationPostingService` now puts it in the Ref # (`ref_no`) of the new Income AND fee Expense; new placeholder `{number}`; `DEFAULT_EXPENSE_NOTE` = "Stripe fee for donation {number} from {donor} — {gross} on {date} — {donorNote}"; `withDonationIdentity` appends "(donation DON-…, from <donor>)" to any fee note (saved old template or reviewer's own text) that lacks either. Donation Review: `/api/donations` rows carry `donationNumber` and, for historical rows, `historicalExpenseId`; Accounting cell shows the DON- badge, Income/Expense links, and "no fee expense recorded" when a historical donation has none; review drawer shows "Donation reference". Transactions report (`/transactions-report`): checkbox **"Online Donations & Contributions"** → `GET /api/reports/online-donations?startDate&endDate` (same role gate as the report — SuperAdmin/Admin/Accountant; no API permission check, per the permissions-are-for-pages rule / `ApiPermissionCheckRatchetTest`). Query `DonationRepository.reportOnlineDonationsInRange` (native, read-only): donation joined to a non-deleted Income — `posted_income_id`, or (historical) PaymentIntent `import_ref` AND review_status COMPLETED — filtered by `income_date BETWEEN` start and end (inclusive); fee from the linked Expense (`posted_expense_id` or `<ref>:stripe-fee`). Columns Contributor (linked member "Last, First", else donor name), Email, Phone Number, Note, Date, Donation Amount, Fees, Deposited Amount (+ DON- number, Member Portal / "posted before review" / edited-income-amount sub-labels). `feeStatus` RECORDED / NO_FEE (posted, Stripe $0) / STRIPE_ONLY (Stripe's actual fee, no Expense) / NOT_RECORDED (fee and deposited null → "Not recorded" / "—", excluded from fee and deposited totals, counted in `feeNotRecordedCount` with a note). Excluded: pending, never-posted, failed, unreviewed historical, and deleted-income donations. Ordinary report: expense table gains a Note column; Income/Expense rows of online donations get `donationId`/`donationNumber` (by posting links or PaymentIntent; `labelDonationRows`, best effort, never changes rows or totals; `import_ref` is fetched as a trailing column and stripped before the response). Quick Period fixes: local dates instead of `toISOString()` (UTC put tomorrow in End Date after ~7 PM Central) and month-end clamping (Mar 31 − 1 month = Feb 28/29, was Mar 3); typing a date clears the Quick Period. Finder names avoid "income/expense" (`findCreditPostedTo`, `findFeePostedTo`) to keep `DonationReviewStatusKeepsLedgerTest`'s no-ledger-writes guard intact.
TESTS — `controller/OnlineDonationReportTest` (12: $50/$1.75/$48.25, missing fee never assumed, STRIPE_ONLY, NO_FEE, edited income flagged, one row per donation, inclusive dates + session tenant, bad dates 400, role gate, labels by link and PaymentIntent with totals unchanged and no import_ref exposed, report identical without labels / on label failure, DON- number format). `DonationPostingServiceTest` +3 ($50 → Income $50.00 / Expense $1.75 with Ref # DON-000042; old saved template gets number + donor; reviewer's note that already names both kept) and updated expectations (Ref #, new default note, reviewer note suffix). Full suite 2,544 run / 0 failures / 3 skipped (2026-10-18). Local E2E `webbuild/don1.mjs` 35/35 (local PostgreSQL, demo tenant, invented donors, everything removed after): posting, double post / batch re-run write nothing, #42-shaped historical row shown "Not recorded", pending/unreviewed/deleted excluded, Member Portal included, inclusive single-day and range filtering, totals, labels, UI columns and checkbox label, Quick Period YTD local, Donation Review badges, signed-out refused. Quick Period logic also checked in node for evening / Mar 31 / leap year / year-crossing cases.
LIMITS — The donation number is not stored in the database, so it cannot be searched in SQL directly (use the id: `DON-000042` = donation 42). Income/Expense rows posted before this change keep their empty Ref # and old notes (never rewritten); the report and Donation Review find them through the stored links / PaymentIntent. The Online view's PDF uses the same html2pdf path as the ordinary report (not exercised in the cloud container, which cannot reach the CDN).

**2026-10-16 (Phase 5): ETL Import — Migration Phase 5 of 5 (Wizard, history, audit). Dev/test only, NOT deployed. Flyway `V22__etl_file_data_type.sql` NOT applied in production (mirrored in `scripts/sql/migrate_production.sql`, additive, guarded with `to_regclass` → no-op on a fresh database).**
WHAT — `/etlImport` is now a six-step wizard (Start → Files → Map → Review → Load → Summary) with a progress rail and a sticky run header (church · source + preset badge · run · status · last audit action · Audit trail drawer with CSV · Close run). The page moved from `static/etl-import.html` to `src/main/resources/admin-pages/etl-import.html` and is served only by `ServiceAdminController#etlImportPage` when the session has `serviceAdminId` (otherwise 302 → `/serviceadminlogin`, no-store); `/etl-import.html` 302 → `/etlImport`. URL `?clientId=&run=` resumes a run at the right step; `?new=1` starts fresh. Start: preset cards (badge "Preset (unverified)", what to export per product = `EXPORT_HINTS`), date order, notes, AI opt-in, the church's run history with Open. Files: multi-file drop; each file is extracted, its type detected by `util/EtlTypeDetector` (headers only: AUTO matches + ½ REVIEW + 3 for the type's own id header − 4 if a required column is missing; HIGH when it leads by ≥ 2) and re-read with that type so its own id column is used; editable type per file; duplicate hash → "Import it again"; warnings for two files of one type / no type. `import_file.target_table` / `suggested_table` (V22). Map: one tab per file, required columns first, dictionaries, date panel (`GET /runs/{id}/date-preview`: each mapped DATE/DATETIME column's samples read AUTO/MDY/DMY/YMD, `ambiguous` flag) + `PUT /runs/{id}/settings` (date order / label; `ImportRunService.updateSettings` refuses once APPROVED/LOADED/terminal; audited SETTINGS). Review: existing tiles/filters/decisions/households/validation CSV + "Stage & validate all mapped files". Load: ordered checklist with per-type Approve / Load / Retry remaining / Report / Roll back, "Approve all ready types", "Load all in order" (live progress by polling `/plan`), Roll back run, Verify. Summary: `GET /runs/{id}/migration-summary` (+ `-csv`): per type staged/added/updated/skipped/failed/errors/not loaded, totals, last load / rollback; `GET /runs/{id}/audit-csv`; "Undo entire import". `import_audit.batch_id` is now filled from the event detail's `batchId`. Service Admin home: card "Data Migrations" (`GET /api/serviceadmin/etl/overview`: up to 10 in progress + last 5 completed across churches, with church, loaded counts, data types) + "New migration". Church side: `controller/DataImportHistoryController` `GET /api/data-imports` — session tenant only (no clientId parameter), Admin/SuperAdmin or church login (`requireAdminOrChurch`; member portal / other roles 403, none 401), only LOADED / ROLLED_BACK runs that have a LOAD event: date, "ChurchGeniusPro support", source label, Imported / Undone (+ date), added/updated per data type — no notes, file names, operator or row data; card "Data Imported for Your Church" on `logo.html`. Fix found by the wizard: `SourceProfileService.profile` flushes the deletes before re-inserting, so re-uploading a file into its own slot of a run no longer hits `uq_src_profile_col` (500). Operator guide: `docs/ETL-MIGRATION-GUIDE.md`.
TESTS — `etl/EtlPhase5Test` (21: page gate no session / tenant session (incl. SuperAdmin) / service admin / old URL; church history gated, own tenant only, other tenant's batch ignored, nothing internal exposed, church login; type detector on Breeze people & giving, p4 pledges / registrations / check-ins / people, CHMeetings giving, unknown headers; updateSettings ok / refused / wrong church; audit batch id; Phase 5 endpoints gated, wrong church 404, unknown 400, settings 409, migration summary + CSV, audit CSV, date preview ambiguity, overview; V22 guarded + mirrored). New sanitized fixture `src/test/resources/etl/breeze_giving_sample.csv`. Full suite 2,528 run / 0 failures / 3 skipped (2026-10-16). Local E2E `etl5.mjs` 37/37 — a Breeze migration (people + giving) end to end from the wizard alone: gate, admin-home card, preset badge, MDY, both files dropped at once and typed correctly, mapping/date/fund panels, stage all, decisions, approve all, giving waits for people, load in order (4 people + 6 gifts, donor-less gift saved with its guest name, 5 linked to donors), verify passes, summary totals 10, both CSVs, batch ids, audit drawer, resume by URL, Undo entire import back to baseline. `etl5b.mjs` 10/10 (church admin sees own import on Church Settings; church session refused `/etlImport` and the ETL API; member portal 403; Undone after rollback). `etl1–4.mjs` updated for the wizard: 25/25, 27/27, 31/31, 29/29.
LIMITS — The Files step keeps the dropped file in the browser only for the session: changing a file's type after a page reload needs the file dropped again. One file per data type per run: a second file of the same type is flagged on the Files step — combine the files or use a second run. Presets still unverified (no real export run yet).
V19/V20 FIX (2026-10-16, user decision) — resolves the open decision: a brand-new database failed at V19 (`relation "family" does not exist`; Flyway runs before Hibernate). V19/V20 now wrap every ALTER TABLE / CREATE INDEX on an existing table in `DO $$ IF to_regclass('public.<table>') IS NOT NULL` (same effect on existing databases; import_link still created outright). Checksums changed (V19 -1360049728 → -726679248, V20 833216381 → 1456191994): a database that already applied them refuses to start with "Migration checksum mismatch" until `scripts/sql/flyway_repair_v19_v20.sql` is run once (updates only rows still holding the old checksum; idempotent; touches no schema). Production: not needed unless V19/V20 were applied there. `migrate_production.sql` unchanged. Verified locally: blank database starts with V2–V22 all successful and every ETL column created by Hibernate; existing database refused before / starts after the repair script; new test `EtlPhase5Test.etlMigrationsAreFreshDatabaseSafe` (V19–V22: every statement on a table the file does not create is guarded — fails when a guard is removed); full suite 2,529 / 0 failures / 3 skipped; `etl5.mjs` 37/37. LIMIT: on a brand-new database the two lookup indexes `ix_family_source` / `ix_family_member_source` are not created (Hibernate does not make them; V19 skips them there) — performance only.
BUILD NOTE — run `./mvnw clean package`: a non-clean build leaves the old `target/classes/static/etl-import.html`, which would still be served as a static file without the gate.

**2026-10-16 (Phase 4): ETL Import — Migration Phase 4 of 5 (New data types). Dev/test only, NOT deployed. Flyway `V21__etl_record_types.sql` NOT applied in production (mirrored in `scripts/sql/migrate_production.sql`, additive only; fresh-database safe).**
WHAT — Nine new import types: pledge campaigns, pledges, events, event registrations, attendance, groups, group memberships, Kids Ministry children, Kids Ministry check-ins. Staged in ONE generic table `staging_record` (`hibernate/StagingRecord`, typed values in `values_json`, resolved references in `links_json`) instead of nine typed staging tables; family/income/expense keep their own tables and code paths unchanged. `EtlTargetSchema`: `RECORD_TABLES`, `LABELS`, `rowIdColumns(table)` (the type's own id header — used only when the admin names the data type), new column types DATETIME/TIME (`Transforms.parse_datetime` honours the run's date order, `parse_time` → HH:mm), dictionary `attendanceStatus`. `TransformStageService.stageRecords` (same transforms/dictionaries/provenance guard). `service/EtlRecordService`: validation per type (required values; references to a parent staged in this run "s<id>", an earlier run's `import_link` "l<id>", or a live record by name — unresolved/ambiguous REQUIRED parent (campaign, event, child) = ERROR, unresolved person = WARN; missing group / classroom / campaign fund = INFO "will be created"); match tiers per the approved plan table (EXACT = `import_link`, STRONG / POSSIBLE → REVIEW / IN_FILE); load (`build` per type, UPDATE/MERGE with before-images, MERGE never for pledges, money UPDATE only from EXACT/STRONG) and rollback (soft delete; registrations + check-ins — no delete flag — are hard-deleted, only the rows the import created; refuses atomically when a campaign / event / group / child the import created has records added outside it). `service/EtlLiveLookup`: tenant-scoped JPQL reads of the live tables (no new methods on feature repositories, no bare id lookups). `EtlLoadService`: `LOAD_ORDER` (12 tables: family, pledge_campaign, event, group, km_child, income, expense, pledge, event_registration, attendance, group_member, km_checkin), `DEPENDS` (load waits for parent batches; rollback waits for dependants), `waitingFor` (rows pointing at parents/people staged but not loaded), record branches in updateRow / createBatch / load / rollback / runPlan (record types appear in the plan once the run uses them). `EtlLinkService` cleans up `ref:group` / `ref:km_classroom` and keeps a fund a live campaign uses. Native conventions: event_code left blank (unique across churches), event type "One Day"; campaign start date → `created_date` (pledge collection window, audit M4); member pledges collected = 0 (computed from income), guest pledges keep the file's collected amount; registrations marked checked in after insert (`@PrePersist` resets it) at the event's start; attendance/check-ins without a check-out are closed at check-in time (method "import") so they never show as currently checked in / trigger pickup alerts; name-only attendance = VISITOR with person_name (no visitor row). `group_member.family_member_id` added (decision 6: nullable, informational, unused by the Groups feature). Other: people files marked "Families & members" now take Person Id / Individual Id / Member Id as the row id; family phone synonyms + mobile_phone / cell_phone / home_phone; MappingService leaves a record file's own id column out of mapping candidates. UI `etl-import.html`: nine new data types in both selects, server labels on plan tiles, Merge offered for all non-financial types, "linked to" chips on record rows.
TESTS — `etl/EtlPhase4Support` (harness), `EtlPhase4PledgesTest` (7), `EtlPhase4EventsTest` (6), `EtlPhase4GroupsKidsTest` (7), `EtlPhase4PipelineTest` (10); fixtures `src/test/resources/etl/p4_*.csv` (invented data). Full suite 2,507 run / 0 failures / 3 skipped (2026-10-16). Local E2E `etl4.mjs` 29/29 (all ten files in one run; dependency gating; live rows checked field by field; same files again → every row EXACT; rollback refused while a church-added registration exists, nothing changed; then full rollback to baseline incl. created fund/group/classrooms; verify passes); `etl1/2/3.mjs` 25/25, 27/27, 31/31.
LIMITS — Presets have no aliases for the new types (generic matcher + schema synonyms only; still no real exports). Attendance has no link to an event (the live table has none). Rollback's "records added outside the import" guard covers campaigns/events/groups/children, not people (unchanged from Phase 3). OPEN DECISION: V19/V20 (Phases 2–3) use un-guarded ALTER TABLE and would fail on a brand-new database; fixing them changes their checksums (local `flyway repair` needed).

**2026-10-16 (latest): ETL Import — Migration Phase 3 of 5 (Multi-table runs, persistent links, households, truthful rollback). Dev/test only, NOT deployed. Flyway `V20__etl_import_link.sql` NOT applied in production (mirrored in `scripts/sql/migrate_production.sql`, additive only).**
WHAT — One run now carries people + income + expenses: each table is staged/validated on its own (`stage`/`extract` allowed while another table is APPROVED/LOADED; the run status is no longer moved backwards), loaded in dependency order and reversed in reverse order. New table `import_link` (`hibernate/ImportLink`, `repository/ImportLinkRepository`, `service/EtlLinkService`): tenant + source system + entity (`person`/`household`/`income`/`expense`/`ref:sub_source`/`ref:main_source`/`ref:purpose`/`ref:transaction_type`) + source key → live id, with run/batch provenance; learned on INSERT/UPDATE/MERGE, used by validation (`EtlValidationService.validateMoney` people map) and the loader (`buildMemberIndex`) so a later run's "Person Id" resolves to the member an earlier run created; `EtlReferenceResolver.Session.created()` records funds/purposes/methods a load created → `recordReferenceRows`. Households (approved decision 2): `EtlValidationService.proposeHouseholds` groups people rows whose key is just their own id by normalised last name + street (`normAddress`: Street→st, Ave, Rd, N/S/E/W, apt…) + zip-or-city; stored on `staging_family.household_proposal/household_decision`; INFO-level `_household` message; `GET/PATCH /runs/{id}/households` (ACCEPT/REJECT/CLEAR; locked once the batch is approved/loaded); `EtlLoadService.householdKey` uses an ACCEPTED proposal as the family grouping key (never a source id) — undecided/rejected rows stay separate, matched live people keep their household. Loader: `loadRun` (`POST /runs/{id}/load`, APPROVED batches family→income→expense), `requireDependenciesLoaded` (income batch refused while the run's people batch is unloaded), part-way failure → batch `FAILED` + `import_batch.error_message` + audit `LOAD_FAILED` with the committed counts (chunks already committed stay reversible; retry = `loadBatch` on the FAILED batch continues the still-APPROVED rows), `rollbackBatch` accepts LOADED or FAILED, refuses a people batch while the run's gift batch is live, deletes the batch's links and soft-deletes reference rows the import created that nothing uses (kept + reported when used), and marks the RUN `ROLLED_BACK` only when no batch of it is still live (`runStillLoaded` in the audit); `rollbackRun` (`POST /runs/{id}/rollback`, @Transactional, reverse order); `runPlan` (`GET /runs/{id}/plan`: per table counts, batch state, canApprove, dependsOn, + canLoadRun/canRollbackRun). `TransformStageService.stage` refuses to re-stage a table whose batch is APPROVED/LOADED/FAILED (provenance). UI `etl-import.html` Step 4: plan tiles (switch table), households panel (Accept / Keep separate / Undo), per-row household chip, INFO messages, "Load run" / "Roll back run", FAILED batch → "Retry remaining" + "Roll back" + reason, resume opens Step 4 when anything is staged. Ratchet: `TenantScopedLookupRatchetTest` baseline +`EtlLinkService` (4, tenant-filtered).
TESTS — `etl/EtlPhase3RunTest` (12: proposals incl. suffix normalisation + decision survival; link record/index/resolve/rollback with used-vs-unused reference rows; accepted household = one family; ordered run load + dependency refusal + staged-id resolution; part-way failure/retry/rollback of a FAILED batch; truthful run status + reverse-order rollbackRun; runPlan; stage guard; donor resolution via staged id / skipped duplicate / earlier-run link), `etl/EtlPhase3ControllerTest` (4). Full suite 2,477 run / 0 failures / 3 skipped (2026-10-16). Local E2E `etl3.mjs` 31/31 (one CHMeetings run with people + giving: 2 proposals, accept one in the UI → 1 family of 3 + 2 singles + 1 solo, gift batch refused before people, re-stage refused, Load run → 6 members / 4 families / 3 gifts linked by Person Id, links incl. created fund; later giving-only run resolves Person Id; batch-level rollback leaves run LOADED, run rollback → ROLLED_BACK, fund removed, verify passes); `etl1.mjs` 25/25 and `etl2.mjs` 27/27 still pass (scripts now force re-upload of the shared fixtures because rolled-back runs keep their `import_file` rows).
LIMITS — Household proposals only group rows that will be INSERTed into new households (a staged person matched to an existing member keeps their existing household; attaching new people to an existing live household is not proposed). Proposal key needs a street address and zip/city. Reference rows created by Phase 1/2-era loads (before links) are not cleaned up retroactively. A FAILED batch's run stays at its previous status (APPROVED/LOADED), never FAILED, so it remains retryable/reversible. Presets remain unverified (no real exports). Phases 4–5 (new data types; wizard/Service Admin card/church-side history) not started.

**2026-10-16 (latest, later): ETL Import — Migration Phase 2 of 5 (Identity and duplicates). Dev/test only, NOT deployed. Flyway `V19__etl_source_identity.sql` NOT applied in production.**
IDENTITY — `Family` + `FamilyMember` gained `source_system` (preset code) + `source_id` (the product's own id; family = Family ID / household key) — V19 additive + index `(app_client_id, source_system, source_id)`, mirrored in `scripts/sql/migrate_production.sql`; written on INSERT (and learned on UPDATE/MERGE) only when the file carried real ids (`TransformStageService.hasSourceIdsForTarget` ← `import_file.id_column`). Money rows use the existing H8 `import_ref`: `TransformStageService.importRef(preset, hasIds, sourceRowId, parts…)` = `ETL:<preset>:<source id>` when the file has ids, else `ETL:<preset>:fp:<sha1 of date|amount|fund|link|guest|ref>` (ordinal-independent, case/space-insensitive) — stored on `staging_income/expense.import_ref` at stage, copied to the live row at load; "import as new" over a live duplicate → ref suffixed `:<staging id>` (`uniqueRef`) so the partial unique index accepts it.
TIERS — NEW `service/EtlMatchService` (`FamilyIndex` over `findActiveByTenantForEtl` + `MoneyIndex` over `find…DateBetween` of the staged date window; NEW repo methods `findByAppClientIdAndDeleteFlagFalseAndIncomeDateBetween/ExpenseDateBetween`). Family: EXACT = source id (same preset, only when the file has ids — row ordinals never match) · STRONG = e-mail | first+last+phone (last 10 digits) | first+last+birth date · POSSIBLE = first+last only · IN_FILE = same key earlier in the upload (points at the first staging row) · NONE. Income: EXACT = import_ref · STRONG = person(member id or guest name)+date+amount+fund · POSSIBLE = date+amount+fund, donor unresolved. Expense: EXACT = import_ref · STRONG = category+date+amount · POSSIBLE = date+amount. Staging rows: `match_tier`, `match_detail`. `EtlValidationService` (new 7-arg @Autowired ctor; 5-arg compat ctor kept): EXACT/STRONG/IN_FILE → WARN + default action SKIP (an admin's earlier decision survives re-validation); POSSIBLE → row status **REVIEW**, no default; NONE → INSERT; `ValidationSummary` gained review/newRows/exact/strong/possible/inFile. Money person resolution now also by the member's `source_id` of the same preset (a CHMeetings "Person Id" on a gift finds the member imported from the CHMeetings people file); unresolved → WARN + guest name.
DECISIONS — `EtlLoadService.updateRow` actions INSERT | UPDATE | MERGE | SKIP with guards (`decide`): MERGE people only (fills blank live fields only — `mergeFamilyFields`), UPDATE/MERGE need a matched live row, money UPDATE only from EXACT/STRONG (never POSSIBLE), decided REVIEW row → WARN, already LOADED/SKIPPED rows refused. `createBatch` refuses while any row of the table is REVIEW (`countReview`). `buildMemberIndex` + source ids. Controller: `GET /runs/{id}/summary?targetTable=` (total/valid/warn/error/review/new/exact/strong/possible/inFile/willInsert/willUpdate/willMerge/willSkip/processed/canApprove), `GET /runs/{id}/report-csv?targetTable=` (CSV, BOM, quoting, `'` guard for formula-leading cells; path deliberately not `.csv` — suffix negotiation returned 406), preview `filter=` + `review|duplicates|new|skipped`, row views + matchTier/matchDetail/dedupeAction/importRef. `etl-import.html` Step 4: status tiles + match tiles + decision tiles, review note, filter pills, per-row decision select (Skip / Update existing #n / Merge into #n / Import as new; Update hidden for POSSIBLE money), Approve disabled until `canApprove`, "Download validation report (CSV)".
TESTS — `etl/EtlPhase2DuplicatesTest` (10: tiers incl. in-file and cross-system, validation statuses/defaults/decision persistence, income link by source id, REVIEW gate, decision guards, MERGE + identity, import refs + suffix, fingerprint shape), `etl/EtlPhase2ControllerTest` (4: summary, filters, PATCH forwarding/400, CSV). Full suite 2,461 run / 0 failures / 3 skipped. Local E2E `etl2.mjs` 27/27 (demo tenant, rolled back): Breeze people → ids on live rows; same file forced again → 4 EXACT, 0 inserted; generic look-alike file → 1 STRONG (e-mail) + 1 POSSIBLE blocks approval → UI review filter → Merge → 1 merged/1 inserted/1 skipped, blank country filled, e-mail kept; giving re-import → 3 EXACT skipped, look-alike gift → POSSIBLE, UPDATE refused, "import as new" loads with a distinct ref; ledger +4 over two giving imports; rollback restores everything.
NOT IN PHASE 2 (by plan): multi-table runs, `import_link` resolver, household inference, run-level rollback, new data types, wizard/history — Phases 3–5.

**2026-10-16: ETL Import — Migration Phase 1 of 5 (Foundation). Dev/test only, NOT deployed. Plan: "ETL Import Migration Plan" (claude.ai doc, 2026-10-16); user approval 2026-10-16 with 8 decisions (phases 1→5 in order; presets stay "Preset (unverified)" until a real export passes end to end; household inference = propose + admin confirm (Phase 3); unresolved donor → guest name (Income.guestName exists) never an uncertain member; church-side read-only history (Phase 5); AI mapping opt-in per run, column names+types only; GroupMember.family_member_id only if additive-safe (Phase 4); PCO + CCB presets included in Phase 1 as CSV presets). Hard rules: no production DB/config/deploy without explicit approval; tests on local PostgreSQL + sanitized fixtures only.**
FILES — NEW `util/SourcePreset` (enum GENERIC, BREEZE, CHMEETINGS, GRACELY, PLANNING_CENTER, CCB: per-table header aliases seeded from vendors' documented import templates/grid columns, id columns (people files: Breeze ID / Person Id / Individual ID; giving files: Payment ID / Transaction Id / Donation ID — a Person Id on a gift is a LINK → familyLink, never the row id), default dateOrder (Breeze MDY), value dictionaries; `verified()` false for all), NEW `hibernate/ImportFile` + repo (run_id, client_id, source_table, file_name, size, sha256, format csv|json|xlsx|xls, row_count, id_column, uploaded_by, forced), NEW `hibernate/ImportValueMap` + repo (run, dictionary fund|method|category|gender|role|memberType, source_value → target_value), NEW `service/EtlDictionaryService` (distinct file values per dictionary column + church's existing funds/types/purposes + fixed gender/role/memberType vocab; save per run), `ImportRun` + `source_preset`, `date_order` (AUTO|MDY|DMY|YMD), `ai_assist` (@ColumnDefault false), `StagingIncome` + `guest_name`. `SourceExtractService`: one `extract(run, table, format, bytes, targetHint)` door — CSV (delimiter sniff `, ; TAB |`, BOM, UTF-8 else windows-1252, UTF-16 BOMs), JSON, XLSX/XLS via POI (first non-empty sheet, dates → ISO, whole numbers without .0, formulas evaluated), `detectFormat`, `sha256`; `source_row_id` = preset/literal row-id column value else ordinal (legacy). `EtlTargetSchema.TargetColumn` gained `required`, `dictionary`, `virtual` (family `fullName` → split first/last, `birthDate` → birthday month/day/year); income: `purposeName` REMOVED (Income has no purpose), `guestName` ADDED, amount+date required; expense: `familyLink` REMOVED (Expense has no member), `payee` kept and loaded as "Payee: X" at the head of the note; EMAIL default transform `first_of_list`. `Transforms.apply(t, raw, Context(dateOrder))` — legacy 2-arg keeps AUTO (ISO → month-first slashed → dotted = day-first); new: unambiguous day-first in AUTO, month names, ISO/US timestamps (date part), Excel serials, `€ £ ₹ USD EUR GBP`, European "1.234,56", trailing `-`/CR, `first_of_list`, `split_name_first/last` ("Last, First" or "First Last"). `MappingService.suggest`: preset pass first (alias hit → conf 1.000 AUTO, rationale "… preset column"), then `MappingMatcher.match(table, remainingSources, placedTargets)` (new overload: placed targets take no source), AI fallback ONLY when `run.aiAssist` && OpenAI enabled, prompt = column names + inferred types (NO sample values); NEW `review()` → missingRequired (fullName satisfies first/last), unmappedSources, dictionary/virtual columns, preset, ready. `TransformStageService`: Context from run.dateOrder, virtual columns, dictionaries (admin `import_value_map` wins, preset fills gaps), family-key candidates + breeze_id/person_id/individual_id. `EtlValidationService`: missing income/expense date is now ERROR ("Date is required"; live column NOT NULL — was WARN then FAILED at load). `EtlReferenceResolver` + `TransactionTypeRepository` (@Autowired 4-arg ctor; 3-arg compat ctor kept): `Session.transactionType(name)` get-or-create tenant-scoped, `transactionTypeById(id, tenant)` (no bare findById — ratchet unchanged). `EtlLoadService`: income/expense `method` → `transactionType` (was validated then DROPPED), income `guestName` when the member link is unresolved, expense payee → note; before-images + restore cover `transactionTypeId` and `guestName`. `ImportEtlController`: `GET /api/serviceadmin/etl/presets`; `POST /runs` body + sourcePreset/dateOrder/aiAssist; `POST /runs/{id}/extract` + `format`, `targetTable` hint, `force` — same SHA-256 for the same church in any non-DISCARDED run → 409 `{duplicateOf, duplicateFile}` unless force=true (same run+slot re-upload allowed); `import_file` row per upload; EXTRACT audit carries fileName/size/sha256/idColumn/forced; `GET /runs/{id}/files`; `GET /runs/{id}/mappings/review`; `GET|PUT /runs/{id}/dictionaries`; suggest response + `review`, and PROFILED → MAPPED when any AUTO rule exists (was: only on a manual confirm); mappingView + required/dictionary/virtual/targetType. `etl-import.html`: Step 1 source application select (badge Supported / Preset (unverified)), date-order select, AI checkbox; Step 2 accepts .xlsx/.xls, "Data type in this file", files table, 409 → confirm → force; Step 3 review panel (missing required blocks with a confirm, unmapped source chips), ★ required marks, dictionary chips, transform datalist, dictionary editor panels (datalist of the church's values, Save → re-stage applies). `scripts/sql/migrate_production.sql` + additive block (import_run columns, staging_income.guest_name, import_file, import_value_map) — NOT run in production.
TESTS — `etl/EtlTransformsAndPresetsTest` (13), `etl/EtlPhase1ServicesTest` (13: CSV/XLSX/XLS extraction + ids + charset, preset mapping, AI opt-in/privacy, review, staging with dictionaries/virtual columns/DMY, missing-date ERROR, loader method/guest/payee + before-image restore), `etl/EtlPhase1ControllerTest` (3: gate, presets/run fields, repeat-file guard incl. force/discarded/same-slot); fixtures `src/test/resources/etl/*.csv` (invented data). Full suite 2,447 run / 0 failures / 3 skipped (2026-10-16). **2026-10-15 (later 4): Mobile app — Phase 6 (biometric unlock + per-device sessions). Dev/test only, NOT deployed; needs the mobile repo's versionCode 2 build.**
RULE KEPT — session policy is untouched: 30-minute inactivity timeout for web and app alike, and *Don't log out automatically* (`NoAutoLogoutFilter`, `member_preference.no_auto_logout`) still means NO timeout at all. Biometrics only change how a lapsed app session is re-entered; nothing extends a session.
SERVER — `RememberMeService`: `issue()` → `issueRow()`; NEW `issueDeviceSecret(user, request, deviceName)` returns the raw secret (no cookie), `resolveSecret(raw, request)` (≤128 chars) reuses the hash → prev-hash grace path, `rotateSecret(row)` returns the new secret, `revokeSecret(raw)`, `revokeOne(signupId, rowId)`, `revokeOthers(signupId, request, currentDeviceSecret)`, `currentRowId(request, deviceSecret)`; `platformOf()` falls back to the UA marker "(android)"/"(ios)". `LoginController./login`: `rememberMe && isAppShell && body.deviceStore==true` → body `deviceToken` (FALLBACK, found on the phone 2026-10-15: `deviceStore` is sent by login.html only when the bridge found both plugins and `checkBiometry` says the device can gate a secret — `window.CGP_NATIVE.deviceStore`; a 1.0 app or an unlocked phone gets the ordinary Phase 1 remember cookie instead, otherwise Remember me did nothing there; `DeviceLoginWebTest.appWithoutSecureStoreFallsBackToCookie`) (hash-only `remember_me_token` row, platform android|ios, device_label = body `deviceName`) and the browser remember cookie is revoked/never set for the app. NEW `POST /api/auth/device-login {token}` (public at AuthFilter like the rest of `/api/auth/**`): app UA required, IP throttle via `loginProtection.check`, unknown/revoked → `{status:"none"}` + `recordFailure(..., "/api/auth/device-login")`, the same enabled/subscription/demo/kids-portal re-checks as `check-remember` (shared `autoLoginRefusal` / `establishAutoLoginSession`), success → rotated `deviceToken` in the body, audit logged. `/api/auth/logout` body `{deviceToken}` → `revokeSecret` (that device only; a remembered browser stays). NEW `controller/DeviceSessionController` `/api/auth/devices`: `GET ?deviceToken=` (live rows of the session's login, `current` flag by cookie or token), `DELETE /{id}` (own rows only, else 404), `POST /revoke-others {deviceToken}`; 401 without `session.username`. Tests `security/DeviceLoginWebTest` (7, MockMvc standalone with `InMemoryRememberMeTokens`), `NativeBridgeRatchetTest.biometricContract`, `ClientCredentialRatchetTest` logout assertions updated to the `{pushEndpoint?, deviceToken?}` body.
WEB — `native-bridge.js` section 8: secret kept ONLY in the OS secure store (`@aparajita/capacitor-secure-storage` key `cgp_device_token`, Keychain `whenUnlockedThisDeviceOnly`), prompt via `@aparajita/capacitor-biometric-auth` (`checkBiometry` → `internalAuthenticate`, device-PIN fallback allowed); `window.CGP_NATIVE.storeDeviceToken/getDeviceToken/hasDeviceToken/clearDeviceToken/biometricLogin/deviceName`; `cgp:native-biometric-login` → `cgp:native-biometric-result`; `status:"none"` from device-login clears the stored token. `login.html`: `applyLoginData` awaits `storeDeviceToken` (4 s cap); after check-remember "none" in the shell, waits for `cgp:native-ready`, and if a token exists shows `#biometricBtn` → `doBiometricLogin()`; `/login` body carries `deviceName`. NEW `static/devices.js` (`window.CGP_DEVICES.render(el)/openModal()`) loaded by `shell.js` and `memberHome.html`: staff → *Signed-in devices* footer in the notification bell (session.js `#cgpNotifDevices`), members → `#devicesCard` in Settings (hidden for child/temp sessions). LOGOUT KEEPS THE DEVICE CREDENTIAL (user decision 2026-10-15, banking-app style): no logout path sends or clears `deviceToken` (session.js `CGP_deviceToken()` is read-only now; `/api/auth/logout {deviceToken}` still works server-side but nothing calls it); after Logout login.html shows `#biometricBtn` (not auto-prompted — `waitForBridge()` + `hasDeviceToken()`); forgetting the phone = *Signed-in devices → Sign out* or a login with Remember unticked. Ratchets pin `doesNotContain("body.deviceToken")` on all three pages. Browser behaviour pinned unchanged by the ratchets.
