**2026-10-15 (later 3): Mobile app — Phase 5 (native push: FCM for Android, APNs for iOS). Dev/test only, NOT deployed.**
ONE TABLE, TWO TRANSPORTS — `push_subscription` gained `platform` ('web' | 'android' | 'ios', default 'web' so every existing row is unchanged) and `device_name` (Flyway `V18__push_subscription_platform.sql`, mirrored in `scripts/sql/migrate_production.sql`; entity has `@ColumnDefault("'web'")` so ddl-auto can add it to a populated table). Native rows keep the FCM/APNs token in `endpoint` (unique, like a browser endpoint) and '-' in p256dh/auth. `WebPushService` is still the ONLY send entry point the schedulers call: `sendToSubscriptions` routes `sub.isNative()` rows to NEW `service/NativePushService` and the rest to VAPID as before; `isEnabled()` is now "either transport configured" (schedulers keep running when only one is), `isWebPushEnabled()` is what `/api/push/vapid-public-key` checks (204 → browsers don't subscribe), `isNativePushEnabled()`. An UNREGISTERED result deactivates that one row (same as 410 on Web Push).
`NativePushService` — Android: FCM HTTP v1 with a Firebase service-account JSON (`push.fcm.service-account-file` / `-json`; RS256 JWT-bearer → cached access token; payload = notification + data{url,tag} + android.notification{channel_id cgp_default, tag} + apns block). iOS: APNs directly over HTTP/2 with the .p8 auth key (`push.apns.key-file`/`key-pem`, `key-id`, `team-id`, `bundle-id` com.churchgeniuspro.app, `production` true|false → api.push.apple.com / sandbox; ES256 provider token cached 50 min, re-signed on ExpiredProviderToken; apns-topic/push-type alert/priority 10/collapse-id tag; payload aps{alert,sound,thread-id} + url/tag). No SDK dependency — `java.security` + `java.net.http`. Neither configured → warns at startup, rows skipped (like VAPID). 404/UNREGISTERED/invalid-token (FCM) and 410/BadDeviceToken/Unregistered (APNs) → UNREGISTERED.
`PushController` NEW `POST /api/push/register-native {token, platform: android|ios, deviceName?}` — session required (401), platform validated (400), upsert by token: re-keyed to THIS user + re-activated (shared phone), device name kept if the app sends none. Logout deactivation is unchanged: both logout paths now resolve the app token as the `pushEndpoint` (session.js `CGP_pushEndpoint`, memberHome `cgpPushEndpoint` → `window.CGP_NATIVE.getPushToken()`), so Phase 1's `/api/auth/logout {pushEndpoint}` deactivates the row. `MemberAccountDeletionService` already deactivates by user key, platform-agnostic.
WEB — `native-bridge.js` section 5: `window.CGP_NATIVE.registerPush()` (checkPermissions → requestPermissions once → register(); `registration` listener posts the token once per user+token via sessionStorage `cgp_native_push_synced`; token cached in sessionStorage `cgp_native_push_token`; `pushNotificationActionPerformed` → `location.href = data.url` (same-site only); `pushNotificationReceived` → `window.refreshNotificationBadge()` (session.js, NEW global = `refreshNotifications`) / `pollUnreadCount()` (memberHome)); `getPushToken()`, `forgetPushSync()`; dispatches `cgp:native-ready` at the end because shell.js injects it deferred (session.js may run first). session.js `setupPushNotifications`: `if (window.Capacitor) { CGP_nativeRegisterPush(); return; }` (waits for `cgp:native-ready` if needed); memberHome `registerMemberPush` same. Browser path untouched (pinned by `NativeBridgeRatchetTest.nativePushContract` — no page outside the bridge references `register-native`).
MOBILE (`C:\workspace\ChurchGeniusPro_Mobile`) — `@capacitor/push-notifications` 8.1.3 synced (Package.swift, capacitor.settings/build.gradle, capacitor.plugins.json). Android: `MainActivity.createPushChannel()` creates channel `cgp_default` (IMPORTANCE_HIGH) = server `push.fcm.android-channel`; manifest meta-data default channel + `@drawable/ic_stat_notification` (white bell vector); `google-services.json.example` placeholder — the Capacitor template's `app/build.gradle` applies the Google Services plugin only when the real `google-services.json` exists, so the build never depends on it. iOS: `AppDelegate` forwards `didRegisterForRemoteNotificationsWithDeviceToken` / `didFail…` to Capacitor (`.capacitorDidRegisterForRemoteNotifications`); no Firebase in the iOS target (APNs direct); `aps-environment` entitlement from Phase 3; `PushNotifications.presentationOptions: badge,sound,alert`.
VERIFIED — unit: `NativePushServiceTest` (11: RS256 + ES256 JWTs verified with the public keys, FCM/APNs request shape, token caching, 401/ExpiredProviderToken refresh-and-retry, UNREGISTERED vs FAILED, disabled/one-platform), `PushRoutingTest` (9: routing split, deactivation, org fan-out logs once per user, register-native 401/400/upsert/re-key, VAPID 204). Live (PostgreSQL + packaged JAR, Playwright with a fake Capacitor shell + stub PushNotifications plugin): staff login → prompt → register → row `USR-p1staff|android|staff|Pixel 8`; second page re-checks permission only, no duplicate POST; notification tap → /followups?tab=prayer; logout sends the token → row inactive; member signs in on the same phone → same row re-keyed `MBR-bob|member|active`; member logout deactivates; permission denied → nothing posted, no errors; plain browser → no native call, bridge inert, SW still registered; unauthenticated register-native → 401 — 25/25. Android debug APK builds (7.2 MB) with PushNotificationsPlugin + channel + icon. Full suite 245 classes / 1084 tests / 0 failures. iOS not compiled (no Mac).
STORE SETUP DONE 2026-10-15 — Play app created (com.churchgeniuspro.app), release 1.0 (1) on Internal testing (upload key `ChurchGeniusPro_Mobile/android/keystore/churchgeniuspro-upload.jks`, git-ignored). `.well-known/assetlinks.json` now carries the REAL fingerprints: Play app-signing key 64:B2:AC:… (what installed apps are signed with) + upload key B8:B8:67:… (locally built release APKs). Firebase project churchgeniuspro-6b8c6; `google-services.json` in `android/app/`; FCM service account configured locally via application-local.properties (`push.fcm.service-account-file`), prod via `PUSH_FCM_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_JSON`. iOS AASA still has the TEAMID placeholder.
TO CONFIGURE (prod, user side) — Firebase project → Android app com.churchgeniuspro.app → `google-services.json` into `android/app/`; service-account JSON → `PUSH_FCM_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_FILE` (or `_JSON`). Apple Developer → Keys → APNs key → `PUSH_APNS_KEY_FILE` + `PUSH_APNS_KEY_ID` + `PUSH_APNS_TEAM_ID`; `PUSH_APNS_PRODUCTION=false` for TestFlight/dev builds. **2026-10-14 (latest, later 2): Mobile app — Phase 4 (shared web-to-native bridge). Dev/test only, NOT deployed.**
WEB (this repo) — NEW `static/native-bridge.js`, loaded on every page (`shell.js` `add('/native-bridge.js')` first in its list; explicit `<script>` tags on `memberHome.html` and `login.html`, which do not load shell.js). In a browser `window.Capacitor` is undefined → the file sets `window.CGP_NATIVE={isNative:false,platform:'web'}` and returns; nothing else runs (pinned by `NativeBridgeRatchetTest`). Inside the Capacitor shell: (1) DOWNLOADS — one capture-phase click listener on `a[download]` with `blob:`/`data:` (or same-origin + download) href = what jsPDF `save()`, SheetJS `writeFile()` and FileSaver do → `Filesystem.writeFile` (CACHE, `cgp-exports/<name>`) → native `Share.share({files:[uri]})` (Save to Files / Drive / Print / Mail); `window.CGP_shareBlob(blob,name)` for pages that want it directly. (2) `window.open` — `''`/about:blank → a stand-in window whose `document.write` collects HTML and whose `.print()` (or a `window.print()` inside the written HTML) → `Printer.printHtml`; `blob:`/`data:` → share sheet; other origins or `_blank` files → `Browser.open` (system browser); plain same-origin → navigation. Clicks on links to other origins → `Browser.open`; `mailto:/tel:/sms:` left to Capacitor. (3) `window.print()` → `Printer.print` (falls back to sharing the page HTML without the plugin). (4) PWA off in the shell: unregisters any service worker; `session.js` `setupPushNotifications`, `pwa.js` (install banner), `login.html` SW registration and `memberHome.registerMemberPush` all `return` when `window.Capacitor` exists (native push comes in Phase 5). (5) Android back button → history.back / exitApp on the root pages. `<html>` gets `cgp-native cgp-native-<platform>` classes. `CspFilter` connect-src now includes `blob: data:` (same-document objects; needed to read a generated PDF back — no network origin added).
MOBILE (`C:\workspace\ChurchGeniusPro_Mobile`) — plugins `@capacitor/filesystem`, `@capacitor/share` added; NEW in-app **Printer** plugin: Android `PrinterPlugin.java` (`print` → `PrintManager` with the bridge WebView's print adapter; `printHtml` → off-screen WebView loaded with the site as base URL, then PrintManager) registered in `MainActivity.onCreate` before `super`; iOS `PrinterPlugin.swift` (`CAPBridgedPlugin`, `UIPrintInteractionController` with `viewPrintFormatter`, off-screen `WKWebView` for HTML) registered by `MainViewController: CAPBridgeViewController.capacitorDidLoad`; `Main.storyboard` initial VC → `MainViewController` (module App); both Swift files added to `project.pbxproj`. VERIFIED — `./gradlew assembleDebug` green with the plugin (classes present in the APK; 7 Capacitor plugins listed); iOS not compiled (needs a Mac). Playwright on the live app with a FAKE `window.Capacitor` (app UA): bridge active, no SW/banner, jsPDF-style download → Filesystem+Share, `window.open('')`+write+print → `Printer.printHtml` with the HTML, `window.print()` → `Printer.print`, external link + `window.open(other origin)` → `Browser.open` ×2 without leaving the page, member portal loads it via the tag; plain browser: bridge inert, downloads still normal browser downloads, SW still registers — 14/14. Full suite 243 classes / 0 failures.

**2026-10-14 (later): Mobile app — Phase 1 (Must-Fix M1, M2, M3, M7 of the Mobile Feasibility & Gap Analysis). Dev/test only, NOT deployed. Plain web release; no Capacitor code yet.**
WHY — the report's must-fixes before any app stores a credential: login.html kept the base64 password in localStorage and replayed it behind a WebAuthn "biometric" gate (M1); one clear-text `signup.remember` token per account meant a second device silently un-remembered the first and logout/unticked-box wiped every device (M2); the push subscription stayed keyed to the previous user after logout (M3); `/api/**` had no Cache-Control (M7).
M2 — NEW `hibernate/RememberMeToken` (`remember_me_token`: signup_id, token_hash SHA-256 unique, prev_token_hash + rotated_at (5-min grace for a second tab), device_label, platform web|android|ios, created/last_used/expires/revoked_at), `RememberMeTokenRepository`, `service/RememberMeService` (the ONLY owner of the `rememberToken` cookie: `issue` = 32 random bytes base64url, hash stored, sliding 30-day expiry, cap 10 live rows per login (oldest revoked); `resolve` by hash → prev hash in grace → LEGACY `signup.remember` lookup that migrates the device to a row and clears the column; `rotate` on every auto-login; `revokeCurrent` = this device only; `revokeAllFor` = sign out everywhere; nightly purge `scheduler.job.remember-me-purge` 03:30 Chicago of dead rows >7 days). `LoginController`: login ticked → issue; unticked → revoke THIS browser only (was: wipe all devices); `check-remember` → resolve, the existing enabled/subscription/demo re-checks, rotate; `/api/auth/logout` → revoke this device only + optional body `{pushEndpoint}` → `deactivateByEndpoint`. `SessionController./api/logout` now delegates to the full logout (lazy-injected LoginController; falls back to invalidate-only when absent). `ForgotPasswordController` reset-success → `revokeAllFor(signup.id)`. Flyway `V16__remember_me_token.sql` (CREATE IF NOT EXISTS, no FK — Flyway runs before Hibernate makes `signup` on a fresh DB) mirrored in `scripts/sql/migrate_production.sql` before P6. `signup.remember` column KEPT, no longer written — drop in a later release.
M1 — `login.html`: no `rememberedPassword`, no password restore into the field, no `navigator.credentials` (registerBiometric/doBiometricLogin/initBiometricButton removed); one-time `localStorage.removeItem` of the old keys; only `rememberedUsername` kept for prefill; `#biometricBtn` stays hidden with a no-op `doBiometricLogin()` that fires `cgp:native-biometric-login` only when `window.Capacitor` exists (Phase 6). VISIBLE CHANGE: the web "Fingerprint / Face ID" button is gone (it replayed a stored password).
M3 — `session.js` `_subscribePush`: an EXISTING browser subscription is re-POSTed to `/api/push/subscribe` once per signed-in user (`sessionStorage.cgp_push_synced` = clientId) so the row is re-keyed/re-activated; new `CGP_pushEndpoint(timeoutMs)` (never rejects, ≤1.5 s) and `logout()` sends `{pushEndpoint}` to `/api/auth/logout`. Same in `memberHome.html` (own `cgpPushEndpoint` copy — the page does not load session.js) and `home.html` `doLogout`. `access-denied.html`, `stripeIntegration.html`, `ntag-session.js` keep calling `/api/logout`, which now does the full logout.
M7 — `webfilter/ApiNoStoreFilter` on `/api/*`, order −1, sets `Cache-Control: no-store` BEFORE the chain (controllers that set their own header win).
TESTS — `security/RememberMeServiceTest` (13), `RememberMeWebTest` (10: two devices, rotation, unticked-box and logout are device-scoped, tampered cookie cleared, legacy cookie bridged, push deactivation on both logout endpoints, oversize endpoint ignored), `ApiNoStoreFilterTest` (3), `ClientCredentialRatchetTest` (3: login.html holds no credential; every logout path carries pushEndpoint; existing subscription re-keyed); `LoginThrottleWebTest`/`SubscriptionExpiryLoginTest` constructors +2 mocks. Full suite 237 classes / 0 failures. Real PostgreSQL 16 (cloud): fresh boot applies V16, table shape verified, `/api/**` and `/api/auth/check-remember` answer `Cache-Control: no-store`; curl round trip: 2 devices → 2 rows, both auto-login + rotate, old value valid in grace, logout of one leaves the other, legacy `signup.remember` cookie logs in once and migrates. Playwright 390px on the live app 14/14 (no password in localStorage, cookie HttpOnly 30 d, auto-login in a new context, rotation, explicit logout → no auto-login, username prefilled, unticked login sets no cookie).
DATA — additive only: one new table; no existing row of any table is changed; nothing in accounting/ledger code touched. DEPLOY — run `migrate_production.sql` or let Flyway apply V16; nothing else. NEXT — Phase 2 (M4 self-service deletion, M5 UGC report/block).

**2026-10-09: Worship — Manual Assignment brought up to Automatic Assignment's features; the two workflows now own separate rows. Dev/test only, NOT deployed.**
WHY — `/memberWorship` → Assignments → Manual Assignment only offered one date at a time (no skip, songs, group view, print, PDF, group delete), and `worship_assignment` was keyed by (client, group, date) in the upsert, so a manual save and an automatic save for the same group+date overwrote each other's members and type. Production pre-flight (user, pgAdmin, 2026-10-08): NO unique constraint on `worship_assignment` (pkey + 2 indexes only), 63 active auto / 165 soft-deleted auto / 1 manual row, 0 NULL types, 0 duplicates.
DB — Flyway `V13__worship_assignment_per_workflow_key.sql` (+ `config/WorshipSchemaInitializer` re-running it after Hibernate, same pattern as V8/V11/V12; mirrored in `scripts/sql/migrate_production.sql` BEFORE the P6 section because `MigrationScript.section()` reads P6 to end-of-file): `assignment_type` NOT NULL DEFAULT 'manual' (NULL → 'auto'), new `skipped BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false`, `DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS uq_worship_assignment_client_group_date` (only Hibernate-created dev DBs have it), partial unique index `ux_worship_assignment_active (client_id, group_id, assignment_date, assignment_type) WHERE delete_flag = false` (soft-deleted rows no longer block a regenerate; created only when no active duplicates — notice otherwise); `worship_song.schedule_type VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'auto'` + index (every existing dated song list = Automatic; library rows ignore it). Entities: `@UniqueConstraint` removed from `WorshipAssignment`, `@ColumnDefault` on all three new/changed columns (NOT NULL rule). Additive — no row deleted or re-keyed.
API (`WorshipPlanningController`, additive) — `GET /assignments` gains `type=manual|auto` (400 otherwise; omitted = all, as before) and `includeSkipped` (default false → the read-only views Worship Planning / member portal never show a skipped manual date); detail maps carry `skipped`. `POST /assignments` upserts by (client, group, date, TYPE); absent/unknown type = manual, so a manual save can never land on the auto row and vice versa. New `PUT /assignments/{id}/skip {skipped}` — flag only, members never read/written, manual rows only (400 for auto). New `POST /assignments/manual-dates {groupId, dayOfWeek 1-7, startDate, weeks 1-52}` — EMPTY manual rows only (no roster read, no rotation, nothing from the auto-assign path; existing dates reported, not touched). Songs: `GET /songs`, `/songs/batch`, `POST /songs`, `/songs/bulk`, `/songs/upload` take optional `scheduleType` (default auto = every pre-existing caller); songMap carries it. `/auto-assign` untouched. `TenantScopedLookupRatchetTest` +1 for the skip handler's `findById` (getClientId compared, like delete).
UI (`static/memberWorship.html` only; `target/classes` copy synced) — Manual tab = per-group collapsible pivot tables (same CSS as Auto): "+ Add Empty Dates" (group · weekday or "Start date only (1 date)" · start · weeks), "+ Add" cell modal, roster drag, chip move, × remove, ⊘ Skip / ↩ Unskip (row dimmed, members still shown; strike-through only on the date text), 🗑 per date, Delete per group, 📚 Manage Songs (shared library), ✎ Songs per date (manual list), 🖨 Print / ⬇ PDF per group + all groups. Loads `?type=manual&includeSkipped=true`, saves `assignmentType:'manual'` per dirty row (debounced, coalesced; members re-synced from the response so chips carry DB ids), songs with `scheduleType=manual`. Shared helpers (`openCellAdd`, `onCellDrop`, `removeCellMember`, `onMemberChipDragStart`, `openWeekSongMgr`, `loadInlineSongs`/`renderInlineSongs`, `refreshSongPanel`, `buildGroupTableHtml/Data`, `printGroupTable`, `downloadGroupPdf`, `printAllGroupTables`, `downloadAllGroupsPdf`) take a trailing `sched` defaulting to `'auto'`; a chip can never be dropped across workflows. **Automatic Assignment unchanged**: its markup block and its generation/rotation/skip-rebalance/pivot JS are byte-identical to the pre-work file and pinned by SHA-256 in `worship/ManualAssignmentPageTest`.
ALSO FIXED (approved 2026-10-08 23:45, the ONE change to Automatic behaviour): `renderMemberRoster` passed `mem.memberId` but the groups API field is `id`, so a roster-dragged chip in the Automatic table had an undefined memberId until reload (its × and chip-move missed). Now `mem.memberId ?? mem.id`; sits outside the pinned Auto regions, so the hashes are unchanged. Playwright: Auto roster drag then × now removes the member (was broken before).
TESTS — `worship/ManualAssignmentIsolationTest` (13, in-memory fake repos `InMemoryWorshipRepos`: separate rows per workflow, re-save isolation both ways, default/unknown type, list filters + tenant, skip keeps members + hidden by default, skip refused for auto/other tenant/deleted, manual-dates empty rows only + validation, songs per workflow, library shared, entity defaults; mutation-checked: the old type-blind lookup fails 5/13), `AutoAssignmentUnchangedTest` (3: preview writes nothing + rotation order, Auto tab's own calls, regenerate after soft-delete), `ManualAssignmentPageTest` (4). Full suite 2,170 run / 0 failures (+ 26 migration-script tests green). Playwright desktop 1280 + 390px (cloud Chromium, stubbed API modelling the new endpoints): 100/100 — add dates, single date, cell add, roster pick, roster drag, chip move, skip/unskip with members intact and no member write, songs isolation, print/PDF content, deletes, auto rows byte-identical, Auto generate/skip/cell-add unchanged, no overflow. V13 on real PostgreSQL 16 (embedded, VM): prod shape / migrate_production mirror / dev shape with old constraint / fresh / active duplicates — 49/49; full app boot on a fresh DB: Flyway 13 success, index + columns present, initializer logs OK.
DEPLOY — run `scripts/sql/migrate_production.sql` (or let Flyway apply V13 on first start — both idempotent); nothing else. Rebuild + restart.

**2026-10-07 (later 7): Phase 5 — payroll SSN last-4 encryption-key rotation. Dev/test only, NOT deployed; Azure variables NOT set; production NOT rotated.**
Prod has 2 `payroll_employee` rows: 1 legacy plaintext, 1 encrypted under the derived dev key (no `PAYROLL_SSN_ENC_KEY` was ever set), so the key could not simply be set. `payroll/service/SsnCrypto` now takes `payroll.ssn-enc-key-previous` (`PAYROLL_SSN_ENC_KEY_PREVIOUS`; base64 key or the sentinel `derived` = the built-in dev key); decrypt = current key first, previous second; `classify()` returns an explicit `State` (BLANK / LEGACY_PLAINTEXT / CURRENT_KEY / PREVIOUS_KEY / UNREADABLE); `reEncrypt()` package-private; startup fails if PREVIOUS is set without a current key, or either key is bad base64 / wrong length (message names the variable only); GCM/IV format unchanged; single-arg constructor kept for tests. New `payroll/service/SsnRotationService` (mirrors `PlaidTokenRotationService`): refuses on the derived key; classifies every row; rotates LEGACY_PLAINTEXT + PREVIOUS_KEY rows one `REQUIRES_NEW` transaction each via a `@Lazy` self proxy; returns **counts only** (`examined, blank, legacyPlaintext, alreadyOnCurrentKey, wouldRotate|rotated, unreadable, pending, status, dryRun, rotationInProgress`) — unreadable rows are logged server-side by employee id, never a value; one `payroll_audit_log` row per tenant (`SSN_KEY_ROTATED`, counts only). New `payroll/controller/ServiceAdminPayrollController`: `GET /api/serviceadmin/payroll/ssn-rotation` (dry run) / `POST` (apply), behind `ServiceAdminAuthFilter` + `CsrfOriginFilter` + the controller's own role check, no new exemptions. `SecretsStartupReport` lists `PAYROLL_SSN_ENC_KEY_PREVIOUS` as rotation-only; `PAYROLL_SSN_ENC_KEY` stays WARN-only (NOT fail-fast) until the rotation has completed and that follow-up is approved. `TenantScopedLookupRatchetTest` baseline +1 for the service's own-loop `findById`. Tests: `payroll/service/SsnCryptoRotationTest` (7), `SsnRotationServiceTest` (5), `payroll/controller/ServiceAdminPayrollControllerTest` (6: unauthenticated, tenant user with role string, service admin without CSRF, role re-check, valid, no exemption). Context boot verified on local PostgreSQL (beans wire, transactional proxy present, dry run OK). Suite 2,198 / 0 fail / 234 classes.
**Runbook — rotate the payroll SSN key (do each step only with explicit approval):** (1) deploy this build; (2) generate a key locally `openssl rand -base64 32`; Azure: `PAYROLL_SSN_ENC_KEY=<key>`, `PAYROLL_SSN_ENC_KEY_PREVIOUS=derived`; restart — log shows `[Secrets] PAYROLL_SSN_ENC_KEY: set` and the SsnCrypto "rotation in progress" WARN; (3) Service Admin session → `GET /api/serviceadmin/payroll/ssn-rotation` → expect `examined=2, legacyPlaintext=1, wouldRotate=2, unreadable=0`; (4) `POST` the same URL (same-site Origin) → `rotated=2, pending=0, unreadable=0`; open the employee screen / a paystub to confirm the last-4 still shows; (5) remove `PAYROLL_SSN_ENC_KEY_PREVIOUS`, restart; (6) separately approved follow-up: promote `PAYROLL_SSN_ENC_KEY` to required/fail-fast in `SecretsStartupReport`. If `unreadable>0` stop and investigate the employee ids in the server log before removing PREVIOUS.
**Fix after first local run of the JAR (2026-10-07, later 7b):** `SecretsStartupReport` lines were `NAME: missing` — `MaskingPatternLayout` treats `*_KEY: value` / `*_SECRET: value` as a credential and printed `PAYROLL_SSN_ENC_KEY: ***REDACTED***`. Format is now `[Secrets] <status>  <NAME> …` (status first, no `:`/`=` after the name); `SecretsStartupReportTest` runs every line through `SensitiveDataMasker.mask` and asserts it is unchanged. `DB_*_PROD` lines are reported only under `prod` (other profiles get the datasource from their profile file, so "set" was misleading).
**Observation (pre-existing, not changed):** `app.base-url` is defined only in the `prod`/`local`/`trial` profile files, so a context started with only the `it` profile (the Docker `*IT` classes) cannot resolve `${app.base-url}` in `TrialRegistrationLinkService`/`EmailService`/`ServiceClientService`; verified this fails identically with and without the old `spring.profiles.active=prod` line, so Phase 4.2 did not introduce it. Proposed fix (needs approval): base-file default `app.base-url=${APP_BASE_URL:http://localhost:8080}`, prod file keeps its prod default.

**2026-10-07 (later 6): Production-readiness / configuration-security audit — Phase 3 (repo cleanup) + Phase 4 items 4.1–4.5, 4.8 (hardening). Dev/test only, NOT deployed.**
Read-only audit first (evidence/severity report in chat). Phase 3: `DecryptTest.java` (legacy key + real token) deleted; `.gitignore` now excludes `logs/`, `*.log`, `_bak_*/`, `_to_delete/`, `_sync_tmp/`, `~$*`, `Claude outputs/`, `application-trial.properties`; `migrate_production.sql` + `fix_worship_song.sql` → `scripts/sql/`, `seed_test_data.sql` → `src/test/resources/sql/` (only executable refs were `schema/MigrationScript` + `integration/MigrationScriptIT`, updated); all 20 `System.out/err`/`printStackTrace` sites (ForgotPassword/ForgotUsername controllers, EmailService, ReminderSchedulerService) → SLF4J with `EmailMask.mask` on addresses and the exception object kept on failures; ratchet `architecture/NoStdoutLoggingTest`. Phase 4: `application-prod.properties` `logging.level.com.churchgeniuspro=INFO`; `spring.profiles.active=prod` REMOVED from the base file (Azure sets `SPRING_PROFILES_ACTIVE`; `restart-app.bat` passes `-Dspring-boot.run.profiles=local`; a run with no profile fails fast); `config/SecretsStartupReport` rewritten — present/missing per variable (required / expected / optional / rotation-only), never values; **missing `CGP_CID_KEY` now refuses to start in `prod`**; `PAYROLL_SSN_ENC_KEY` deliberately WARN-only until the payroll key-rotation phase; `DataSeeder.seedServiceAdmin` (placeholder-hash `signup` insert that always failed — table has no `role` column) removed; `CsrfOriginFilter` no longer trusts `X-Forwarded-Host`, and `localhost`/`127.0.0.1` Origins pass only when `app.base-url` is local (`Week2HardeningTest` assertion flipped accordingly); `pom.xml` `maven-jar-plugin` excludes `application-local/trial.properties` from the packaged JAR only (verified: still in `target/classes`, absent from `BOOT-INF/classes`, nothing else excluded). Tests: `webfilter/CsrfOriginFilterTest` (6), `config/SecretsStartupReportTest` (5, one `@EnabledIf` CGP_CID_KEY), `config/DataSeederNoServiceAdminTest` (1). Suite 2,180 / 0 fail / 231 classes. **HELD for separate approval:** 4.6 `ClientIpResolver` X-Forwarded-For hop choice (needs read-only analysis first), 4.7 HSTS `includeSubDomains` (pending Porkbun DNS confirmation), payroll SSN key rotation (prod has 1 row encrypted under the derived dev key: needs `PAYROLL_SSN_ENC_KEY_PREVIOUS`-style decrypt-old/encrypt-new before the key is set), AES/ECB→GCM for `EncryptionUtil`, CSP nonces, plaintext-password fallback removal, Key Vault, `ddl-auto=validate`. Still outstanding in Stripe dashboard: register `payment_intent.succeeded/payment_failed/partially_funded` on the webhook; restricted key needs Customers write (first intent per client creates a Customer).

**2026-10-07 (later 5): Voice "limit reached" shown for every refusal — fixed to show the real reason. Dev/test only, NOT deployed.**
Income/Expense said "Voice limit reached" while Service Admin showed 10.7/30 min: the quota was fine (`/api/openai-usage/status` voiceAvailable=true) and so were the per-church Voice Features flags. `voice-openai.js` treated EVERY 403 from `POST /api/voice/command` as `onDisabled(res.d.status||{})`, and the pages said "limit reached" unless `status.voiceEnabled===false`; only the quota gate attached a status map, so staff-only (member-portal login on a staff page), plan (`SubscriptionFeatureFilter` aiVoice) and feature-flag refusals all read as a limit. FIX — `VoiceCommandController`: every 403 carries `reason` (`STAFF_ONLY` / `FEATURE_OFF` / `VOICE_OFF` / `LIMIT`) + `status`; quota text split into "disabled by your administrator" vs "limit reached". `voice-openai.js`: `onDisabled(status, {reason, error})`. `income-voice.js`, `expense-voice.js`, `home-voice.js`, `voice-nav.js`: `voiceRefusalMessage(st, why)` — LIMIT → limit text, VOICE_OFF/voiceEnabled=false → disabled text, otherwise the server's error (plan filter message included), fallback "not available for this login". The on-load `/api/openai-usage/status` check is unchanged. No gate, limit, Service Admin or subscription behaviour changed; whether member logins may use voice is still "staff only" (product decision pending). Tests: `voice/VoiceRefusalReasonTest` (2); Playwright (fake microphone WAV) on /income and /expense: each of 5 refusal kinds shows its own text. Full suite 2,167 / 0 failures / 50 skips.

**2026-10-07 (later 4): AI Search / Help Center know every feature; "not available" no longer means "does not exist". Dev/test only, NOT deployed.**
WHY — "What is ticketing?" answered "not a feature available for your account": Ticketing was in none of the three knowledge sources (`ai-assistant.js` DESTS, `HelpContentCatalog`, `helpCenter.html` HC_KB), the client resolver treated any feature mention as a navigation request, and both prompts told the model that anything outside the user's permitted list is "not available". FIX — (1) `ai-assistant.js`: every `shell.js` menu page is a DESTS entry with a `desc` (68 entries; added Attendance, Volunteers, Sunday School, Bank Sync, Files & Notes, Stripe/WhatsApp Integration, Private Page Access, NTAG Login, Temporary Access, Ticketing, AI Assistant, Song Book (+Access), Guess It, Email Settings; Donation → "Donation/Give"); entries may declare `perm` (session.js NAV_PERM key; `CGP_PERMS[perm]===false` denies; `optIn:true` = Member logins need explicit true) and `feature` (`CGP_hasFeature`); `classify()` gained `explain` ("what is / explain / tell me about"): `resolveLocal` answers from `desc`, offers Open only when available, otherwise "part of ChurchGeniusPro but not enabled for your current role, permissions or plan — an administrator can enable it"; a plain unavailable navigation answer now appends the description; `buildContext()` adds `allFeatures:[{label,url,category,description,available}]` (availablePages unchanged). (2) `OpenAiVoiceService.assistPrompt`: explain any feature in allFeatures, add the not-enabled sentence when available=false, relevantPages/open only from availablePages. (3) `HelpContentCatalog` +21 articles (ticketing, ai_assistant, donation_give, member_portal, membership_requests, pledges, bank_sync, worship_planning, song_book, sunday_school, stripe_integration, whatsapp_integration, email_settings, files_notes, private_page_access, temporary_access, users_permissions, favorites, install_app, plans_trial, guess_it); `HelpAssistantService` grounds the model on the WHOLE catalogue tagged `[accessible=true|false]` (links still only for accessible; `restricted` echoed); keyword fallback explains an inaccessible article without steps/link and sets `restricted`. (4) `helpCenter.html` HC_KB +10 cards. Tests: `help/AssistantKnowledgeTest` (5: menu→DESTS+article inventory ratchet via `MENU_TO_ARTICLE`, prompt rules, keyword fallback with/without permission, AI grounding + link filtering, HC_KB cards). Playwright: User without `more.ticketing` → "What is ticketing?" explains + not-enabled, "Open ticketing" still refused, no `.aa-open`; with permission → Open offered; Member without opt-in → unavailable; LLM request carries 68 allFeatures with availability. Full suite 2,165 / 0 failures / 50 skips.

**2026-10-07 (later 3): Trial feature-discovery tips. Dev/test only, NOT deployed.**
`static/trial-tips.js` (loaded by `shell.js` after product-tour.js; self-activating) shows ONE small fixed card (bottom-right desktop / bottom-centre phone, no backdrop, no focus steal, no scroll lock; 💡 Tip · title · text · Got it · X · optional "Open …" link) to Trial STAFF logins only. Server decides on every call (`TrialTipController`, `GET /api/trial-tips/state`, `POST /api/trial-tips/{key}/shown|dismissed`): staff session of a managed tenant (`TestDataService.isManagedTenant` = TRIAL-/DEMO-) or of a church on the TRIAL plan (`SubscriptionService.trialInfo != null`); Church-portal, Member, temp (`tempAccessId`), NTAG (`ntagCredId`), ServiceAdmin and Free/Standard/Pro get `eligible:false` → script inert (cached for the session). Rules: one card per page view; nothing while `#cgpDemoTrialOverlay` / `#cgpTourOverlay` / `#cgpTrialNotice` is on screen; ≥10 min between tips; ≤3 per day; a tip shows at most twice (second time on a later day) unless dismissed (Got it / X / link = retired); context tips (page prefix) win over general (`pages:['*']`); never offered when `CGP_PERMS[perm]===false`, `!CGP_hasFeature(feature)` or the role is excluded. 23 tips, content in the script (`TIPS` array; add an object = new tip; keys `[a-z0-9-]`), wording verified against the code (meeting purge is 90 days; "Upload Scanned Form"; worship skip-dates; printable temporary badges). State = new table `trial_tip_state` (client_id, username, tip_key, shown_count, last_shown_at, dismissed_at; unique triple; ddl-auto) — keys and timestamps only; 200 keys per login cap; recording for an ineligible login answers `ignored`, never an error. Tests: `trialtips/TrialTipControllerTest` (4), `TrialTipsScriptTest` (2: loader order, catalogue shape, perm/feature keys exist in session.js); Playwright desktop + 390px: context tip on /bankSync, page typeable/clickable with the card up, X and Got it recorded and never return, 10-min gap, daily cap, feature/permission gating, ineligible cached (no further state calls), blocked by tour overlay, general tip on plain pages. Full suite 2,160 / 0 failures / 50 skips.

**2026-10-07 (later 2): /ai-assistant is a chat window. Dev/test only, NOT deployed.**
`ai-assistant.html` rebuilt: header · scrolling transcript (`#chatLog`, user bubbles right / assistant left, timestamps, welcome bubble with example chips) · composer pinned at the bottom (`<textarea id="aiSearchInput">` + Send; Enter sends, Shift+Enter = newline; auto-grow). The shared engine keeps its hooks (`.ai-search-wrap > .ai-search-bar`, `#aiSearchInput`, hidden `#aiResultsWrap`) so its Type / Voice / Converse / Guide / Help controls attach to the composer unchanged. NEW optional adapter in `ai-assistant.js`: `window.CGP_ASSISTANT_UI { question(text,mode), answer(cardEl, card, q), busy(on), notice(text) }` — `runAssist` reports the question ONCE per turn (Type, Voice transcript and Converse follow-ups all enter there), `renderCard` hands the finished card over instead of writing `#aiResultsWrap`, `busy` → typing indicator, `toast` → notice bubble; `CGP_ASSISTANT.open` exposed for restored Open / New-tab buttons. Without the adapter (Home + every injected bar) the code path is unchanged. Answers: escaped then formatted (paragraphs, line breaks, `- `/`1.` lists, mixed blocks); page suggestions, permission-denied style (`intent==='denied'`) and the Help Center link stay in the bubble. Transcript persisted in sessionStorage `aa.chat` (≤60 messages, bubble html ≤20k, discarded when a different user signs in; "Clear conversation" removes it → welcome state). The page's old placeholder `window.runAiSearch=function(){}` is gone: the engine used to capture it as the "data lookup" delegate, so plain (non-help, non-data) questions on this page produced no answer — now they reach the LLM flow. Tests: `ai/AiAssistantChatPageTest` (2); Playwright desktop 1280 + mobile 390: Shift+Enter newline, Enter send, typing indicator, 5 questions → 5 user + 5 assistant bubbles (Type, data, denied, Converse ×2), lists/Open buttons, reload + navigate-away restore, restored Open → engine `open`, clear → welcome + storage removed, chip send, no horizontal scroll. Full suite 2,154 / 0 failures / 50 skips.

**2026-10-07: Phase 6 hot-fix — live card payment was refused by Stripe; payment methods now Dashboard-managed (card, ACH debit, bank transfer, …). Dev/test only, NOT deployed.**
ROOT CAUSE — the platform Stripe account's default API version is `2026-09-30.endive`, on which `payment_method_types` is removed (Stripe 400 "no longer supported… managed from your Dashboard"); the create failed → invoice page said "Card payment is temporarily unavailable". Keys/account were fine (Test connection = `GET /balance` does not hit this).
FIX — `PlatformStripeService.createIntent` sends NO `payment_method_types` (Stripe offers what the Dashboard enables); optional `PLATFORM_STRIPE_EXCLUDED_METHODS` (comma list → `excluded_payment_method_types[i]`); `customer=` when the client has a billing Stripe Customer — `createCustomer` (name, billing email, client id) on first use, stored in NEW nullable `service_client.billing_stripe_customer_id` (an id, not a secret; needs *Customers: write* on a restricted key; refusal = logged, intent still created without bank transfer). `retrieveIntent` expands `payment_method`; `Intent` gained `paymentMethodType` / `instructionsUrl` / `lastErrorMessage` (masked); 404 → `StripeNotFoundException`. `BillingPaymentService`: stale stored intent (404) is replaced, not fatal; `customerFor`; `confirm` states PAID / PROCESSING (ACH clearing) / AWAITING_TRANSFER (+ Stripe hosted instructions URL) / FAILED (Stripe's masked reason) / PENDING; `payConfig` adds `pending` + `instructionsUrl` for an invoice already under way; method actually used is recorded (`methodCode`: card→CARD, us_bank_account→ACH_DEBIT, customer_balance→BANK_TRANSFER, link→LINK, cashapp→CASH_APP, …; a webhook carries only the method id so it is re-read from Stripe) on invoice + charges + receipt (`methodLabel`); webhook also handles `payment_intent.payment_failed` (outcome PAYMENT_FAILED) and `payment_intent.partially_funded` (PARTIALLY_FUNDED, remainder in detail) — audit row, invoice unchanged, Support Email alerted, redeliveries ignored. ACTOR = "stripe (online payment)". `invoice.html`: "Pay online", pending banner, per-state messages; `serviceadminhome.html` Stripe tab: methods managed in Dashboard, three webhook events, excluded list shown. Church donation Stripe untouched (`StripeSeparationRatchetTest` passes).
DEPLOY — (1) Stripe Dashboard → Developers → Webhooks: endpoint `/api/billing/stripe-webhook` for `payment_intent.succeeded`, `payment_intent.payment_failed`, `payment_intent.partially_funded`; set `PLATFORM_STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET` — REQUIRED before enabling ACH debit / bank transfer (they are recorded only by the webhook). (2) Restricted key needs PaymentIntents write + Customers write. (3) Enable methods in Dashboard → Settings → Payment methods. (4) ddl-auto adds `service_client.billing_stripe_customer_id`. IDEMPOTENCY — after the first deploy the live create still failed: `idempotency_error` (key `cgp-inv-1-5000-first` had been used at 7:49 with the old body; Stripe keeps keys 24 h). `createIntent` now appends an 8-hex SHA-256 of the request form to the caller's key (`cgp-inv-<id>-<cents>-<first|pi>-<hash>`): identical concurrent calls still share one intent, a changed body gets a fresh key; `idempotency_error` is named in the masked message. Tests: `BillingPhase6Test` 37 (+12), `BillingPaymentIT` 4 (real PostgreSQL 16 here); full suite 2,152 / 0 failures / 50 skips.

**2026-10-05: Subscriptions & billing plan — Phase 1 (client pricing) and Phase 2 (trial rules).** Plan doc: "Subscriptions, Trials & Billing — Audit and Implementation Plan" (claude.ai artifact). Phases 3 and 4 done 2026-10-05; Phase 5 DEPLOYED to production 2026-10-05 (Flyway V7–V11 applied 22:43–22:44 server time; post-deploy DB checks A1–A5 + B2/B3 all passed — see Claude outputs/phase5-deploy/PHASE5_DEPLOY_RUNBOOK.md); Phase 6 (Service Admin Stripe) implemented 2026-10-05 in dev/test only, awaiting review, NOT deployed (Phase 5 must be deployed and verified first).
PHASE 1 — `subscription_plan.yearly_price` (null = not offered yearly). `service_client`: subscription_type widened to 40, `billing_frequency` MONTHLY|YEARLY, `subscription_price` (the client's own price, copied from the plan's monthly/yearly price when plan or frequency changes, else kept — later plan price changes never touch existing clients), `price_overridden` (negotiated price). `service/SubscriptionLifecycleService` is the ONLY writer of price/frequency, validates a NEWLY assigned plan (must exist + be active), writes append-only `subscription_change` history, and evicts the per-client plan/status/messaging caches. V7 back-fills (current list price, LIMITED/FULL mapped). Register/Edit Client lists active plans from the DB; legacy LIMITED/FULL still resolve.
PHASE 2 — (A) Trial Requests: one OPEN (PENDING_VERIFICATION|VERIFIED) request per email (service check + partial unique index `ux_trial_request_open_email` on lower(email), V8 re-applied after Hibernate by `config/TrialRequestSchemaInitializer`); unverified requests expire after 24 h → `VERIFICATION_EXPIRED` (lazy sweep on submit/list); a fresh unverified duplicate returns 409 AWAITING_VERIFICATION + its reference so the page resumes code entry; a code that could not be emailed does not block. (B) /trialRegistration requires a choice: SAMPLE = existing TRIAL- provisioning (unchanged) / EMPTY = `ServiceClientService.save` + existing `approve` (CHR id, TRIAL plan, Chicago start, link's days, Bank Sync sandbox). A request with no choice (old page) = SAMPLE. (C) Conversion rule `SubscriptionLifecycleService.checkConversionAllowed`: a TRIAL- (sample-data) client may not leave the TRIAL plan; CHR and DEMO- unaffected. NO demo_data column — the id prefix is the marker. (D) `util/AppClock.today()` = America/Chicago for trial dates and expiry checks; `LoginRepository.countValid*Login` are default methods passing that date to `…On(username, today)`; temporary-access and NTAG sign-in refused when the church's subscription has ended; SignupController invitation rule = login rule; `POST /api/serviceadmin/clients/{id}/extend-trial` (Trial plan only; never touches plan/status/login flags). Member/Kids portal sign-in stays fail-open when the church cannot be identified (deliberate). (E) `SubscriptionService.trialInfo` → `/api/subscription/features.trial` and the trial agreement; `session.js` shows a dismissible trial notice for CHR Trial-plan accounts (TRIAL-/DEMO- get the agreement modal). (F) People limit also enforced in Follow-Ups → Add Volunteer and Import (family batches: new people counted; over the limit → nothing loaded).
PHASE 3 — Request for Subscription: `static/subscriptionReq.html` + `SubscriptionRequestController` (`/api/subscription-request`, AuthFilter-public, allowed by AccountStatusFilter and DemoTrialAgreementFilter for ended/unaccepted sessions). Church identified by `service_client.subscription_request_token` (stored as issued, @JsonIgnore, valid 60 days after the end date, reused by every reminder; `SubscriptionRequestService.linkFor`) OR a signed-in church owner/admin session. Registered Email must match the client email, church registration email or an active app_user. One open (NEW/IN_PROGRESS) request per church (service + V9 index `ux_subscription_request_open_client`, re-applied by `config/SubscriptionRequestSchemaInitializer`). Support Email notified (request kept if mail fails). TRIAL- clients' requests flagged registerAsNewClient. `SubscriptionExpiryNotifier`: TRIAL-plan clients get trial wording + the request link; paid-plan notices unchanged. Service Admin: Subscription Requests card (in progress / decline / complete / Register New Client pre-fill) + Convert dialog → `POST /api/serviceadmin/clients/{id}/convert` (`ServiceClientService.convert`: TRIAL plan → paid plan, CHR only, same client id/logins/data; history CONVERSION; optional confirmation as account mail; completes the linked request).
PHASE 4 — Monthly allowances (emails/SMS/online giving) reset every 30 days from `service_client.start_date` (America/Chicago): `SubscriptionService.periodStartFor(anchor, today)` = anchor + 30×N (future start → the start; no start → calendar month). `subscription_usage` keyed by (client_id, period_start) — constraint `uq_subscription_usage_period`; `usage_month` kept as history. No separate anchor column: a new start date (Convert, or a renewal with a new start) starts a fresh period; plan/frequency changes keep the count. V10 back-fills period_start = 1st of each old row's month and seeds each client's current period with this month's counts. KNOWN LIMITATION (accepted 2026-10-05): the FIRST migrated period cannot be reconstructed exactly from monthly aggregates (no daily dates) — usage between a period start in the previous month and the 1st is not counted in it; seeding from last month was deliberately rejected (could wrongly reduce allowances). Exact from the first normal period after V10. Edit Client: a CHANGED start date needs confirmation (409 START_DATE_CONFIRMATION_REQUIRED → page confirm() → resend with confirmStartDateChange=true); Trial-plan CHR clients cannot change plan in Edit — Trial → paid only through Convert. `describe().usage` adds periodStart/periodEnd (month kept).
PHASE 5 — Platform billing, no card payments (Stripe is Phase 6; promotion codes deferred). New tables (Hibernate): `client_charge` (UNBILLED→BILLED→PAID, or VOID; `invoice_id` set while a DRAFT reserves it), `billing_invoice` (DRAFT→SENT→PAID, DRAFT/SENT→VOID; kind RENEWAL|REQUEST|MANUAL; `@Version`; number `INV-yyyy-<id 5 digits>`; subtotal/discount/total; `access_token_hash` = SHA-256 hex of a 32-byte base64url token, expires due date (or today if later) + 60 days), `billing_invoice_line` (SUBSCRIPTION|CHARGE|CUSTOM). V11 = partial unique index `ux_billing_invoice_open_renewal` (client_id, due_date) WHERE kind='RENEWAL' AND status IN (DRAFT,SENT,PAID), re-applied after Hibernate by `config/BillingSchemaInitializer`. `service/BillingService`: renewal draft = client's own `subscription_price` (list price only if the client has none) for the period starting at `end_date`, plus the client's free UNBILLED charges; REQUEST draft from an open Subscription Request (requested plan's list price; sample-data requests refused); MANUAL = charges/custom lines only. Review: lines/discount (0…subtotal)/due date/billing email/note editable while DRAFT; charge lines always bill the charge's amount (editing a charge on a draft updates its line). Send: conditional DRAFT→SENT with version check, freezes totals, emails the link via `sendComposed` (no client id, display name "ChurchGeniusPro Billing", no amount in subject); email failure → back to DRAFT. Re-send rotates the token (old link dies; on failure the old hash is restored). Mark paid (SENT→PAID; method CHECK|BANK_TRANSFER|CASH|CARD|OTHER) marks charges PAID but never touches the client's end date / payment status. Void (DRAFT/SENT) clears the token and returns charges to UNBILLED. Public `GET /api/invoice/view?t=` (`InvoiceController`; AuthFilter public, AccountStatusFilter + DemoTrialAgreementFilter allowed): looked up by hash only, SENT/PAID and unexpired only, refused if a church session belongs to another client; one generic 404; no-store + no-referrer; 10 bad tokens / 10 min per IP → 429. `static/invoice.html`. Service Admin: `/api/serviceadmin/billing/{upcoming,invoices,charges,settings}` + Billing card in serviceadminhome (Upcoming / Invoices / Additional Charges / Reminder settings, invoice review dialog, mark-paid dialog) and "Create invoice" on Subscription Requests. `BillingReminderScheduler` (07:30 Chicago, `scheduler.job.billing-reminders`), paid-plan clients 10/3/0 days before `end_date` (not DEMO-/TRIAL-, TRIAL or FREE plan, or payment status NOT_REQUIRED); two switches in platform_setting, both OFF by default: `billing_reminder_support_enabled` (digest to Support Email), `billing_reminder_client_enabled` (create+send renewal invoice, or re-send an unpaid one; admin drafts, paid and voided invoices left alone). Dedupe in `reminder_sent_log` (BILLING_CLIENT / BILLING_SUPPORT, key `<due>_<days>`), released on email failure. SubscriptionExpiryNotifier unchanged. Tests: `service/BillingPhase5Test` (53), `integration/BillingConcurrencyIT` (7, real PostgreSQL).
PHASE 5 FIXES (2026-10-05, after read-only verification): (A) Send = ONE transaction (`TransactionTemplate` from the required `PlatformTransactionManager`): `lockById` (SELECT … FOR UPDATE), checks, every charge line's charge must still be reserved+UNBILLED, `markSent` (version-checked), `ClientChargeRepository.billForInvoice` (count must equal charge lines, else rollback) — email only after commit; failure → one compensating transaction `revertSend` (conditional on the minted hash) + `unbillForInvoice`; if that fails the invoice stays SENT with BILLED charges and the admin is told. Mark paid / void use bulk conditional `payForInvoice` / `releaseForInvoice` in their transaction. (B) Charges reserved only via conditional `claim` (invoice_id IS NULL AND UNBILLED) / `release`; void/mark-paid of a free charge via `voidFree` / `payFree`; `ClientCharge` has `@Version`. (C) Renewal identity = `period_start` (billing date, `updatable=false`), never due date: `renewalFor`/`anyRenewalFor` by period_start; V11 index now (client_id, period_start) WHERE kind='RENEWAL' AND status IN (DRAFT,SENT,PAID). (D) Reminder job never sends any DRAFT (the retry of its own failed draft was removed); a failed automatic email leaves a draft that is listed in the Support digest. Link expiry = due date + 60 days exactly (send/re-send refused when already past; due date in review cannot be set that far back). Renewal (and request) drafts take free charges with billing_period <= the billing month; manual drafts take all free charges. `BillingConcurrencyIT` needs `app.base-url` (set on the test; the `it` profile lacks it — existing ITs share that gap).
PHASE 6 — Service Admin Stripe: card payment of SENT platform invoices on invoice.html through the Service Admin's OWN Stripe account. Keys ONLY in Azure app settings `PLATFORM_STRIPE_SECRET_KEY` / `PLATFORM_STRIPE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY` / `PLATFORM_STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET` (properties `platform.stripe.*`), never in the DB, never returned except the publishable key (Stripe.js) and the invoice's own PaymentIntent client secret to the link holder; status shows masked hints (`sk_test_…K1l2`). `service/PlatformStripeService` (direct REST via JDK HttpClient, card only `payment_method_types[]=card`, no ACH; test connection = read-only `GET /v1/balance`; webhook HMAC-SHA256 `t.payload` verify, ±300 s, constant-time; every Stripe error text through SensitiveDataMasker). `service/BillingPaymentService`: start = one PaymentIntent for exactly Amount Due in USD cents, metadata purpose=cgp_platform_invoice + invoice_id, Idempotency-Key `cgp-inv-<id>-<cents>-<first|previous pi>`, attached conditionally (`attachFirstPaymentIntent` / `replacePaymentIntent`); confirm re-reads the PI from Stripe; confirm + webhook both end in `recordCardPayment` = ONE transaction (audit row `billing_payment_event` with unique stripe_event_id → redelivery = "duplicate", conditional SENT→PAID method CARD ref=pi, charges PAID); PAID-by-other / VOID / mismatch (amount, currency, invoice) → never applied, Support Email alert (refund in Stripe dashboard; refunds not built). Void → `afterVoid` cancels the open PI. Receipts: card → billing email + BCC Support Email; manual mark paid → optional `sendReceipt`. Payment never renews the subscription. Endpoints: public `/api/invoice/pay/config|start|confirm` (token-only, generic 404, PublicFormGuard rate limit, no-store), `POST /api/billing/stripe-webhook` (AuthFilter public + CsrfOriginFilter EXEMPT; 400 on bad signature), Service Admin `/api/serviceadmin/billing/stripe`, `/stripe/test`, `/invoices/{id}/payments`. V12 = partial unique index `ux_billing_invoice_payment_intent` (re-applied by BillingSchemaInitializer, which now runs V11 and V12). SensitiveDataMasker gained Stripe shapes (sk_/rk_/pk_ live|test, whsec_, pi_…_secret_…). `architecture/StripeSeparationRatchetTest` fails the build if billing code references StripeSettings/stripe_settings or donation code references PLATFORM_STRIPE/PlatformStripeService. DonationController, StripeSettingsController, StripeSettings, stripe_settings, stripeIntegration.html untouched. Tests: `service/BillingPhase6Test` (25, fake Stripe `FakeStripeHttp`), `integration/BillingPaymentIT` (4, real PostgreSQL incl. 15 concurrent confirm-vs-webhook rounds); billing test fakes moved to `service/BillingTestBase`. Full suite 2,019 / 0 failures / 43 skips (11 = Billing*IT without Docker; pass on real PostgreSQL 16).
TESTS — tests that build subscription dates must use `AppClock.today()` (America/Chicago), not `LocalDate.now()`: between ~7pm Chicago and UTC midnight the two differ (SessionReCheckTest, SubscriptionExpiryLoginTest, DemoSubscriptionTest were converted).
VERIFIED — Phases 1–4: full suite 1,926 tests, 0 failures (32 pre-existing skips); V7–V10 run on PostgreSQL fresh / existing / re-run. Phase 5 + fixes: full suite 1,986 tests, 0 failures (39 skips = 32 pre-existing + 7 BillingConcurrencyIT without Docker; those 7 pass against real PostgreSQL 16); V11 run on PostgreSQL fresh (app startup) / existing / re-run / pre-existing duplicates. Deploy: V7–V11 applied in production 2026-10-05; Phase 6 adds V12 (not deployed).

**2026-10-04: Subscription plans (price / Bank Sync limit / configurable Trial length), Support Email setting, public Trial Request flow.**
PHASE 1 — `SubscriptionPlan` gains `monthlyPrice`, `maxBankAccounts` (null = unlimited), `trialDays`; Flyway
`V6__plan_price_bank_limit_trial_days.sql` back-fills (Free $0/none, Standard $14.99/3, Pro $34.99/unlimited,
Trial $0/as Pro/30 days), strips the old "NN-day free trial · " prefix from the TRIAL description and removes
`bankSync:false` from STANDARD features (Standard now HAS Bank Sync, capped at 3). `service/TrialPolicy` is the
SINGLE source of the trial length (30 s cache, `refresh()` called by SubscriptionPlanAdminController on save →
no restart); used by TrialRegistrationLinkService (captured on each link at issue; explicit per-link override
kept), TrialRegistrationService, ServiceClientService, DemoAccessService. Changing it affects NEW trials/links
only. `TrialDurationSingleSourceTest` forbids literal plusDays(n) in those files. Bank limit counts ACTIVE
`PlaidAccount` rows (individual accounts, not items); `PlaidLinkService` refuses link-token creation at the limit
and refuses an over-limit exchange whole (itemRemove, nothing saved; unknown account count + a limit = refused).
Downgrades never disconnect — plaidReview shows "N of M accounts" and blocks adding until within the limit.
PHASE 2 — `platform_setting` key/value (`PlatformSettingService`, 30 s cache cleared on set): `support_email`
(default support@churchgeniuspro.com) used by Trial Request notifications, SupportTicketService and

Flow (`service/TrialRequestService`, entity `TrialRequest`, table `trial_request` via ddl-auto):
PENDING_VERIFICATION → 6-digit code (VerificationStore, type TRIAL_REQUEST, keyed by reference; resend 60 s

approval stands and the URL is returned to the admin) or Reject (PENDING/VERIFIED; no email to the requester).

VERIFIED — `TrialRequestFlowTest` (23), plus BankAccountLimitTest, StandardPlanTest, TrialPolicyTest,

**2026-10-02 (later): Ledger scalability, Part B — `ledger_month_summary` (dashboard reads a trigger-kept summary).**
WHAT — Flyway `V5__ledger_month_summary.sql`: table `ledger_month_summary` (PK app_client_id, period_month
[1st of month], kind 'I'/'E', main_source_id, sub_source_id, purpose_id; total_amount, txn_count, updated_at),
AFTER INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE row triggers `trg_ledger_income_sync` / `trg_ledger_expense_sync` (functions
`ledger_income_sync`, `ledger_expense_sync` → `ledger_summary_apply`) that subtract the OLD row's
contribution and add the NEW one's in the same transaction as the write — one rule covers insert, hard
delete, amount/date/fund edits, soft delete and un-delete; untracked edits (note, ref) short-circuit;
NULL-tenant rows are ignored; rows that reach 0/0 are deleted. Income keys are (sub_source_id) only
(main_source_id=-1, fund resolved via sub_source at read time); expense keys are (main_source_id,
purpose_id). SQL functions `ledger_summary_rebuild(client|NULL)` and `ledger_summary_reconcile()` (every
key where summary ≠ fresh aggregate; missing side = 0). Backfill runs only when the table is empty.
RULE — the income/expense ROWS are the source of truth; the summary is a derived cache and is NEVER
written by application code. If they disagree, rebuild the summary. After a DB restore:
`SELECT ledger_summary_rebuild(NULL)`. Reports still read the ledger rows; only the dashboard reads the summary.
FRESH DB — Flyway runs V5 before Hibernate creates income/expense, so V5 no-ops; `config/
LedgerSummarySchemaInitializer` (@DependsOn entityManagerFactory) executes the SAME V5 file on every
startup (idempotent: IF NOT EXISTS / OR REPLACE / trigger-if-absent; H2 failure → warning only).
JAVA — `service/LedgerSummaryService` (plain JdbcTemplate, no JPA entity — ddl-auto must never manage
this table): totalIncome, incomeBySubCategory, expenseByMainSource, expenseByPurpose, *ForYear series,
rebuild(client), reconcile(). `AccountantDashboardController` now takes it as a 7th constructor arg and
reads every total/per-fund/per-month figure from it; the 9 repository aggregate queries it replaced were
deleted (`sumTotalAmountByAppUser` kept). New `POST /api/accountant/ledger-summary/rebuild`
(accountant/admin, session church only). `service/LedgerSummaryReconcileScheduler` — nightly
`scheduler.job.ledger-summary-reconcile=0 20 2 * * *` (America/Chicago): logs each drifted key with both
figures, rebuilds each drifted church in its own try/catch, never throws.
VERIFIED — on the 800K/120K benchmark DB: backfill 206,745 rows/21 churches inside the migration; totals
equal the row SUMs to the cent; 13 write-transition cases all reconcile to 0; injected drift detected and
repaired by a one-church rebuild; migration re-runnable; insert with trigger 0.4 ms; full reconcile 1.4 s;
dashboard aggregate reads 48 ms total (was ≈4.5 s before Part A, ≈0.5 s after). 105 accounting tests
green incl. new `LedgerSummaryReconcileSchedulerTest`; `AccountingTenantIsolationTest` verifies the
dashboard and rebuild endpoint only ever pass the session's church to the summary service.
NOT YET DONE — a live run against the real app (restart-app.bat → open the Accountant dashboard; compare
Total Income with the Income page / financial report). Period locking (closed months) was discussed as a
separate accounting feature and is not implemented.

**2026-10-02: Ledger scalability, Part A (query/index fixes; no behaviour change except list paging).**
WHY — benchmarked a church with 800K income / 120K expense rows (10 years) on a 21-tenant PostgreSQL 16:
the balance SUM itself was fine (125 ms), but the Accountant dashboard loaded EVERY income and expense row
as entities to show 10 "recent" items (2.3 s + 0.4 s of DB time, ~110 MB, before Hibernate work), and
`/api/income` sent all rows to the browser (~100 MB JSON). Year filters used `EXTRACT(YEAR …)` (no index).
CHANGES — (1) Flyway `V4__ledger_date_indexes.sql`: partial covering indexes `idx_income_tenant_date`
(app_client_id, income_date) INCLUDE (amount, sub_source_id) WHERE delete_flag=false, and
`idx_expense_tenant_date` likewise (+ main_source_id, purpose_id); repeated in `DatabaseIndexInitializer`
for fresh DBs (Flyway runs before Hibernate creates the tables); `db/window/V4_concurrent_indexes.sql` =
the CONCURRENTLY variant to run by hand first if a lock-free build is ever needed. (2) `IncomeRepository` /
`ExpenseRepository`: every `EXTRACT(YEAR)` → half-open date range via `…InRange` queries; the old
`…ForYear(int year, …)` signatures survive as default methods, so callers and mocked tests are untouched.
`(:appClientId IS NULL OR …)` dropped from dashboard/report/list queries (tenant always session-checked);
deliberately KEPT in 5 bounded queries that can get a null tenant (searchIncome/searchExpense from
`RoleGuard.clientId`, `reportIncomeEntriesInRange` from the member portal, findByMemberActiveByAppUser,
findQuickAdd*, sumMemberSubSourceTotal). Deleted 3 unused untenanted reports (reportIncomeByMemberAndSubSource,
reportAnnualIncomeByMember, reportIncomeFiltered). Unbounded `findAllActiveByAppUser` replaced by
`findActivePageByAppUser(appClientId, Pageable)` (sort …, id DESC for stable paging);
`findRecentForDashboardByAppUser` now takes a Pageable. New `repository/OffsetWindow` (Pageable with
arbitrary offset — services over-fetch size+1 for hasMore) and `model/PageSlice` record.
(3) `AccountantDashboardController`: sub-category query once not twice; expenseByMonth derived from the
fund-by-month aggregate (was a Java loop over all rows); recent activity = LIMIT 10 per table.
(4) `GET /api/income` and `GET /api/expense` take `?page=0&size=50` (size clamped 1..200), body still a
plain JSON array, `X-Has-More` header; `income.html`/`expense.html` fetch 50 at a time and append —
"Load More" reveals 10, fetching the next page when it runs out (target/classes copies synced).
VERIFIED — `mvn compile`; 100 accounting tests green (`AccountingTenantIsolationTest` updated for the new
signatures); all 22 native queries run on the 800K benchmark DB (year queries 141→28 ms, tax 224→91 ms,
one-month transactions 110→10 ms, recent/list 2.3 s→<1 ms); H2 context boot confirms the JPQL and that
Hibernate pushes OFFSET/FETCH FIRST into SQL. Dashboard DB time ≈4.5 s → ≈0.5 s for the 800K church.
NEXT (Part B, approved design, not started) — `ledger_month_summary` (tenant × month × kind × fund) kept
by DB triggers on income/expense, nightly reconcile/repair job, dashboard all-time totals read from it
(≈0.5 s → ≈60 ms). NOTE: V3's comment says the church_event.image_data drop would be "V4" — it is now V5.

{status, sendEmail default true, subject?, body?} — status saved first, email failure reported). Pages:

Playwright 40 checks (tickets page incl. required fields/success/ref/list/detail read-only/filters/trial note/

**2026-10-01 (later 4): Meetings — after editing a date, show the meeting in the list it now belongs to.**
Investigated History vs Scheduled: classification was ALREADY by the saved date (`isMeetingExpired`: one-time
`meetingDate < todayStr()`, recurring `endDate < todayStr()`; `todayStr()` = browser-LOCAL date) and Save
re-fetches the list; server `applyBO` stores the new date. Verified on the user's localhost (read-only, other
tenant) that `/api/meetings` is not cached (fresh `Date` header; SW passes /api through). Not reproducible as
"stays in History"; the real gap: saving from Meeting History left the user on History while the meeting
silently vanished (it had moved to Scheduled). Fix (`meeting.html` only): `saveMeeting` → new
`showSavedMeeting(id, wasEdit)` after `loadMeetings()`: if the saved meeting's list differs from the one in view,
`toggleHistory()` + "✅ Meeting updated — moved to Scheduled Meetings (its date is now today or later)" / "…
Meeting History (its date is now in the past)", highlight row (`tr.row-flash`, 2.6 s), reset
`.meetings-table-wrap` scrollLeft (mobile), scroll into view. Same-list saves unchanged. Playwright: 24 checks
desktop + Pixel 5 (past→future from History, future→past from Scheduled, same-list edit, recurring end date
extended), 4 at a fixed 23:30 CDT clock (UTC already next day: today stays local; dates sent unshifted), and the
earlier 18 Edit/purge checks still green. No backend change.

**2026-10-01 (later 3): One-off "Fill Demo Data in Existing Trial Accounts" (Service Admin).**
New card on `serviceadminhome.html` (below Trial Registration Links): 🔍 Preview → table per TRIAL- account
(Will add / Already has data — kept / Result), only accounts with something to add are selectable, confirm →
"Fill selected". APIs (`TestDataController`, service-admin session guard): `GET
/api/serviceadmin/test-data/trial-backfill/preview` (read-only) and `POST …/trial-backfill` (body
`{clientIds:[…]}`, omitted = all trials; each account in its own transaction via
`TestDataService.backfillTrialDemoData` → one failure doesn't stop the rest; logs who ran it). Rules — the
"don't break existing data" guarantee: NO update/delete of existing rows; an area is filled only when the
account has no rows of that kind (`TrialDemoDataSeeder.wouldFill` = the exact checks `seed` uses, so preview
== run); invented attendance/gifts/pledges/assignments use only the account's ORIGINAL demo members
(`demoMembers`: email `@<tenant>.test`, not Visitors) — never people the church added; meetings added only
when the account has NO upcoming meeting (`hasUpcomingMeeting`: one-time ≥ today, or recurring without/with
a future end date) and existing categories are reused by name (case-insensitive) — this guard also applies at
provisioning; pledges only if no campaign exists and demo adults + funds + payment types exist. TRIAL- only
(DEMO-, regular and unknown ids refused). Bank Import sample needs no backfill (endpoint answers for any
TRIAL-). Tests: `TrialDemoDataTest` (+2), `TrialDemoDataProvisionIT` (+1, run here on real PostgreSQL 16:
old trial + church-added member/Connect submission + purged meetings → preview read-only and correct; after
backfill every pre-existing row in 14 tables byte-identical (md5 per table up to the old max id); church's
member gets no attendance/gifts; categories reused; 2nd run adds 0; refusals). Playwright 11 checks on the
card. Full suite 1733/0. Rebuild + restart.

**2026-10-01 (later 2): Trial demo data for every main screen + Meetings History "Edit" opened a blank form.**
(A) MEETINGS EDIT — ROOT CAUSE (reproduced in Chromium with the real page): `runAutoPurge()` runs ~1.2 s after
load and soft-deletes one-time meetings >90 days old (unless Don't auto-delete), but never redrew the table; so
History kept showing e.g. Sun Jun 14 2026, and `editMeeting` didn't check `res.ok` — the 404 body was used as
the meeting and every field was set to blank under an "Edit Meeting" title. Fix (`meeting.html` only): purge
reloads the list when it purged anything and leaves edit mode if the edited meeting went; `editMeeting` on a
non-OK response shows "This meeting is no longer available — it may have been auto-deleted…" and refreshes.
Playwright 18 checks: one-time/weekly/monthly populate + save payloads, purged row gone, race (edit open
when purge hits), stale row. Purged meetings are soft-deleted (`delete_flag`), recoverable in the DB.
(B) TRIAL DEMO DATA — new `service/TrialDemoDataSeeder` (@Component), called from
`TestDataService.provisionTenant` (the single provisioning path → new TRIAL- AND DEMO- tenants; existing
tenants untouched; refuses a non-managed client id). Seeds, all with the new tenant's id, fictional (`.test`
emails, 555-01xx phones, test-card endings 4242/4444/0005, no processor ids): 8 donations (some fee-covered),
3 pending membership applications (head address, state code 16=KS), ~6 Sundays + 4 Wednesdays of attendance
("Sunday Worship"/"Bible Study") + 2 visitors, 4 Connect submissions (Visitor member + CONNECT follow-up, as
the real form), 5 public prayer requests (+ PRAYER follow-ups), 3 unsubscribed. Each area skips if rows exist
(idempotent). created_at/created_date of connect/prayer/membership are forced to now by @PrePersist and
non-updatable → back-dated by a tenant-scoped native UPDATE (whitelisted table/column). `TestDataService`:
`seedMeetings(clientId, today)` now = weekly Sunday Service (10:00–11:30) + weekly Wednesday Bible Study +
monthly 1st-Saturday Prayer Meeting + 2 one-time meetings, all first occurring AFTER the creation date
(America/Chicago); `seedPledges` = 2 campaigns whose createdDate is backdated (progress window =
createdDate..endDate, see PledgeController.collectedForPledge) + 1–2 "Pledge payment" incomes per pledger in
that window. Bank Import (stores nothing) → `bankimport/BankImportDemoStatement` (18 fictional rows, last 30
days, categorizer keywords) + `GET /api/bank-import/demo-statement` (role check only; 404 unless TRIAL-/DEMO-)
+ `bank-import.html` "📄 Load sample statement" button shown only when that answers 200 (regular churches: page
unchanged). Tests: `trial/TrialDemoDataTest` (17), `integration/TrialDemoDataProvisionIT` (Testcontainers;
executed here against a real PostgreSQL 16 via zonky binaries in scratch — real provisionTenant transaction:
all areas populated, back-dates before today, meetings future, other tenant untouched, 2nd run adds 0).
NOTE: the ITs need `app.base-url` set (added on the new one; older ITs would hit the same if run).
Full suite 1732/0. Rebuild + restart (Java + static).

**2026-10-01 (later): Event Reminders list showed reminders of DELETED events ("Event #18", "Event #17").**
Events are soft-deleted (`ChurchEventService.delete` sets `delete_flag`); `event_reminder` rows are never
touched. `ReminderService.getAllEventReminders` listed every row for the church; `eventToMap` resolved the
name via a `...DeleteFlagFalse` lookup, got "", so the page fell back to `Event #<id>`. Fix (read path only):
the list now keeps a reminder only if `eventId` is null (applies to all events) or is one of the church's
non-deleted events (one query, `findByAppClientIdAndDeleteFlagFalseOrderByCreatedDateDesc`). Reminder rows are
NOT modified, disabled or deleted, so restoring an event brings its reminder back unchanged and live-event
reminders behave exactly as before. Sending was ALREADY safe and is unchanged: `runEventReminderRule` loads
the event with `findByIdAndDeleteFlagFalse`, so a deleted event's reminder never sent — now pinned by test.
Tests: `reminders/DeletedEventReminderTest` (4) — the 7-step scenario through the REAL `ChurchEventService.delete`,
`ReminderService` and `ReminderSchedulerService` over delete-flag-aware in-memory repos (deleted event: not
listed, no SMS; active event: listed, one SMS; no reminder row saved/deleted), pre-existing orphans hidden,
foreign event id hidden, null-event reminder still listed. Mutation-checked: the old list code fails 3 of 4.
Full suite 1715/0. Rebuild + restart needed (Java change).

**2026-09-30: Follow-Ups → Prayer Requests: per-row delete + transparent Manage modal fix.**
`followups.html` only. (1) The shared Manage modal (`#mgOverlay`) used `class="modal"`, which has no
CSS on this page — the styled box is `.modal-box` — so it rendered with no background/padding over the
page (unreadable). Now `.modal-box` (white, rounded, shadow, scrollable). (2) Each prayer-request row
has a 🗑 button (`.pr-del-btn`) → `prayDeleteAsk(id)` opens `#prayDeleteModal` naming the person and
request → `prayDeleteConfirm()` calls the EXISTING `DELETE /api/ppr/{id}` (PublicEngagementController,
tenant-scoped soft delete) and removes only that row from `PRAY`, updates the tab count, closes the
Manage modal if it showed that request. No backend change. jsdom: 9 checks. Rebuild needed.

**2026-09-29 (later): /home dashboard — 1 s hold, downward drag fix, add/remove/re-add sections.**
`home.html` only. (1) `LP_HOLD_MS` 2000 → 1000 (CSS ring animation too). (2) ROOT CAUSE of "can drag up
but not below People": the dashboard scrolls inside `.page-content` (overflow-y:auto; `.app` is 100vh
overflow:hidden), so `window.scrollBy()` in `_startAutoScroll` never scrolled anything and a drop target
had to be already on screen. New `_scroller()` finds the nearest scrollable ancestor of `#dashSections`
and auto-scrolls its `scrollTop` (edge zones from ITS rect); `_startAutoScroll(y, onScrolled)` re-aims
the drop line while content moves under a still pointer (both handle-touch and press-and-hold paths).
`_afterTarget(el)` = next VISIBLE section (skips hidden ones / the indicator) for "insert after". (3)
Section management: the ✏️ FAB has been `display:none` in CSS (init's `style.display=''` cannot
override a stylesheet rule) so ✕/tray/▲▼ were unreachable — left as is; NEW inline `#dashToolbar` →
`#dashCustomizeBtn` ("✏️ Customize layout" / "✓ Done") above the sections toggles the existing edit
mode, and the edit banner gained `#addSectionBtn` + `#addSectionMenu` (lists role-allowed sections
currently removed; count in the label; "All available sections…" when none; outside click closes; on
phones the menu is position:fixed full-width under the banner). Remove = existing ✕ (`inlineHideSection`,
hidden flag only, never deleted); re-add = `inlineShowSection` (menu or tray) which now refuses
role-disallowed sids and scrolls/highlights the section. `_domOrder()` now returns ALL sections (hidden
ones keep their DOM slot via applyDashPrefs), so removing/re-adding never disturbs the others and a
re-added section returns to its previous position; duplicates impossible (one element per section).
(4) Touch: a non-passive `touchmove` blocker is registered on the container up-front (preventDefault
only while `_td`) so the browser never converts the drag into a pan/pointercancel; `.lp-arming/.lp-moving`
get `-webkit-touch-callout:none; user-select:none`. Verified in real Chromium (Playwright, cloud
container; stubbed /api): desktop 1280×800 mouse + Pixel 5 touch — up/down/between/first/last incl.
off-screen targets, quick swipe scrolls without reordering, order + hidden set persist across refresh and
across a "re-login" (localStorage wiped, DB stub kept); add/remove/re-add/duplicate/role checks; no
overlap of banner/menu/toolbar with sections or FAB. Rebuild needed (static file).

**2026-09-29 (later): Donation Review — editable Stripe fee + Remaining.** `donationReview.html` only.
All Donations heading row gained two inputs, `#feePct` (default 2.9) and `#feeFixed` (default 0.30) — defaults
only, never hard-coded in the maths; blank/invalid/negative → 0. `feeSettings()` / `feeCentsFor(amount)`:
integer cents, `round(amountCents × pct/100) + fixedCents`, rounded PER donation; both zero → fee 0. Each
Amount cell has an ⓘ (`.fee-info` / `.fee-btn`) toggling a popover (Donation · Stripe fee "x + y = z" ·
Remaining); one open at a time, outside click closes; icon stays visible at $0.00. Summary card "Average
Gift" → **"Remaining"** = Total Received − Σ per-donation fees (same `#statAvg` element id kept; title shows
the breakdown); with zero fees Remaining == Total Received. Fee change re-renders `currentRows` /
`currentTotals` (tracked in load/filter/clear), so the date filter and the server-total path (M11) are
unchanged. Settings are per page load (not persisted). jsdom: 26 checks incl. six amounts, blank/zero/
negative inputs, filter interplay. Rebuild needed (static file).

**2026-09-29 (later): Permission work, Phase 5 — verification & close-out.** Source scan of every
`RoleGuard.require*` call: 74 API-side calls identical (file + key) to the pre-work inventory; 56 page-route
calls on `requirePagePermission`/`requireMemberPagePermission`; `RoleGuard` diff vs pre-work copy is
additions only (role guards byte-identical); `AuthFilter` +1 line. Suite 1708/0. Completion report with the
18-gap closure matrix and the four decisions taken without an explicit answer (A4 endpoint check, A7
Admin/Payroll, legacy-key alignment on save, `sendOrgEmailOrThrow`) is section 10 of the Claude Doc
"Permission Gaps Implementation Plan". Gaps 10/12/13/18 closed without code. `scripts/permission_inventory.sql` (read-only; validated on PostgreSQL 16 with synthetic data) = key inventory across all three generations + A7 pre-flight (sections A–G); could not be run from the VM (local pg_hba rejects host 192.168.1.99; prod creds only in Azure) — user runs it. A7 RESOLVED 2026-09-29 20:22 from the production inventory (run by user in pgAdmin): E = 0 rows, F = 0 rows, G = 6 member rows / 0 false → nobody affected, NO migration, `migrate_production.sql` and production permission records untouched. User confirmed A4, save-time legacy-key alignment, `sendOrgEmailOrThrow`, and the API guarantee as implemented. RULES going forward: a page
route uses `requirePagePermission` (aliases), an API keeps `requirePermission` (alias-free) and never gains
one without raising `ApiPermissionCheckRatchetTest`'s ceiling with a comment; new checkbox keys go in
`PERM_TREE` + `NAV_PERM`/`SECTION_CHILDREN` + (if renaming) `PERMISSION_ALIASES` and `CGP_PERM_ALIASES`
together; never rename stored keys.

**2026-09-29 (later): Permission work, Phase 1 — foundation.** Decisions: permissions govern PAGES,

`RoleGuard.requirePermission` / `requireMemberPermission`. `AppUserController.savePermissions` calls
`markFresh` after its self-refresh. (2) `RoleGuard.PERMISSION_ALIASES` (current key → legacy names from
all three tree generations) + `pagePermissionAllows` / `requirePagePermission` /
`requireMemberPagePermission`: PAGE-route variants that also deny on a legacy explicit false. API checks
(`requirePermission`) are alias-free by design. Phase 2 will switch page routes to the page variants.

called it). (4) `permissions/ApiPermissionCheckRatchetTest` pins per-file `requirePermission` counts so no
controller gains one; `TenantScopedLookupRatchetTest` ceiling +2 for PermissionRefresher. Tests:
`PermissionRefresherTest` (12), `RoleGuardAliasTest` (31). Suite green at 1673.

**2026-09-04: Demo-data phases 2 and 4 complete.** `TestDataService.addDemoRole` adds one
